cila camayaṅkaḷil aracaṉ kōyiṟ paṇattaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ. āṉāl itu āpattāṉa ceyalāka viruntatu. kōyiṟ cottil oru turumputāṉum payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟavarkaḷ mītu kurumār koṭiya cāpaṅkaḷaip poḻintārkaḷ aracaṉukkup potumakkaḷiṭamuḷḷa atikārattilum mēlākak kurumārukku iruntatu. aracaṉ iṟantupōkiṉṟavaṉ. kaṭavuḷar eppoḻutum iruppavar. kurumār tērnteṭukkappaṭukiṉṟavarkaḷallar. avarkaḷukku varikaḷumillai; pōril kalappatāl nērum tuṉpamum illai. vaṇikar nāṭṭaic celvattāl ceḻippaṭaiyac ceytārkaḷ. kurumār ataṉai anupavittārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉil pala teyvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kiṟittuvukkumuṉ oṉpa tām nūṟṟāṇṭil eṭukkappaṭṭa kaṇakkiṉpaṭi aṅku 65,000 kōvilkaḷ iruntaṉa. ovvoru paṭṭiṉattilum, kirāmattilum pala teyvaṅkaḷ irun taṉa. ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷ camāṣ eṉavum, tiṅkaṭ kaṭavuḷ naṉṉar eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. ovvoru vīṭṭilum vīṭṭut teyvam iruntatu. avai kaḷukkuk kālaiyilum mālaiyilum paliyiṭappaṭṭatu. muṉpu ñāyiṟṟuk kaṭavuḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭa mārṭuk ippoḻutu tēvarkaḷukku aracaṉ āṉāṉ.
cuvarkkattil tēvarkaḷ māttiram vāḻntārkaḷ. iṟantavar aralu eṉṉum narakattil, kaiyum kālum kaṭṭuṇṭu kuḷirāl naṭuṅkikkoṇṭum, paci tākaṅkaḷāl varuntikkoṇṭum iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ putalvar camātiyil kālantōṟum uṇavai vaittālaṉṟi ivarkaḷukku uṇavu kiṭaippatillai. ivvulakil keṭṭa vāḻkkai naṭattiyavarkaḷukku atika taṇṭaṉaikaḷ koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. ivarkaḷiṉ uṭalil kuṭṭa nōyum piṟa nōykaḷum uṇṭāyiṉa.
piṇaṅkaḷ nila aṟaikaḷil putaikkappaṭṭaṉa; cila koḷuttap paṭṭaṉa. piṇaṅkaḷ tailamiṭṭup pakkuvañ ceyyappaṭavillai. kūlikku aḻuvōr avaikaḷaik kaḻuvi, vācaṉai pūci, uṭutti, kaṇṇukku mai eḻuti viralkaḷil mōtiramiṭṭu alaṅkarittārkaḷ. peṇ piṇaṅkaḷiṉ pakkattē vācaṉaip puṭṭikaḷ, cīppu, mai tīṭṭuṅ kucci, kaṇṇukkut tīṭṭum mai mutaliyaṉa vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. nalla muṟaiyākap piṇaṅkaḷ putaikkap paṭāviṭṭāl iṟantavar iruppavarkaḷukkut tuṉpam viḷaivippar; uṇavukkāka alaintu tirivar; nāṭṭil koḷḷai nōyai uṇṭākkuvar eṉa eṇṇiṉar. iv vaccaṅ kāraṇamākavē pāpilōṉiya makkaḷ kurumārukkum kaṭavuḷarukkum nalla uṇavukaḷai aḷittu vantārkaḷ.
kaṭavuḷukku uṇavum nīrum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa; āṭṭukkuṭṭi pali yiṭappaṭṭatu. āṭṭukkuṭṭi maṉitaṉukkup patil iṭappaṭum pali. kurumār puriyum palavakaik kiriyaikaḷōṭu paliyum iṭappaṭṭatu. oḻuṅkupaṭṭa vāḻkkaiyilum pārkka muṟaiyāṉa kiriyaiyē mēlāṉatu eṉak koḷ ḷappaṭṭatu. oruvaṉuṭaiya kaṭamai kōvilkaḷukku muṟaiyākap palika ḷiṭṭuc cariyāṉa tutikaḷ pāṭuvatākum. oruvaṉ viḻuntu kiṭakkum etiriyiṉ kaṇkaḷaip piṭuṅkiviṭalām; kaikaḷai veṭṭi viṭalām; avaṉuṭaiya iṟaicciyai aṟuttu vaṟukkalām; avaikaḷaik kuṟittuk kaṭavuḷ veṟuppaṭaiya māṭṭār. kurumār kaṭavuḷukku etiril cāmpirāṇip pukai iṭṭārkaḷ; kaṭavuḷarai aḻakiya uṭaikaḷālum aṇikalaṉkaḷālum alaṅkarittārkaḷ; kurumār avarkaḷmītu pala tutikaḷaip pāṭiṉar. cila camayaṅkaḷil palar oṉṟucērntu niṉṟu pāṭiṉar.
pāpilōṉiya camaya nūlkaḷum tutikaḷum cumēriya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kattōlikkak kiṟittuvak kōyilkaḷil ilattiṉ moḻi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭuvatupōla, cumēriya moḻi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. tutikaḷ pūtaṅkaḷai ōṭṭiṉa. ivai paḻaiya mantira vittai toṭarpāka eḻuntavai. pūtaṅkaḷiliruntu tammaip pātukāttuk koḷvataṟkut tāyattukaḷ, mantiraṅkaḷ eḻutiya ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷ kaṭavuḷariṉ vaṭivaṅ kaḷ mutaliyaṉa uṭampil aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa; ciṟiya kaṟkaḷ kayiṟṟil allatu caṅkiliyil kōttuk kaḻuttil kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. kaṟuppu, veḷḷai, civappu mutaliya kayiṟukaḷ nōkkattukku ēṟṟavāṟu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paricutta māṉa taikiras, yūpirātas āṟukaḷiṉ nīrait teḷippatālum pūtaṅkaḷ vilakiṉa. acur pāṉippāliṉ nūlnilaiyattil kiṭaitta kaḷimaṇ ēṭu kaḷil, cōtiṭam, kaṉaviṉ palaṉ, cakuṉaṅkaḷiṉ palaṉ pōṉṟavaikaḷaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paliyiṭappaṭṭa vilaṅkiṉ īralaip pārttup pāpilōṉiyak kurumār varuṅ kāriyam kūṟiṉārkaḷ. iv vaḻakkam kirēkkak kurumārālum kaiyāḷappaṭṭatu. nimittam pārppavaṉāvatu kōviṟ kuruvāvatu cakuṉam pārttup palaṉ kūṟuvataṉ muṉ aracaṉ pōrukkuc cellamāṭṭāṉ.
ilakkiyam
pāpilōṉiya vāṇikam caṭṭa campantamāṉa oḻuṅku peṟṟiruntatu. mempis, tīps nakaraṅkaḷiliruntatu pōlap pāpilōṉil eḻuttāḷar palar iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ īramāṉa kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukkaḷil nuṉi mukkōṇamāka veṭṭappaṭṭa eḻutukōlāl eḻutiṉārkaḷ. eḻutap paṭṭatu tirumukamāyiṉ atu kaḷimaṇ uṟaiyil iṭṭu eḻutiyavaṉiṉ muttirai mēlē poṟikkappaṭṭatu. kōyilkaḷilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum nūlnilaiyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ cāṭikaḷil aṭukki oḻuṅkupaṭuttit taṭṭukkaḷil niraiyāka vaikkap paṭṭaṉa. pōrcippā nūl nilaiyattiliruntu nūlkaḷ paṭiyeṭuttu acur pāṉippāliṉ nūl nilaiyattil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. acīriyāvil kāṇap paṭṭa 30,000 kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷiṉ mūlam pāpilōṉiyariṉ vāḻkkaiyaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷ aṟiyappaṭṭaṉa.
nīṇṭa kālam pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ vācikkappaṭāmal iruntaṉa. heṉṟi iraviliṉcaṉ eṉṉum āṅkilar paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷāka muyaṉṟu 1847il av veḻuttukaḷai vācittār. pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ cumēriya akkēṭiya eḻuttukaḷiṉ cemittiya muṟaiyāṉa vaḷarcciyāl tōṉṟiyavai. toṭakkattil nūlkaḷ cumēriya moḻiyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. kālattil am moḻi māṟutalaṭaintatu. paḻaiya puṉita moḻiyākiya cumēriyattiliruntu nūlkaḷaip pāpilōṉiya moḻippaṭuttuvataṟku akarātikaḷum ilakkaṇaṅkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. niṉēvā nūl nilaiyattil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ perumpālum cumēriya, pāpilōṉiya acīriya moḻi akarātikaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. av vakarātikaḷ akkāṭ aracaṉākiya cārkaṉ kālattil ceyyappaṭṭavai.
cumēriyattilum pāpilōṉiyattilum eḻuttukaḷ colliṉ pakuti kaḷāka iruntaṉa. pāpilōṉiyattukkuc contamāṉa eḻuttukkaḷ illai; colliṉ pakutikaḷai uṇartta 300 aṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ varaiyil iruntaṉa. ivvaṭaiyāḷaṅkaḷ mūlam kaṇakku, camayam, vāṇikam pōṉṟa pāṭaṅkaḷ kōyiṟ kurumārāl māṇavarukkup payiṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. aḻipāṭu oṉṟil paḷḷikkūṭa māṇavar pārttu eḻutiya nītivākkiyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. pāpilōṉiya eḻuttukaḷ vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷaip paṟṟiya kaṟpaṉaik kataikaḷ, pāṭalkaḷ, camayak kiriyaikaḷ, viḷakkaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavai eḻutappaṭṭa ēṭukaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. aracāṅka varalāṟukaḷ, aracariṉ camayappaṟṟu, avarkaḷiṉ veṟṟikaḷ pōṉṟavaikaḷaik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. perōcas (ki.mu.280) eṉṉum pāpilōṉiya varalāṟṟāciriyar paṭaippuk kālam mutal maṉitaṉuṭaiya varalāṟṟaik kūṟiyuḷḷār. pāpilōṉiṉ mutal aracaṉ kaṭavuḷāl teriyap paṭṭāṉ. avaṉ periya veḷḷapperukku varaiyil 36,000 āṇṭukaḷ āṇṭāṉeṉṟum ulakam toṭaṅki 691,200 āṇṭukaḷākiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum avar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
oḻukkam
kōyilkaḷil tēvatācikaḷ iruntaṉar. maṇaṅkaḷ peṟṟōrāl oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. maṇamakaṉ peṇṇiṉ tantaikku vekumati aḷit tāṉ. peṇṇiṉ tantai peṇṇukku av vekumatiyilum pārkka atika peṟumatiyuḷḷa cītaṉam koṭuttāṉ. pāpilōṉiyaṉ oruvaṉ oru peṇṇaiyē maṇantāṉ. viyapicārak kuṟṟattōṭu campantappaṭṭa iru pālārum āṟṟil eṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. oruvaṉ peṇṇiṉ cītaṉap paṇattait tiruppik koṭuttut tirumaṇa nīkkam ceytukoḷḷalām. kaṇavaṉ koṭumaiyāka naṭattukiṉṟāṉ, vicuvāca millātirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉap peṇ attāṭcip paṭuttiṉāl avaḷ kaṇavaṉaik tirumaṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷalām.
peṇkaḷ āṭavaraippōla veḷiyē ceṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷiṭam cottu iruntatu. avarkaḷ ataṉait tām virumpiyavāṟu ceyya urimai peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ cilar vāṇikam purintaṉar; cilar eḻuttāḷarāka iruntaṉar. uyar kuṭumpaṅkaḷil peṇkaḷ vīṭṭil taṉiyiṭaṅkaḷil vāḻntaṉar. avarkaḷ veḷiyē ceṉṟapōtu aṇṇakarum vēlaiyāḷkaḷum uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. tāḻnta vakuppiṉar aṭimaikaḷ pōl vāḻntaṉar. ekiptiyar, pāpilōṉiya makkaḷai atika nākarikam aṭaiyātavarkaḷ eṉak karutiṉar.
kalaikaḷ
pāpilōṉiyariṉ vāṇikam, poḻutupōkku, kaṭavuḷ patti mutaliya vaikaḷai veḷiyiṭum ciṉṉaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. miṉukkañ ceyyap paṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ, veṇkalam, irumpu, veḷḷi mutaliya ulōkaṅkaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ, nuṭpamāṉa taiyal vēlaikaḷ, mirutuvāṉa tuṇikaḷ, aḻakiya cāyam ūṭṭiya uṭaikaḷ, kampaḷaṅkaḷ, mējaikaḷ, paṭukkaikaḷ, nāṟkāli pōṉṟavai pāpilōṉiyariṉ nākarikattai viḷakku kiṉṟaṉa. palavakai aṇikalaṉkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. teyva uruvaṅkaḷ poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. icaik karuvikaḷ pala payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kuḻal, yāḻ, vīṇai, mēḷam, kompu, nāṇaṟkuḻal, ekkāḷam, kaittāḷam, mattaḷam eṉpaṉa avaikaḷuṭ cila. kōyilkaḷilum, araṇmaṉaikaḷilum, celvariṉ viruntukaḷilum pāṭakar taṉittum, palar oṉṟu kūṭiyum pāṭiṉārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉil ōviyam eḻututal taṉik kalaiyāka vaḷaravillai. cuvarkaḷil ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai ekiptiya camātic cuvar kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟavaipōla niṟaṅkaḷāl eḻutappaṭavillai. ciṟpak kalaiyum naṉṟāka vaḷarcciyaṭaiyavillai. uruvaṅkaḷiṉ muka irēkaikaḷ perumpālum orē vakaiyākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pōriṟ piṭikkap paṭṭavarkaḷaik kāṭṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷiṉ mukaṅkaḷ orē vakaiyākak kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāpilōṉiyak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ taraikku mēlē cila aṭikaḷē uyarn tirukkiṉṟaṉa. kōyilkaḷaiyō araṇmaṉaikaḷaiyō kāṭṭum ōviyaṅ kaḷum ciṟpaṅkaḷum kiṭaikkavillai. vīṭukaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl kaṭṭap paṭṭaṉa. celvar ceṅkallaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. vīṭukaḷukkuc caṉṉalkaḷ irukkavillai. vīṭukaḷiṉ katavukaḷ vītiyai nōkki irukkavillai. uḷ muṟṟattai nōkkiyiruntaṉa. mūṉṟu allatu nāṉku māṭikaḷuṭaiya vīṭukaḷum iruntaṉa. kōyil vīṭukaḷaippōlavē caturamāka viruntatu. kōyiliṉ mēlē ēṟic celvataṟku caṅkup puripōl paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ amaikkappaṭṭa vaḻi veḷiyē iruntatu. itu ik kaṭavuḷiṉ uyaramāṉa vīṭākum. vāṉa ārāyccit toṭarpākavum itu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉ ucciyiliruntu kurumār viṇmīṉkaḷai nōkkiṉārkaḷ. pōrcippā vilirunta ivvakaik kōpuram ēḻu maṇṭalaṅkaḷiṉ iruppiṭam eṉak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ovvoru māṭiyum ovvoru kirakattukku uriyatu. ovvoru māṭiyum vevvēṟu niṟam tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. kīḻē uḷḷa māṭi caṉikku uriyatu. itu kaṟuppu niṟam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. ataṟku mēluḷḷatu veḷḷikkuriyatu. atu veḷḷaiyā yiruntatu. mūṉṟāvatu māṭi viyāḻaṉuk kuriyatu. im māṭi karuñcivappu niṟam ūṭṭappaṭṭatu. putaṉukkuriya nālāvatu māṭi nīla niṟamuṭaiyatu. aintāvatu paccai kalanta civappu niṟamuṭaiyatu. itu cevvāykkuriyatu. āṟāvatu māṭi veḷḷi niṟam pūcappaṭṭiruntatu. itu tiṅkaḷukkuriyatu. ēḻāvatu māṭi poṉṉiṟamāṉatu. itu ñāyiṟṟukkuriyatu. ivvāṟu ovvoru māṭiyum vārattiṉ ovvōr nāḷaik kuṟippatā yiruntatu.
ceṅkaṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ veḷippuṟaṅkaḷ miṉukkañceytu niṟaṅkaḷ pūcappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaikaḷil vilaṅkukaḷ, paṟavaikaḷ, marañceṭikaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. veḷippakkam aḻuttañ ceyyappaṭṭatu aḻakukkāka māttiramaṉṟu, veyyil maḻaikaḷāl avai paḻutaṭaiyāmal iruppataṟkumākum.
viññāṉam
vāṇikattāl kaṇakku vaḷarcci yaṭaintatu. kaṇakku camayattōṭum, vāṉa ārāycciyōṭum toṭarpu peṟṟiruntatu. nītipatikaḷ, aracāṅka nirvākikaḷ, payirttoḻilukkut talaivar, varuṅkālam colvōr, vāṉa ārāycciyāḷar ellōrum kurumārākavē yiruntaṉar. avarkaḷē viññāṉam tōṉṟuvataṟkuk kāraṇarāyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ aṟintirunta viññāṉa muṟaikaḷaik kirēkkar camayat toṭarpākak kaiyāṇṭaṉar.
pāpilōṉiyak kaṇitam vaṭṭattai 360 pākaikaḷākavum, āṇṭai 360 nāṭkaḷākavum pirittatu. itiliruntē aṟupatākak kaṇakkiṭum vaḻakku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. aṟupatākak kaṇakkiṭuvatiliruntu paṉṉiraṇṭākak kaṇakkiṭum vaḻakkup piṟkālattil terintatu. nila aḷavaippaṟṟiya kaṇakkum pāpilōṉil vaḷarcciyaṭaintiruntatu. vāṉa ārāycciyil pāpilōṉiyar pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. pāpilōṉiyar vaṉāntarattuk kūṭākac cellum vaṇikar kūṭṭattukkum kaṭal vaḻiyākac cellum kappal kaḷukkum vaḻikāṭṭuvataṟku vāṉa ārāycci ceyyavillai; makkaḷukku nērum vāyppu, vāyppiṉmaikaḷai aṟivataṟku ārāyntārkaḷ. ovvoru kirakamum ovvoru teyvamākavum, ovvoru kirakattiṉ celavum varuṅkāriyattai aṟivippatākavum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. cōtiṭa maṟinta kurumār aracariṭamum, potu makkaḷiṭamu miruntu atika ūtiyam peṟṟaṉar; cārkaṉ kālattil cōtiṭa nūlkaḷ eḻutap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟu paḻaiya varalāṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. niṟainta cōtiṭap paḻakkamiṉṟi ūtiyattiṟkākac cōtiṭam kūṟukiṉṟavarkaḷaip paṟṟik kurumār kuṟai kūṟiṉārkaḷ. nepucaṭṉēcar kālattil kirakaṅkaḷukkum naṭcattiraṅkaḷukku muḷḷa vēṟu pāṭu aṟiyappaṭṭiruntatu. pūmi cūriyaṉaic cuṟṟivarum pātai paṉṉiraṇṭu vīṭukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. pākai ‘miṉiṭ’kaḷākavum, ‘miṉiṭ’ noṭi kaḷākavum pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. aṟupatu miṉiṭ oru pākai eṉavum 60 noṭi allatu ‘cekaṇṭ’ oru miṉiṭ eṉavum kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. nēram nāḻikai vaṭṭam (Water-Clock) cūriya niḻal vaṭṭam (Sun-dial) ākiya ivaikaḷāl aḷakkappaṭṭatu.
āṇṭu paṉṉiraṇṭu cantira mātaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭatu. mutal āṟu mātaṅkaḷ muppatu nāḷum, piṉ āṟu mātaṅkaḷ 29 nāṭkaḷum koṇṭavai. ākavē āṇṭil 354 nāḷkaḷ iruntaṉa. iṭai iṭaiyē oru mātam cērttuk kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭatu. mātattil nāṉku vāraṅkaḷ uṇṭu. cantiraṉ eḻum nērattiliruntu nāḷ kaṇakkuc ceyyappaṭṭatu.
maruntum, cōtiṭamum camayat toṭarpu peṟṟiruntaṉa. hamurapi aracaṉ kālattil maruttuvam camayattiṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭṭatu. ak kālattil maruttuvar palar iruntaṉar. avarkaḷukkuk koṭukkavēṇṭiya kūliyum, tamatu kaṭamaiyaic carivarac ceyyāviṭṭāl avarkaḷ aṭaiya vēṇṭiya taṇṭaṉai kaḷum caṭṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maruttuvaṉ oruvaṉai vīṭṭukku aḻaikkumoruvaṉ avaṉukkuk koṭukka vēṇṭiya kūliyai aṟintiruntāṉ. nōyāḷi vaṟiyavaṉāṉāl kūli kuṟaikkappaṭṭatu. maruttuvaṉ muṟaiyāka maruttuvam ceyyāviṭṭāl avaṉ nōyāḷikku naṭṭa īṭu ceyya vēṇṭiyiruntatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil maruttuvaṉiṉ kaiviralkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. maruttuvarilum pārkka mantira vittaikkārarukku atika matippu uṇṭāyiruntatu. nōy pāvattiṉāl uṇṭāvatu eṉṟu karutappaṭṭatu. ākavē atu tuti, mantira vittaikaḷāl pōkkappaṭṭatu. maruntukaḷ uṭalaic cuttañ ceyvaṉa eṉṟu karutap paṭavillai. uḷḷē irukkum pūtattaip payamuṟuttukiṉṟatu eṉak karutap paṭṭatu. mika aruvarukkattakka poruḷkaḷ cērttu araitta maruntukaḷ oḷaṭatamākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. cātāraṇa maruntuc carakkukaḷ paccai iṟaicci, pāmpiṉ iṟaicci, marattūḷ mutaliyaṉa. ivai cārāyamum eṇṇeyum ūṟṟi araikkappaṭṭaṉa. aḻukiya uṇavu, elumputtūḷ, koḻuppu, aḻukku mutaliyaṉavum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ allatu maṉitaṉiṉ mala mūttiraṅkaḷum cērttu araikkappaṭṭa kūṭṭukaḷ maruntākap payaṉpaṭut tappaṭṭaṉa. pūtattait tiruptippaṭuttum poruṭṭup pāl, tēṉ, veṇṇey, naṟumaṇamuḷḷa mūlikaikaḷ uṭkoḷḷumpaṭi koṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. maruntukaḷāl nōy kuṇamaṭaiyāviṭiṉ nōyāḷi cantaikkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭāṉ. aṅku avvakai nōy aṭaintu kuṇap paṭṭavarkaḷ tāmaṟinta maruntukaḷai avaṉukkuk kūṟiṉārkaḷ.
pāpilōṉiṉ viḻukai
pāpilōṉiṉ kaṭaici aracaṉ nepucaṭṉēcar. ivaṉ 54 kōyil kaḷaik kaṭṭiṉāṉ. ivaṉ taṉatu vāḻnāḷiṉ iṟuti nāṉku āṇṭukaḷil paittiyam koṇṭiruntāṉ. ki.mu. 562il ivaṉ maraṇamaṭaintāṉ. ivaṉ maraṇa maṭainta muppatu āṇṭukaḷuḷ pāpilōṉ irācciyam tuṇṭu tuṇṭākac citaṟuṇṭatu. ivaṉukkup piṉ āṭci naṭattiya pōṉiṭas eṉpavaṉ irācciyattil karuttuc celuttātu cumēriyāviluḷḷa aḻipāṭu kaḷai veṭṭi ārāycci ceyvatil kālattaip pōkkiṉāṉ. irāṇuvat til kuḻappa muṇṭāyiṟṟu. vāṇikattilum kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷilum paḻakiya makkaḷ pōrai maṟantārkaḷ. kurumār aracāṅka atikārattaik kaippaṟṟit tamatu paikaḷai nirappiṉar. ippoḻutu cairas eṉṉum pāracīka aracaṉ pāpilōṉai veṟṟikoṇṭāṉ. iraṇṭu talaimuṟaikaḷākap pāpilōṉ pāracīkar āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. piṉpu alakcāntar veṟṟiyāḷaṉākip pāpilōṉai āṇṭāṉ. pāpilōṉiyariṭamiruntē kirēkkarkaḷ, kaṇakku, vāṉacāttiram, maruntu, ilakkaṇam,nikaṇṭu, carittiram, tattuva cāttiram mutaliyavaikaḷaip peṟṟaṉar. urōmar kirēkkariṭamiruntu ivaikaḷaip peṟṟaṉar. kirēkka moḻiyil ulōkaṅkaḷ, irācikaḷ, niṟaikaḷ, aḷavai kaḷ, icaikkaruvikaḷ, maruntuc carakkukaḷaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷ perumpālum pāpilōṉiya moḻikkuriyaṉavē. kirēkkak kaṭṭakkalai ekiptiya, kirīttiya, pāpilōṉiyak kaṭṭaṭak kalaikaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi eḻuntatu. cuṟṟi varap paṭikkaṭṭukkaḷuṭaiya pāpilōṉiya kōpuraṅkaḷaip piṉpaṟṟi mukamatiya kōyiṟ kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ amaikkap paṭṭaṉa. hamirapiyiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷiliruntu urōmariṉ niyāyac caṭṭaṅkaḷ utittaṉa.
cintuveḷi
cintu eṉpatu intiyāviṉ vaṭamēṟkiluḷḷa ōr āṟu. ivvāṟṟiṉ peyaraiyē nāvalantīvu eṉṟu peyar peṟṟirunta ematu nāṭṭukku mēṟkut tēca makkaḷ peyarāka vaḻaṅkiṉar. cintu eṉpatu intu eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. cintu āṟṟiṉ camaveḷikaḷil aḻipāṭukaḷ 1 pala kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷuḷ arappā, mokañcatarō eṉṉum iru iṭaṅkaḷiluḷḷa aḻipāṭukaḷ paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷarāl akaḻap paṭṭaṉa. ivai aḻintu maṇmēṭāka māṟiya iraṇṭu periya nakaraṅkaḷākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. aṅku vāḻnta paḻaiya makkaḷiṉ varalāṟukaḷai aṟintu koḷvataṟkēṟṟa pala paḻam poruḷkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
cintuveḷip putaiporuḷ ārāyccikku muṉpu airōppiya amerikka ārāycciyāḷar, yūpirātas taikiras āṟukaḷum nīla natiyum nīr pāyccukiṉṟa mecapatōmiyā, acīriyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷē nākarikattiṉ toṭṭil eṉak karuti vantārkaḷ. an nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ paḻamaiyai oppiṭumpōtu intiya nākarikam pacciḷaṅkuḻavi eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. ākavē, intiya varalāṟṟai alakcāntariṉ paṭai eṭuppu allatu mauriya araca paramparai ārampattiliruntu toṭaṅkuvatu vaḻakkamāyiruntu vantatu. cintuveḷiyilē arappā, mokañcatarō eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa paḻam poruḷ kaḷ, intiya nākarikattiṉ paḻamaiyai mēṟku āciyā, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ toṉmaiyōṭu ottatāka māṟṟiyuḷḷaṉa. cintu veḷip putaiporuḷ ārāycci yiṟ kiṭaitta paḻam poruḷkaḷ pala cumēriyāviṟ kiṭaitta paḻam poruḷkaḷai ottavai. ākavē cintu nākarikam eṉpatu intu-cumēriya nākarikam eṉak koḷḷappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. mēlum kiṭaitta paḻamporuḷkaḷ cintu nākarikam intiyāvil tōṉṟi vaḷarnta paḻaiya nākarikam eṉpataik kāṭṭiṉa. cintu veḷi nākarikattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 3,500 varaiyil eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu.
arappā eṉṉum paḻaiya aḻipāṭu pañcāp mākāṇattil iravi āṟṟukku aṇmaiyiluḷḷatu. iravi āṟu cintu natiyiṉ oru kiḷai. arappā, mokañcatarōvukku vaṭakkē nāṉūṟu mail tūrattiluḷḷatu. arappā aḻipāṭu 1920kkum 1921kku miṭaiyil mutaṉmutal akaḻntu pārkkappaṭṭatu.
mokañcatarō ilārkāṉāvukku vaṭakkē irupattaintu mail tūrattil uḷḷatu. mokañcatarō eṉpataṟku iṟantavarkaḷiṉ mēṭu eṉṟu poruḷ. ivaḻipāṭu 240 ēkkar nilap parappaik koṇṭirukkiṟatu. itil tōṇṭi ārāyappaṭṭa pakuti 13 ēkkarkaḷē. cintu āṟu mokañcatarō vukkuk kiḻakkē mūṉṟarai mail tūram taḷḷi ōṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil atu mokañcatarōviṉ pakkattil ceṉṟirukkalām. in nakar muṉpu āṟṟu maṭṭattiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. cintu āṟu kālantōṟum uyarntu vantamaiyāl makkaḷ nakarattai uyarttip putiya kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷai amait tārkaḷ. ivvāṟu oṉṟiṉmēl oṉṟākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa ēḻu nakaraṅkaḷ mokañcatarōviṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ēḻāvatu paṭai kaṟkāla iṟutiyilum ulōka kāla ārampattilum amaikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷai uṭaiyatu.
vītikaḷ: mokañcatarōvil akaṉṟa periya vītikaḷ vaṭakkiliruntu teṟkē celkiṉṟaṉa. avai oṉṟukkoṉṟu cama tūrattiluḷḷaṉa. periya vītikaḷiliruntu ciṟiya vītikaḷ kiḷaiviṭṭuc celkiṉṟaṉa. kiḷai vītikaḷ ivvāṟu celvatāl nakar pala pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ovvoru pirivilum oṭuṅkiya vītikaḷ pala celkiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷiṉ ōraṅkaḷil vīṭukaḷ neruṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. ovvoru ciṟiya vītiyilum potukkiṇaṟu uṇṭu. perumpālum ovvoru vīṭṭukkum kiṇaṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
vīṭukaḷ: vīṭukaḷ pala perumaiyuṭaiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iraṇṭu aṟaikaḷukkuk kuṟaivāyuḷḷa vīṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai. cila vīṭukaḷ araṇmaṉaikaḷpōl periyaṉa. vīṭukaḷ vicālamākavum kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭa kuḷikkum aṟaiyuṭaiyaṉavākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāpilōṉilum mokañcatarōvilum naṭuvil muṟṟamum muṟṟattaic cuṟṟi nāṟpuṟamum aṟaikaḷumāka vīṭukaḷ amaikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṟṟattukkuc ceṅkaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭaṉa. mēṉmāṭi puḻaṅkavum, nittirai koḷḷavum, kuḷikkavum, kīḻmāṭi camaikkavum, paṇṭaṅkaḷaic cēmittu vaikkavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. vītiyiliruntu vīṭṭukkuc cella oru vāyil māttiram iruntatu. cuvarukku veḷiyē yātum irukkavillai.
kiṇaṟukaḷ: kiṇaṟukaḷiṉ akalam palavāṟu iruntatu. cila kiṇaṟu kaḷiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ēḻu aṭi iraṇṭu aṅkulam. mikac ciṟiya kiṇaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu ōr aṭi ōr aṅkulam. cātāraṇa kiṇaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu iraṇṭu aṭi iraṇṭu aṅkulam. periya nīrāṭum kēṇiyiṉ pakkattē kāṇappaṭum kiṇaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu āṟu aṭi iraṇṭu aṅkulam. perum pālum ovvoru vīṭṭukkum ovvoru kiṇaṟu iruntatu. potuk kiṇaṟukaḷ iraṇṭu vīṭukaḷiṉ mattiyil iruntaṉa. kiṇaṟṟaic cuṟṟic ceṅkaṟkaḷ patikkap paṭṭiruntaṉa. ceṅkaṟkaḷai oṉṟōṭoṉṟu cērttup piṭikkac ceyvataṟkuk kaḷimaṇ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. kiṇaṟṟuk kaṭṭu ōraṅkaḷil kayiṟu urāynta aṭaiyāḷam kāṇappaṭuvatāl, ēṉaṅ kaḷaik kayiṟṟil kaṭṭik kaiyiṉāl iḻuttut taṇṇīr eṭukkap paṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
kuḷikkum aṟai: ovvoru vīṭṭukkum taṉiyākak kuḷikkum aṟaikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaikaḷaic cuṟṟip patikkappaṭṭirunta ceṅkaṟkaḷ mītu taṇṇīr cuvaṟic cellamuṭiyāmal mēlē oruvakaik kaṟuppup poruḷ pūcap paṭṭiruntatu. kuḷikkumaṟai vīṭṭiṉ vāyiṟ pakkamāka viruntatu. ataṉaic cuṟṟic ceṅkaṟ patikkappaṭṭa tarai oru mūlaiyai nōkkic carivāka iruntatu. mēl vīṭṭil kuḷikkum aṟai iruntateṉpatai mēlē yiruntu kīḻē ceṅkuttākac cellum kuḻāykaḷaik koṇṭu aṟiyappaṭṭatu.
tarai: vīṭṭiṉ tarai ceṅkaṟ patikkappaṭṭiruntatu; allatu maṇ iṭṭu aṭittu maṭṭañ ceyyappaṭṭiruntatu. maṇ iṭṭu aṭittut taraiyai maṭṭañ ceyvatu potu nikaḻcci. cila vīṭukaḷukkuc cūḷaiyiṭāta kaḷimaṇ kaṟkaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
katavukaḷ: katavukaḷ marattiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭavai. cuvar muṭivu eytu miṭattuk katavu vaikkappaṭṭiruntatu; atu cuvariṉ mattiyil vaikkappaṭa villai. katavu nilaiyiṉ akalam mūṉṟu aṭi mūṉṟu aṅkulam mutal mūṉṟu aṭi pattu aṅkulam varaiyil iruntatu.
caṉṉalkaḷ: cātāraṇa vīṭukaḷukkup perumpālum veḷiccuvar kaḷil caṉṉalkaḷ irukkavillai. caṉṉalkaḷ cuvaril viṭappaṭṭa veḷikaḷpōla iruntaṉa. periya nīrāṭum kēṇiyaic cuṟṟi amaikkap paṭṭa cuvaril caṉṉalkaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ: perumpālum ellā vīṭukaḷukkum mēlē ēṟic cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uṇṭu. avai cuvariṉ etirē allatu iraṇṭu vīṭukaḷukku iṭaiyē uḷḷa iṭaiveḷiyil ceṅkuttāka amaikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ kūraikku māttiramaṉṟu; mēlmāṭikkum cel kiṉṟaṉa. vītiyiliruntu mēl māṭikkuc cellum paṭikkaṭṭukaḷum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. avvakaip paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ uḷḷa vīṭukaḷil vevvēṟu kuṭumpattiṉar vāḻntārkaḷ ākalām. paṭikkaṭṭukaḷ oṭuṅkiyavaiyāka vum, paṭi ovvoṉṟum patiṉoru aṅkula uyaramum āṟu mutal eṭṭu aṅkula akalamu muṭaiyatāka uḷḷatu.
kūrai: kūraikaḷ taṭṭaiyāṉavai. avai marattiṉāl amaikkap paṭṭavai. cila vīṭukaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷil maraṅkaḷ iṭappaṭṭa tuvāraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maraṅkaḷmītu palakaikaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. vaṟiya makkaḷ maraṅkaḷ mītu nāṇaṟpāyai virittu ataṉmēl marattūḷum umiyum kalantu kuḻaitta kaḷimaṇṇai iṭṭu meḻuki maṭṭañ ceytārkaḷ.
cuvarkaḷ: vīṭṭuc cuvar ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. akalamāṉa cuvarkaḷiṉ veḷippuṟattukkum uṭpuṟattukkum iṭaiyē kaḷimaṇ iṭṭu nirappappaṭṭatu. veḷiccuvarkaḷ uḷḷē carintu cellumpaṭi amaikkap paṭṭaṉa.
katavillāta aṟaikaḷ, malakkūṭaṅkaḷ: katavillāta cila aṟaikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivai nilattukkuk kīḻ uḷḷa aṟaikaḷāka allatu malak kūṭaṅkaḷāka irukkalām. iraṇṭu malak kūṭaṅkaḷait tavira vēṟu malak kūṭaṅkaḷ ituvarai kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭavillai.
kaḻivu nīr cellum kālvāy: cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷil kaḻivunīr veḷiyē celvataṟkuk kaṟpatitta vāykkālkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. maṟunāṭukaḷil viḷaṅkiya ak kāla nakaraṅkaḷil ivai kāṇappaṭavillai. ellā vīṭukaḷukkum vītip pakkamuḷḷa cuvaril oṉṟu allatu iraṇṭu tuvāraṅkaḷ viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivai vaḻiyāka aḻukku nīr veḷiyē yuḷḷa kālvāyiṟ ceṉṟu viḻuntatu. aḻukku nīr viḻumiṭattil pāramāṉa poruḷkaḷ taṅki niṟkumpaṭi kaṟpatitta kuḻikaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ovvoru vītiyilum 18 aṅkulam mutal 2 aṅkulam varai āḻamuḷḷa kālvāykaḷ iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉ mēlē ceṅkaṟkaḷ iṭṭu ivai mūṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ic ceṅkaṟkaḷ vēṇṭiyapōtu eṭukkakkūṭiyaṉa. ciṟiya kālvāykaḷil cellum kaḻivu nīr periya vāykkāliṟ ceṉṟu viḻuntatu. iv vāykkālkaḷum aḻukkut taṅki niṟkum kuḻikaḷum iṭai yiṭai cuttañ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vāykkālkaḷ cuttañ ceyyappaṭṭa iṭattil maṇal kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. mokañcatarōk kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭum tūṇkaḷ caturamāṉavai. cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷiṉ kaṭṭaṭa amaippup pāpilōṉiya allatu ekiptiya kaṭṭaṭa amaippaiviṭa atikam vaḷarcci yaṭaintiruntatu eṉa ṭākṭar pirāṅpōrṭ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
periya tāṉiyak kaḷañciyamum kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷum: arappāvil 169 aṭi nīḷamum, 135 aṭi akalamumuḷḷa tāṉiyak kaḷañciyamoṉṟu kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. iraṇṭu varicaikaḷil taṉittaṉi ovvoṉṟilum ēḻu vīṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya patiṉāṉku kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. iv viraṇṭu niraikaḷaiyum oru pātai pirikkiṉṟatu. ip pātaiyai āṟu pātaikaḷ kuṟukkē pirikkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl ovvoru kaṭṭaṭattaiyum cuṟṟi veḷi irukkiṉṟatu. ovvoru vīṭṭuk kum oru muṟṟamum iraṇṭu aṟai kaḷum uṇṭu. ciṟiya aṟai vāyiṟ pakkattilum periya aṟai muṟṟattukkup piṉṉālum uḷḷaṉa. cila iṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkut tūṇkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl vītikaḷukku veḷiccam iṭap paṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. vītikaḷukku appuṟattil kāṇappaṭum aḻukkuk kuviyalkaḷāl nakar naṉku cuttañ ceyyappaṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. cila iṭaṅkaḷil aḻukkukkup patil caṭṭi pāṉai uṭaivukaḷum aḻukiya ilai taḻaikaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ciṟpam: uruvaṅkaḷ mirutuvāṉa kaṟkaḷil veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa maṉita uruvaṅkaḷil oṉṟu, alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa pōrvaiyai iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāl vantu valatu puyattukkuk kīḻē cellumpaṭi aṇintirukkiṉṟatu. ataṟkuk kuṟukiya tāṭiyum kaṉṉa mīcai yum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. talai mayir naṭuvē vakirntu viṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. talaiyaic cuṟṟi nāṭā oṉṟu kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
cintuveḷiyiṟ kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭavaikaḷuḷ muttiraikaḷ, uṭampil aṇiyum poruḷkaḷ pōṉṟavai mutaṉmaiyuṭaiyaṉa. ciṟiya uruvaṅkaḷiṉ kālkaḷum kaikaḷum uṭaintuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷ vaṭivaṅkaḷiṟ pala peṇ uruvaṅkaḷākum. araiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa oṭukkamāṉa tuṇiyillāviṭil avai nirvāṇat tōṟṟam aḷippaṉavākum. perumpālum ellā vaṭivaṅkaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷ aṇintuḷḷaṉa. aṇil, kuraṅku mutaliyavai niṟak kaṟkaḷilum āmai ōṭṭilum veṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. niṟakkallil veṭṭappaṭṭa aṇil iraṇṭu aṅkula uyaramuḷḷatu. aṇiliṉ vāl nimirntu niṟkiṉṟatu. atu muṉṉaṅ kālkaḷ iraṇṭiṉālum etaiyō piṭittirukkiṉṟatu.
veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭṭiyap peṇṇiṉvaṭivam oṉṟu mikavum kavarccikkuriyatā yuḷḷatu. avaḷ kai mūṭṭuvaraiyum vaḷaiyaṅkaḷai aṇintirukkiṟāḷ. nīlaniṟak kallil veṭṭappaṭṭa oru maṉita vaṭivam aṭṭaṇaik kāliṭṭukkoṇṭu kaṭṭiliṉ mītu iruk kiṉṟatu. iraṇṭu pakkaṅkaḷilum ovvoruvar muḻaṅkāl paṭintu niṉṟu vaṇaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. piṉpuṟattil oru pāmpu uḷḷatu. iṉṟu cintuviṟ kāṇappaṭum makkaḷ mokañcatarō makkaḷaiviṭa vēṟāṉavarkaḷallar eṉac car ārtar kīt kūṟiyuḷḷār.
āṭavar iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāl vantu iṭatu puyattukkuk kīḻē cellumpaṭi pōrvai aṇintiruntārkaḷ. ivvaḻakkē nāḷaṭaivil pūṇūl aṇiyum vaḻakkāka māṟiṟṟu. iṉṟu uṭukkum ‘vēṭṭi’ pōṉṟa uṭaiyai āṭavar araiyil aṇintaṉar. pañciṉālum kampaḷiyiṉālum uṭaikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ūcikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ taikkap paṭṭa uṭaikaḷaiyum aṇintārkaḷ ākalām.
āṭavar talaiyil mayirai vaḷaraviṭṭuk kuṟuka veṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. cilar mayiraip piṉpuṟattil kuṭumiyāka muṭintu nāṭāviṉāṟ kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. iṉṟu malaiyāḷip peṇkaḷ muṭivatupōl mayir ucciyil muṭiyap paṭṭatu. cila uruvaṅkaḷiṉ mayir piṉpuṟamākat toṅkukiṉṟatu. āṭavar mēlutaṭukaḷiṉ mayirai maḻittārkaḷ allatu mayirai oṭṭa veṭṭi ṉārkaḷ.
āparaṇaṅkaḷ: āṭavarum makaḷirum āparaṇaṅkaḷ aṇin tārkaḷ. kaḻuttaṇi, talaikkuk kaṭṭum nāṭā, mōtiram, puyavaṇi mutaliyavai āṇka ḷālum peṇkaḷālum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. kālaṇi, kātu vaḷaiyaṅkaḷ, tōṭu, alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa ūcikaḷ, mūkku vaḷaiyam mutaliyavaikaḷaip peṇkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. celvar poṉ, veḷḷi, niṟakkaṟkaḷ, tantam, vilaiyuyarnta kaṟkaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷāṟ ceyta āparaṇaṅkaḷaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ōṭu, elumpu, cempu, cūḷaiyiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa nakaikaḷ vaṟiya makkaḷāl payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ arai yil aṇiyum paṭṭikaikaḷ maṇi kōtta vaṭaṅkaḷāl ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷiṉ mukappuk kaḷ poṉ, cempu mutaliyavai kaḷālāṉavai. maṇikaḷaik kōttu mālaikaḷ ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. vayirak kaṟkaḷaiyum maṇikaḷaiyum tuḷaiyiṭuvataṟkup pōtiya tiṟamai vēṇṭum. cempil ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭṭiyap peṇ kaimuṭṭa vaḷaikaḷ aṇintirukkiṉṟāḷ. kaivaḷaikaḷ poṉ, veḷḷi, cempu, veṇkalam, niṟak kaṟkaḷ, cūḷai yiṭṭa kaḷimaṇ mutaliyavaikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aḻakiya kaivēlai ceyyappaṭṭa niṟakkaṟkaḷum talaimuṭikkuc cerukum palavakai ūcikaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avai veṇkalam, cempu, elumpu, niṟakkaṟkaḷ, cuṇṇāmpukkal mutaliyavaikaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavai. talaikkuc cerukum ūciyil mūṉṟu kuraṅkukaḷ oṉṟai oṉṟu piṭittuk koṇṭiruppatākiya kāṭci kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷ: cintuveḷi aḻipāṭukaḷil kiṭaitta mēṉi miṉukkup poruḷkaḷ pala kiṣ, ūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kiṭaittavai pōṉṟavai. mēṉimiṉukkup poruḷkaḷ vaikkum peṭṭikaḷ marattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. niṟakkaṟkaḷiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa vum nāṉku aṟaikaḷ uḷḷaṉavumākiya peṭṭi kaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvakaip pēḻaikaḷ kirēttāvil māttiram kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭi veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. atu nīṇṭa vaṭṭa vaṭivuṭaiyatu. kaṇṇukku maitīṭṭum kuccikaḷ veṇkalattiṉālum cempiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. yāṉait tantattil ceyyappaṭṭa cīppukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. mayir maḻikkum kattikaḷ cempiṉālum veṇkalattiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
vīṭṭup poruḷkaḷ: kal, ōṭu, niṟakkal, tantam, ulōkam mutaliyavaikaḷāl vīṭṭupporuḷkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. kal āyutaṅ kaḷukkup patil ulōka āyutaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. niṟakkal, vēlaippāṭuṭaiya ēṉaṅkaḷ ceytaṟku māttiram payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. vīṭṭu ēṉaṅkaḷ perumpālum maṇṇiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. aṭukkaḷai yil payaṉpaṭuttum ēṉaṅkaḷ, kiṇṇaṅkaḷ, taṭṭukkaḷ, cāṭikaḷ, pāṉai caṭṭikaḷ mutaliya palavakaiyiṉa. nīr aruntum ēṉaṅkaḷiṉ aṭippākam muṉaiyuṭaiyatu. nīr uṇṇum ēṉaṅkaḷ pala, kiṇaṟṟaic cuṟṟi uṭaintu kiṭappatāl avai oru muṟai māttiram payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa eṉat terikiṉṟatu. ūciyum, tuḷaiyiṭum karuvikaḷum cempiṉālum tantattiṉālum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. kōṭari, arivāḷ, katti, mīṉ piṭikkum tūṇṭil, kuṭaiyum karuvikaḷ pōlvaṉa veṇkalattiṉālum cempiṉālum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. valaikaḷai nīril āḻttuvataṟku īyat tuṇṭukaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paṭukkaikaḷum cāyvu nāṟkālikaḷum muṉ aṟaiyil vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. cila nāṟkālikaḷiṉ pātaṅkaḷ nāḻikai vaṭṭam pōlavum, iṭapattiṉ kāl pōlavum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa. iṭapakkāl uḷḷa nāṟkālikaḷ ekiptiya mutal aracaparamparaik kālam mutalākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. viḷakkukaḷ, ōṭu, maṇ, cempu mutaliyavaikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. vītikaḷilum viḷakkukaḷ iruntaṉa. meḻukut tiri vaittu erikkun tāḷ oṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu.
vēḷāṇmai: kōtumaiyum, vāṟkōtumaiyum, nellum viḷai vikkappaṭṭaṉa. maṇveṭṭikkup patil kalappai payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatō eṉpatu aṟiya muṭiyavillai. pērīntiṉpaḻa vitaikaḷ mokañcatarōvilum arappāvilum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl aṅkup pērīntu vaḷarntateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
uṇavu: kōtumai, vāṟkōtumai, arici, kāykaṉikaḷ, pāl mutaliyaṉa uṇavākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. arappāvil payaṟum eḷḷum payiriṭappaṭṭaṉa māṭu, āṭu, paṉṟi kōḻi, āmai mutaliyavai kaḷiṉ iṟaicciyum, mīṉ, mīṉ-karuvāṭu, kaṭal uyirkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum uṇavākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
vīṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ: māṭu, erumai, āṭu, yāṉai, paṉṟi, oṭṭakam, nāy mutaliyaṉa vīṭṭil vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. maṇṇiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa erumai, kāṇṭāmirukam, puli, kuraṅku, karaṭi, muyal, aṇil mutaliya viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl akkāla makkaḷ ivaikaḷaip paṟṟi aṟintiruntārkaḷ eṉa viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. kaḻutai, pūṉai, kīri, mayil, kōḻi mutaliyaṉavum avarkaḷāl aṟiyappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷ: caturaṅkam, kōli mutaliyavai mukkiya viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷākum. caturaṅkam āṭum kāykaḷ pala kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. avai kaḷimaṇṇiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavai.
viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷ: maṇṇiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa maṉitar, vilaṅkukaḷ, talaiyāṭṭum vilaṅkukaḷ, kōḻi, puṟā vaṭivāṉa ūtu kuḻalkaḷ, kilukiluppai, ciṟuvar viḷaiyāṭum pāṉai caṭṭi, iraṇṭu cakkaraṅkaḷil vaikkappaṭṭuk kayiṟu kōttu iḻuppataṟkuk kaḻuttil tuḷai iṭappaṭṭa āṭṭuk kaṭā, viḷaiyāṭṭu vaṇṭi mutaliyavai viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷiṟ cila. iṅkuk kāṇappaṭṭa viḷaiyāṭṭu vaṇṭikaḷ ki.mu. 3,200il ūr eṉṉum mēṟku āciya nakarilē kalliṟ poṟikkap paṭṭa tēr pōṉṟaṉa. ivai uḷ nāṭṭilēyē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatu caṅku tarōviṟ kāṇappaṭṭa viḷaiyāṭṭup poruḷkaḷāl naṉku aṟiyap paṭukiṉṟatu.
pōkkuvarattu: paḻaiya cintuveḷi makkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttiya vaṇṭikaḷ iṉṟu pañcāppilum cintuvilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum vaṇṭikaḷai ottavai. arappāvil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa cempiṉāṟ ceyta vaṇṭiyiṉāl akkāla vaṇṭi, veyyilaiyum maḻaiyaiyum tāṅka mēlē mēṟkaṭṭi kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṉṟaiya ‘ekkā’ vaṇṭi pōṉṟateṉat terikiṉṟatu.
niṟaiyum aḷavum: cintuveḷi makkaḷ pala vakaiyāṉa niṟaikaḷai vaḻaṅkiṉar. periya niṟaikaḷ kūriya talai uṭaiyaṉavāyum kayiṟu kōkkak kūṭiya tuḷaiyuṭaiyaṉavāyum iruntaṉa. ciṟiya niṟaikaḷ iruṇṭa narai niṟak kaṟkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavai. avai ‘pīppā’1 vaṭivāṉavai. ellam, mecapetōmiyā niṟaikaḷum ivvakaiyiṉavē.
necavu: mokañcatarō vīṭukaḷil nūl nūṟkum katirkaḷ kāṇap paṭṭaṉa. pañcu, kampaḷikaḷil nūl nūṟṟal potu nikaḻcciyāka iruntatu. vilai ēṟappeṟṟa niṟakkaṟkaḷilum ceyyappaṭṭa nūl nūṟkum katirkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl celvarum vaṟiyarum nūl nūṟṟārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. āṭaikaḷukkup palavakaic cāyaṅkaḷ ūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
pōr āyutaṅkaḷ: kōṭari, īṭṭi, vēl, vil, ampu, taṇṭu, kavaṇ, vāḷ, kēṭakam mutaliyaṉa pōril payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa: kōṭarikaḷ cūcāviṟ kiṭaittavai pōṉṟaṉa. vāḷkaḷ cempiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavai. avaikaḷukku iru pakkaṅkaḷilum alaku uṇṭu. ivai ak kālattil ekiptil kāṇappaṭṭa avvakaip poruḷkaḷōṭu oppiṭṭup pārkkat takuntavai. meyuṟai allatu kavacam tōliṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu.
maruntu: cintuveḷi aḻipāṭukaḷil cilācattu eṉṉum maruntu kāṇappaṭṭatu. itu nīriḻivu, paciyiṉmai, vātam, īraṟ kōḷāṟu mutaliyavai kaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum maruntu. maruntiṉ poruṭṭu māṉ kompum piṟa iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. kāṇṭāmirukattiṉ kompum maruntiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. mantiravātam, tāyattup pōṉṟavai nōyai āṟṟattakkaṉa eṉṟu makkaḷ nampiṉārkaḷ.
vāṇikam: vaṭa intiyāvukkum teṉṉintiyāvukkum mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷukkum toṭarpu uṇṭāyiruntatu. cumēriyāvōṭu cintu nāṭu toṭarpu peṟṟiruntataik kāṭṭum cāṉṟukaḷ mikap pala uḷḷaṉa. ekiptōṭum kirēttā (Crete)vōṭum vāṇikam naṭaipeṟṟatu. muttirai oṉṟil marakkalam kāṇap paṭukiṉṟatu. paḻaiya mīṉōva muttirai, cumēriya uruḷai vaṭivāṉa muttirai, ekiptiya araca paramparaikku muṟpaṭṭa maṭpāṇṭaṅkaḷ eṉpavaikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatupōl im marakkalam mēlē uyarntu vaḷainta muṉpiṉ puṟaṅkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. itaṟkup pāymara millai. naṭuvē ōr aṟai irukkiṟatu. marakkalam celuttuvōṉ cukkāṉukkup pakkattē irukkiṉṟāṉ. mokañcatarō, ūr, kiṣ, ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkiṭaiyil vāṇika uṟavu iruntatu.
kaittoḻil: cintuveḷi makkaḷ ellā vakai maṭpāṇṭaṅ kaḷaiyum ceytārkaḷ. cilavaṟṟukku niṟam pūcappaṭṭaṉa; cilavaṟṟil miṉukkam koṭukkap paṭṭaṉa. mēlē kumiḻiyuḷḷa muttiraikaḷ, pāvaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa aḻakākac ceyyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. poṉ, veḷḷi, vilai uyarnta kaṟkaḷil ceyyappaṭṭa āparaṇa vakaikaḷ akkālap poṟkollaṉiṉ kaivēlait tiṟamaiyaik kāṭṭuvaṉa. muttiraikaḷiṟ cetukkappaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷ, puli, yāṉai, māṭu, maram, ceṭi mutaliyavai akkālac ciṟpaṉiṉ kaiviṉai māṭciyai veḷiyiṭuvaṉa. kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu kotta ruṭaiyavum, āṭaiyaik koṇṭu necavāḷaruṭaiyavum tiṟamaikaḷai aṟital amaiyum.
makkaḷ: āriyar varukaikkumuṉ vaṭanāṭu muḻumaiyum tirāviṭa makkaḷē vāḻntārkaḷ eṉap peṟappaṭukiṉṟamaiyālum, iṉṟum palucis tāṉattil vaḻaṅkum pirākūy moḻi tirāviṭattukku iṉamuṭaiyatāyk kāṇappaṭutalālum, piṟa kāraṇaṅkaḷālum mokañcatarō makkaḷ tirāviṭarēyāvar eṉat tuṇiyappaṭṭuḷḷatu. heras pātiriyār oli muṟaiyāka vācitta paḻaiya cintu veḷi eḻuttukaḷiṉ oli muṟaiyāṉa uccarippukaḷil perumpālāṉavai tamiḻc coṟkaḷāka uḷḷaṉa.
moḻi: cintuveḷi makkaḷ oru collaik kuṟikka oru kuṟiyīṭṭai vaḻaṅkiṉar. cīṉamoḻiyil vaḻaṅkum eḻuttukaḷ ivvakaiyiṉavē. mēṟku āciya, ekiptiya, ciṉṉa āciya, cintuveḷi eḻuttukaḷ orē aṭippaṭaiyi ṉiṉṟum ciṟcila vēṟupāṭukaḷuṭaṉ vaḷarcci yaṭaintuḷḷavaikaḷē eṉac cēr yāṉ mārcal, ṭākṭar pirāṅpōrṭ, heras pātiriyār mutali yōrum vēṟu palarum ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. teṉṉamerikkāvukku aṇmaiyiluḷḷa īsṭar tīvukaḷil kāṇappaṭṭa eḻuttukaḷil 150 eḻuttukaḷ varaiyil mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷiṉiṉṟu vaṭa intiya teṉṉintiya eḻuttukaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. piṉīciya eḻuttukkum pirāmi eḻuttukkum uṟavu uṇṭu. piṉīciya eḻuttiliruntu kirēkka eḻuttukaḷum, kirēkka eḻuttukaḷi liruntu urōmaṉ eḻuttukaḷum, urōmaṉ eḻuttukaḷiliruntu airōppiya moḻikaḷiṉ eḻuttukaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷai ottavai haitarāpāttilum tirunelvēliyilum kiṭaitta carittira kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa pāṉai caṭṭikaḷilum, ilaṅkaiyilum kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
“civappuk kallil veṭṭappaṭṭatum tiṟamaiyāṉa vēlaippāṭu uṭaiyatumāṉa ciṟuvaṉiṉ uruvam oṉṟu arappāviṟ kiṭaittatu. ataṉai nōkkumiṭattu atu kirēkkar ki.mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ ceyta kai vēlaippāṭu pōlat tōṉṟum; āṉāl atu ki.mu. 3,000 varaiyil ceyyappaṭṭatu. iv vuruvattaik koṇṭu aṟiyavaruvatu, ciṟpak kalaiyiṉ vaḷarcciyaic carittira mūlam aṟiya muṭiyāteṉpatēyākum. ivvulakam muḻumaiyilum intiyarē mikap paḻaiya ciṟpikaḷāvar eṉat teriyavarukiṉṟatu.”1
* * *
“mokañcatarōvil kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭa oru muttiraiyil veṭṭappaṭṭa iṭapattiṉ vaṭivam mikavum uyirppu uṭaiyatākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉaik kāṇum oruvar ciṟpakkalai vaḷarvatillai; āṉāl atu taṉatu pōkkai māṟṟik koḷkiṉṟatu eṉat taṉakkuḷḷē muṭivu ceytu koḷvar.”2
* * *
mokañcatarōp putaiporuḷ ārāycci, puttar piṟappataṟku muṟpaṭṭa kāla nilaimaikaḷai veḷiyiṭṭuḷḷatu. intiya aracāṅkat tāriṉ cācaṉap paricōtakarāyirunta hiranta cāttiri kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: mokañcatarō nakaril vāḻnta makkaḷ naṉku amaitta vicāla māṉa kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷōṭu oppiṭattakkavai, varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṟpaṭṭa ekiptilō mecapetōmiyāvilō vēṟu evviṭattilō irukka villai. aṅkuḷḷa kuḷikkum aṟaikaḷum, kaḻivu nīr ōṭum vāykkāl kaḷum aṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ ulaka nākarika makkaḷāl māttiram aṟiyap paṭum vāyppukaḷaiyum, ullāca vāḻkkaiyaiyum uṭaiyarāyiruntār kaḷeṉpatai aṟivikkiṉṟaṉa. itu. ki.mu. 5000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa nikaḻcci.”3

tirāviṭa intiyā
muṉṉurai
tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟai nūṟu vakaiyil virittu eḻutalām. avvāṟu nūlkaḷ veḷivariṉ potumakkaḷum māṇavarum tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ uṇmai varalāṟukaḷai naṉkukaṟṟu uṇmai aṟivar. iṉṟu paḷḷikkūṭaṅkaḷukku eṉa eḻutappaṭum varalāṟṟu nūlkaḷil kāṇappaṭuva tirāviṭa makkaḷaip paṟṟiya uṇmaic ceytikaḷ pala kāṇappaṭuvatillai. tirāviṭa intiyā eṉṉum innūl paṟpala ārāycci aṟiñar ārāyntu kaṇṭa uṇmaikaḷai ātāramākak koṇṭu kaṟpōr eḷitiṟ paṭittup poruḷ viḷaṅkum muṟaiyil amaintuḷḷatu. tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ poṟkālam iṟṟaikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ uḷḷatu. caṅkakālam tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ poṟkālattiṉ kaṭaikkālamāka virunta teṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. muṉpiṉ mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷ varaiyil tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ poṟkālam nilaviṟṟu. intiya makkaḷiṉ nākarikameṉpatu tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ nākarikamē. ivvuṇmaiyai iṉṟu varalāṟṟāciri yarkaḷ eṭuttukkūṟa muṟpaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷai, paṇṭitar cavarirāya piḷḷai kālam mutal aṟiyappaṭṭirunta paṟpala uṇmaikaḷ potumakkaḷiṭaiyē paravātum parappuvāraṟṟum kiṭantu iṉṟu paravuvatu ōr naṟkālattiṉ aṟikuṟiyē yākum.
ceṉṉai
1-4-1949 na.ci. kantaiyā.
tirāviṭa intiyā
tōṟṟuvāy
intiya makkaḷ varalāṟṟil tirāviṭa makkaḷ varalāṟu mika mutaṉmai yuṭaiyatu. iṉṟu intiya nākarikam eṉap peyar peṟuvatu āriya tirāviṭa nākarikaṅkaḷiṉ kalappiṉāl tōṉṟiyatu. āriya nākarikattukku muṟpaṭṭatu tirāviṭa nākarikam. itiṟ ciṟitum aiyappāṭu illai. nīṇṭa kālam tirāviṭa nākarikattaippaṟṟiya ceytikaḷ aṟiyappaṭātu kiṭantaṉa; ataṉāl tirāviṭariṉ paṇpāṭṭaik kuṟitta ceytikaḷ varalāṟṟāciri yarkaḷāl paṟpalavāṟu tirittuk kūṟappaṭalāyiṉa. iṉṟu tamiḻariṉ nākarikattaip pulappaṭuttum cāṉṟukaḷ pala kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa. icciṟiya nūl tirāviṭariṉ nākarikac ciṟappukkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu.
tirāviṭar piṟappiṭam
“tirāviṭa makkaḷ ayalnāṭukaḷiliruntu intiyāviṉ vaṭapakuti kaḷai aṭaintārkaḷ. piṉpu āriyar eṉṉum putiya cātiyiṉar aṅku vantaṉar. avarkaḷ tirāviṭarai veṉṟu teṟkē turattiṉar. avarkaḷ teṟkē vantu vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷē tamiḻar eṉappaṭuvōr.” eṉa mēlnāṭṭāciriyar cilar, pala āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ uṇmai aṟiyātu mayaṅki eḻutivaittaṉar. akkoḷkai iṉṟu ciṟitum ēṟṟuk koḷḷat takkatāyirukkavillai. paḷḷic ciṟuvarkaḷukku varalāṟu eḻutum āciriyar palar uṇmaiyallāta paḻaiya koḷkaiyaip piṉpaṟṟi eḻutivaruvatu varuntattakkatē yākum.
tirāviṭa makkaḷ veḷināṭukaḷiliruntu vantu intiya nāṭṭai aṭaintārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvataṟkēṟṟa ātāram oṉṟēṉum kāṇappaṭa villai. paḻaiya kaṟkālam mutal irumpukkālam varaiyil makkaḷ toṭarpākac ceytu payaṉpaṭuttiya āyutaṅkaḷum piṟaporuḷkaḷum intiya nāṭṭiṉ palvēṟu iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.1
tirāviṭa makkaḷ ayalnāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvōrkkut tuṇaiyāyiruppatu āpkāṉistāṉattiṉ oru pakutiyil tamiḻukku iṉamuṭaiya pirākūy moḻi vaḻaṅkuvatu. iṟṟaikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ cintu āṟṟu veḷikaḷil tirāviṭar nākarikam paraviyiruntatu. aṅku tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. immakkaḷiṉ toṭarpuṭaiya varkaḷē āpkāṉistāṉattil taṅki vāḻntārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ cantatiyiṉarē tirāviṭattiṉ campantamuṭaiya pirākūy moḻiyai iṉṟum pēcivarukiṉṟaṉar.1
tirāviṭa makkaḷ paḻamaitoṭṭē intiya nāṭṭil vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ paḻaiya nāṭu kumari muṉaikkut teṟkilum parantu kiṭantatu.
nākarikattiṉ paḻamai
mēṟku āciyā ekiptu mutaliya nāṭukaḷil paḻaiya iṭipāṭṭu mēṭukaḷ tōṇṭi ārāyappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aṅku kiṭaitta paḻam poruḷ kaḷaikkoṇṭu an nāṭukaḷiṉ nākarikam ēḻāyiram allatu eṇṇā yiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭateṉa ārāycciyāḷar tuṇintuḷḷaṉar. maicūrilē ciṟṟālrak eṉṉum iṭattilum cintuveḷiyilum ceyyap paṭṭa akaḻ ārāyccikaḷāl intiya nāṭṭiṉ nākarikamum annāṭṭu nākarikaṅkaḷai otta paḻamaiyuṭaiyateṉa aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. ekiptu, mēṟku āciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil nākarikattait tōṟṟuvittōr ayal nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum ceṉṟavarkaḷāvar eṉṟum, avarkaḷ intiya nāṭṭi ṉiṉṟum, ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉak koḷvataṟkuppala ētukkaḷ uḷḷaveṉṟum ciṟanta mēṟticai ārāycci aṟiñar palar pukaṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ.2
tamiḻum tirāviṭamum
iṉṟu tamiḻaraikkuṟikkat tirāviṭar eṉṉum peyarum vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu. tirāviṭam eṉṉum collaip paṟṟippalar palavāṟu ārāyntu kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. tirāviṭameṉpatu tamiḻ eṉṉum colliṉ uccarippu vēṟupāṭu eṉpatē ārāycci aṟiñar iṟutiyākak koṇṭa muṭivu. iṉṟu tirāviṭam eṉṉum col tamiḻ, teluṅku, malaiyāḷam, kaṉṉaṭam, tuḷu mutaliya moḻik kūṭṭattaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. tirāviṭar eṉṉum peyar im moḻik kūṭṭattukkuriya makkaḷaik kuṟikkiṉṟatu.1
cūḻalnilaikkēṟpa makkaḷ paṇpāṭu aṭaital
ulakil makkaḷ vāḻakkūṭiya iṭaṅkaḷ aintu vakaiyiṉa eṉṟu paḻantamiḻar kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ avvakai nilaṅkaḷait tiṇaikaḷ eṉṟaṉar. tiṇai eṉpatu tiṭ allatu tiṇ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. nilapparappu eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷ. tiṭ allatu tiṇ eṉpatiliruntē tiṭṭu, tiṭṭai, tiṭar, tiṇṇai mutaliya coṟkaḷ piṟantaṉa. tiṇ eṉpatu valimaiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. iṭaṅkaḷiṉ cūḻnilaikkēṟpavē makkaḷiṉ tiruttamum vaḷarcciyaṭaintatu. taṇṇīrillāta vaṟaṇṭa maṇaṟ pakuti pālai eṉavum, malaināṭu kuṟiñci eṉavum, malaināṭṭukkum kīḻnāṭṭuk kum iṭaippaṭṭa kuṟuṅkāṭṭu nilam mullaiyeṉavum, āṟṟōraṅkaḷ marutameṉavum, kaṭaṟkarai neytal eṉavum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ivvakai nilaṅ kaḷum tamiḻnāṭṭil ōraḷavu uṇṭu. makkaḷ tōṉṟiya kālam mutal tamiḻar teṉṉintiyāvil vāḻntuvarukiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ oru nilattiṉiṉṟu iṉṉoru nilattukkuc ceṉṟu paṭippaṭiyāṉa cīrtiruttaṅkaḷai aṭaintaṉar.
malaiyum malaicārnta nilamum
makkaḷ mutalmutalkuṭiyēṟi vāḻnta iṭam malai. mikamika nīṇṭa kālam maḻaiyiṉālum paruvakkāṟṟiṉālum tākkuṇṭatāl tēyntu pōṉa ciṟiya kuṉṟukaḷ pala teṉṉintiya pūmikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. im malai iṭaṅkaḷukkuk kīḻē taṇṭakam eṉṉum iruṇṭa kāṭu iruntatu. aṅku ciṅkam, puli, yāṉai, kāṭṭu erumai, ciṟuttai, maṉitaṉukku aḻivaic ceyyum pūcci vakaikaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ātikāla maṉitaṉ, veyyil, maḻai, vilaṅkukaḷ mutaliyavaikaḷiṉiṉṟu taṉṉaik kāppāṟṟikkoḷḷak kūṭiya kukaikaḷ malaiyiṭaṅkaḷiliruntaṉa. avaṉ taṇṇīraic cēmittu vaippataṟku maṇ pāttiraṅkaḷaic ceyya aṟintirukkavillai. nīrūṟ ṟukaḷ vaṟṟiya kālattil pāṟaikaḷiluḷḷa kuḻikaḷil nīr niṟaintiruntatu. avaṉ ataṉaik kaikaḷāl aḷḷip parukiṉāṉ; kālaṭiyiṟ kiṭanta kaṟ kaḷai eṭuttu avaikaḷiliruntu kōṭari, īṭṭimuṉai, ariyum karuvikaḷ, curaṇṭum karuvi mutaliyavai kaḷaic ceyyap paḻakikkoṇṭāṉ. in nilaiyiliruntu maṉitaṉiṉ cīrtiruttam vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu. mika muṟpaṭṭa tirutta kālam paḻaiya kaṟkālam eṉappaṭum. paḻaiya kaṟkāla nākarikattaik kāṭṭum kaiyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ kaṭappā, nellūr, vaṭa ārkkāṭu, ceṅkaṟpaṭṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
kuṟiñci nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷ paḻaṅkaḷ vitaikaḷ kiḻaṅkukaḷai uṇṭu vāḻntaṉar. paruvakāla māṟutalkaḷāl ivvuṇavup poruḷkaḷ, ciṟcila kālaṅkaḷil aritiṟkiṭaippavāyiṉa. ākavē avarkaḷ ūṉ uṇavaiyum koḷḷattoṭaṅkiṉar. avarkaḷ taṟkāppiṉ poruṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷōṭu atikam pōrāṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. ataṉāl avarkaḷ vēṭṭai āṭuvatil tiṟamai peṟṟaṉar. ākavē toṭakkattil maṉita ṉiṉ toḻil vēṭṭaiyāṭuvatākaviruntatu; appoḻutu avarkaḷ ōriṭattil taṅkātu alaintu tirintārkaḷ. ulakam muḻumaiyilum kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷa paḻaiya kaṟkāla āyutaṅkaḷ ellām orē vaṭiviṉa. itaṉāl ātikāla maṉitaṉ ulakil eṅkum alaintu tiripava ṉāyiruntāṉeṉat terikiṉṟatu.
kuṟiñci nilaccūḻal, maṉita cīrtiruttattiṟku aṭippaṭaiyākiya iraṇṭu putiyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭupiṭikkumpaṭi ceytatu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu villum ampum, maṟṟatu neruppu uṇṭākkutal. malaikaḷil mūṅkil atikam vaḷarkiṉṟatu. malaiyil vāḻum makkaḷ mūṅkiliṉ vaḷaiyun taṉmaiyai nōkki aṟintārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ataṉaip piḷantu iru muṉaikaḷilum koṭiyaik kaṭṭi nīṇṭa muṭkaḷai atil vaittu eyyap paḻakiṉārkaḷ. iṉṟum malaikaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ orē ampiṉāl oru puliyaik kollum tiṟamai peṟṟirukkiṟārkaḷ.
ātikāla makkaḷ malaiyil tī uṇṭāki erivataik kaṇṭārkaḷ. mūṅkil oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu urōñcuvatāl tīyuṇṭākiṉṟateṉpatai avarkaḷ aṟintārkaḷ; ākavē tāmum iraṇṭu taṭikaḷai oṉṟōṭu oṉṟai urōñcit tī uṇṭākkalāmeṉa eṇṇiṉārkaḷ. tām vēṭṭai yāṭik koṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟaicciyai vēkac ceyyum poruṭṭu avarkaḷ mutalil neruppaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
āṭavar vēṭṭaiyāṭac ceṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ kāṭṭiṟ ceṉṟu paḻaṅkaḷaip poṟukkiṉārkaḷ; kiḻaṅkukaḷai akaḻntārkaḷ; tām vāḻiṭaṅkaḷaic cuṟṟit tāṉē viḷainta malai nel, mūṅkilarici mutali yavaikaḷaiyum cēkarittārkaḷ. peṇkaḷiṉ aṭutta kaṭamai kuḻantai kaḷai vaḷarppatu. ivvakai nilaiyilirukkumpōtu avarkaḷ vīṭu amaikka aṟiyavillai. teṉṉintiya veppa nilaikku vīṭu vēṇṭiya tillai; mara niḻalkaḷum malaikkukaikaḷumē otukkiṭaṅkaḷākap payaṉ paṭṭaṉa. makkaḷ toṭakkattil vīṭukaḷai uṟaiviṭattiṉ poruṭṭu amaikkavillai; tamatu celvamākiya uṇavup poruḷkaḷaip pattirap paṭuttivaikkum poruṭṭu amaittaṉar. paḻaiya kaṟkāla maṉitaṉukku uṇavaic cēmittu vaikkum tēvai uṇṭāyirukkavillai. vīṭu illāmaiyālum, alaintu tiriyavēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamaiyāmaiyālum vīṭṭil taṅki vāḻvatākiya uṇarcci āṭavariṭaiyē eḻavillai. ākavē tāyāṭci muṟaiyāṉa vāḻkkai ātiyil vaḷarcciyaṭaiva tāyiṟṟu.
ivvakaiyāṉa vāḻkkaiyai iṉṉoṉṟum ūkkiyatu. muṟkāla maṉitaṉ āṭamparamāṉa maṇakkiriyaikaḷāl kaṭṭuppaṭṭirukkavillai. āṭavarum makaḷirum kātalittu maṇantaṉar. cila nāḷkaḷiṉ piṉ uṟavi ṉarai aḻaittu viruntu iṭṭaṉar. ituvē muṟkālat tirumaṇak kiriyai yāka viruntatu. tirumaṇam eṉṟum nilaiyāṉa taṉmaiyuṭaiyatāka virukka villai. taṉippaṭṭa cottu iṉmaiyum nilaiyāṉa vīṭu iṉmaiyum tāyāṭci muṟaiyāṉa vāḻkkaikku nīṇṭa kālam vāyppu aḷittaṉa.
tamatu mēṉiyai alaṅkarittuk koḷvatil āṭavarukkum makaḷi rukkum viruppu uṇṭu; ivviruppup peṇkaḷukku atikam. kuṟap peṇkaḷ ōyvu nēraṅkaḷil ōṭukaḷaip poṟukkiṉārkaḷ; avaikaḷai mālaiyākak kōttu aṇintārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ kātalar vēṭṭai yāṭuvatiṟ kiṭaitta pulinakam, pulippal pōṉṟavaikaḷaik koṭut tārkaḷ. avaikaḷaiyum avarkaḷ kaḻuttil aṇintārkaḷ. piṟkālattil ivaiyē tāliyiṉ vaṭivākamāṟiṉa. teṉṉintiyappeṇ tālitarittal tirumaṇam ceytukoṇṭamaikku aṭaiyāḷamākum. peṇkaḷiṉ iṉṉoru alaṅkāram taḻaiuṭai. taḻaiuṭai eṉpatu koṭiyiṟ kaṭṭiya taḻaikaḷālāṉa uṭai. ivvuṭai uṭukkum vaḻakkam iṉṟum cila kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉariṭaiyē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
maṇalum maṇalcārnta iṭaṅkaḷum
maṇalum maṇalcārnta iṭamum pālai eṉappaṭum. itu makkaḷ vāḻakkūṭiya iṭattiṉ ōr uṭpirivil aṭaṅkum. vēṭaṉ kāṭṭu vilaṅku kaḷait turattic cellumpōtu pālainilattil nilaiyillātu cilakālam taṅkuvāṉ. pālainilattil cilakālam taṅkuvōr allatu aṅku vāḻvōr maṟavar eṉappaṭuvar. maṟam vīrattai uṇarttum. ivar 1kaḷḷar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. pālai nilam ceḻumaiyaṟṟatātalālum pālainila makkaḷ pōrt toḻilil ciṟantavarkaḷ ātaliṉālum maṟavarum kaḷḷarum celvaraiyum maṟṟanilaṅkaḷil vāḻum valiyaṟṟavarkaḷaiyum koḷḷai yiṭṭu vāḻvārāyiṉar. itaṉāl maṟam eṉpatu koṭumaiyaiyum kaḷḷar eṉpatu tiruṭaraiyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkalāyiṉa.
kāṭum kāṭucārnta nilamum
kuṟiñci nilattil makkaḷ perukiyapōtu uṇavu curuṅkiṟṟu. appoḻutu makkaḷ ayaliluḷḷa mullai nilattiṟkuc celvārāyiṉar. appoḻutu avarkaḷ cīrtiruttattil iṉṉoru paṭiyai aṭaintaṉar. erumai, pacu, āṭu, cemmaṟiyāṭu pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷai ivarkaḷ paḻakki vaḷarttārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kuṟavarāyiruntapōtu nāy paḻakki vaḷarkkap paṭṭatu. mullai nilattil āṭumāṭukaḷ viraiviṟ perukiṉa. ākavē cottu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. itaṉāl kuṭumpaṅkaḷum, kuṭikaḷum tōṉṟi vaḷarntaṉa.
āṭavarum makaḷirum oruvarai oruvar kātalittalum, kātaliṉ aṭaiyāḷamākak kātalaṉ pulinakam, ilaiyuṭai mutaliyavaṟṟaik kātalikkuk koṭuttuk kiriyai iṉṟi maṇantukoḷvatumākiya maṇam kaḷavu eṉappaṭṭatu. kuṟiñci nilattiṟṟōṉṟiya ivvakai maṇaṅkaḷ niṉṟupōyiṉa. ippoḻutu kaṟpu oḻukkam tōṉṟiṟṟu. kātalar oruva rōṭu oruvar paḻakuvataṉmuṉ maṇac caṭaṅku iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. ilaika ḷālum malarkaḷālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa pantariṉkīḻ cuṟṟattiṉar tiraṇ ṭiruntu maṇakkiriyai naṭattappaṭṭatu.
cottuc cērattoṭaṅkiyatum, kaṟpu oḻukkam tōṉṟiyatum camūkam tantai āṭcimuṟaiyāka vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu. tantaiyē āṭumāṭu kaḷukkuc contamuṭaiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. nilaṅkaḷaip pirittāl āṭumāṭukaḷ mēyvataṟku muṭiyāmal pōkum. ākavē kūṭṭuk kuṭumpa vāḻkkai tōṉṟiṟṟu. tantai āṭciyil periya kuṭumpattalaivaṉ aracaṉpōl āyiṉāṉ. im muṟaiyil aracaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ. āṭci muṟai mutalil mullai nilattilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. kōṉ eṉpatu paḻaiya tamiḻccol. itu aracaṉaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. āycci eṉṉum col iṭaicciyaiyum araciyaiyum kuṟikkiṉṟatu.
maṟṟa nāṭukaḷil iṭaiyariṉ vāḻkkai iṭamviṭṭu iṭam alaintu tirivatākaviruntatu. teṉṉintiyā ceḻippuṭaiyatāyum ellāk kālaṅ kaḷilum āṭumāṭukaḷukku vēṇṭiya uṇavu kiṭaikkak kūṭiyatākavum iruntamaiyāl teṉṉintiya iṭaiyarukku iṭamviṭṭu iṭam cella vēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyirukkavillai. ākavē teṉṉintiya iṭaiyariṉ vāḻkkai ōriṭattil taṅkiyiruppatāka amaintatu.
iṭaiyar āṭu māṭukaḷai mēyccal nilattukku ōṭṭic ceṉ ṟārkaḷ. mantaikaḷ mēyumpōtu avarkaḷ maraniḻalil taṅkiyiruntārkaḷ; poḻutupōkkiṉ poruṭṭu mūṅkiṟ kuḻalil iṉiya irākaṅkaḷaip pāṭiṉārkaḷ. āṭu māṭu mēyppavarkaḷil oru piriviṉar kuṟumpar eṉap paṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ mayir nīṇṭu vaḷarum kuṟumpu āṭukaḷai vaḷarttaṉar. kuṟumpu āṭukaḷiṉ mayiriliruntu kampaḷi neyyappaṭṭatu.
kaṭalum kaṭalcārnta nilamum
itaṟku aṭutta paṭiyil kaṭaṟkarai nilam makkaḷāl kuṭiyēṟap paṭṭatu. iṅku vāḻnta makkaḷ karaiyai aṭutta kaṭalil mīṉ piṭittārkaḷ; piṉpu āḻamāṉa nīriṟ ceṉṟārkaḷ. kaṭaṟkaraiyil vāḻvōr paratavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ mīṉ piṭippatum marakkalañ ceyvatumāyiruntaṉa. mikapperiya marakkalaṅkaḷ kaṭṭumaraṅkaḷāyiruntaṉa. itaṉpiṉ tōlāl mūṭappaṭṭa kūṭaikaḷ tōṇikaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. neytal nilattil kiṭaikkum mukkiya poruḷkaḷ uppum mīṉum. paratavar ivaikaḷai maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu piṟa uṇavup poruḷkaḷukkup paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytaṉar. paratavar ivvāṟu vaṇikarāyiṉar. avarkaḷ tamatu paṇṭaṅkaḷaip poti māṭukaḷil ēṟṟic ceṉṟaṉar. intiyap poruḷkaḷai āpirikkā, arāpiyā mutaliya mēṟku nāṭukaḷukkum, kiḻakkē malāyā, cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkum ēṟṟic ceṉṟa kaṭalōṭikaḷ tōṉṟiṉārkaḷ. kaṭaṟkarai nilaṅkaḷ cila payirc ceykaikku ēṟṟavāyiruntaṉa. aṅku payiriṭappaṭṭatu.
vayalum vayalcārnta nilamum
maruta nilamakkaḷ nilattai uḻutu payiriṭṭārkaḷ. āṟṟōraṅkaḷil vāḻnta makkaḷ āṟṟu veḷḷattai vāykkāl vaḻiyāka vayalkaḷukkup pāycca aṟintiruntārkaḷ. ivvāṟu mika mika muṟkālattilēyē payirc ceykai teṉṉintiyāvil mika vaḷarcciyaṭaintiruntatu. āṟṟup paḷḷattākkukaḷukku appāl pañcu viḷaiyum nilam iruntatu. putiya kaṟkāla makkaḷ pañciliruntu nūl nūṟkavum nūlāl āṭai neyyavum aṟintiruntārkaḷ.
ippoḻutu makkaḷ maraṅkaḷāl vīṭu kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. tēvaikku atikamāṉa tāṉiyam vīṭukaḷil cēmittu vaikkappaṭṭatu. maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷi luḷḷa uppu, mīṉ, veṇṇey, tayir, kal āyutaṅkaḷ pōṉṟavaikkut tāṉiyam paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyappaṭṭatu. maruta nilattil paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyamuṭiyāta paṇṭaṅkaḷai veḷiyē koṇṭu cellavēṇṭiya avaciyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. paṇṭaṅkaḷaik koṇṭu celvataṟku vaṇṭikaḷ ceyyak kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. uṇavukkup payaṉpaṭakkūṭiya marañceṭi kaḷum kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu tirāviṭa nākarikam intiya nāṭṭilēyē tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭaintatu. intiyāviṉ ayaliliruntu nākarikam intiyāvai aṭaintateṉpataṟku ātāram ciṟitum kāṇappaṭavillai.1
pēccu
camaiyalum, uṭaiyum allāta iṉṉoṉṟum aṟiyappaṭṭatu. atu pēccu. pēccu icai campantappaṭṭatu allatu icai campantappaṭāta tāka irukkalām. pāṭalukku muṉ pēccu iruntateṉap palar karutukiṉ ṟaṉar. ātiyil pakuti icai campantamum, pakuti icai campantamillātatumā yirunta pēcciṉ vēṟupāṭē pāṭalum vacaṉamum eṉa vēṟu palar karutukiṉ ṟaṉar. itaṉai naṉku aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyavillai. ilakkiya vaḷarcciyil urai naṭaikku muṟpaṭṭatu pāṭal eṉat terikiṉṟatu. paṇ, pāṇ eṉpatu icaiyiṉ pakutikkup peyar. paṇ eṉpatiliruntu pāṭu, pāṭṭu mutaliya coṟkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. pāṭutal tamiḻariṉ ātikālap poḻutu pōkkukaḷil oṉṟu. pāṇar toṭakkattil pāṭakarāyiruntaṉar. nākarikam vaḷarntapōtu araṇmaṉaiyil aracāṅkap pāṭakar iruntaṉar. tamiḻar nākarikam vaḷarcci yuṟṟirunta kālattil pāṇar aracariṉ naṇparum avarukku ālōcaṉai kūṟuvōrumāyiruntaṉar.
camayam
maṉitaṉ āyutam ceyyum, uṇavaic camaikkum, uṭai aṇiyum, pēcum vilaṅkāka māttiram irukkavillai; camayamuṭaiya vilaṅkākavum vāḻntāṉ. avaṉ tāṉ virumpiyavaikaḷaip peṟavum, taṉṉai accuṟuttum tīmaikaḷaip pōkkavum valiya āṟṟalai vēṇṭik koḷḷum vaḻikaḷaiyum aṟintiruntāṉ. avvaḻikaḷ mantira vittai toṭarpāṉa kiriyaikaḷ. avai viruntu, pāṭṭu, kūttu vaṭiviliruntaṉa. piṟkālattil viruntu pāṭṭu kūttu eṉpaṉa vevvēṟākap pirikkap paṭṭaṉa. iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa ovvoru nilattilum teyvaṅkaḷum kiriyai kaḷum tōṉṟiṉa.
paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkaiyaik kaṇṇāṭi pōl kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻaiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ perumpālum maṟaintu pōyiṉa. vāṇikam, āṭci, toḻil, āṭci muṟaikaḷ kaiyāḷappaṭṭaṉa. ilakkiyaṅkaḷ perumpālum maṉappāṭañ ceyyappaṭṭuvantaṉa. paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pala iṟantupōyiṉa eṉpataṟkut tolkāppiyap poru ḷatikārattukku urai eḻutiya paḻaiya āciriyarkaḷ tuṟaikaḷ palavaṟṟukku ēṟṟa mēṟkōḷ kāṭṭamuṭiyāmal iruntamaiyē cāṉṟākum. ip poḻutu kiṭaittuḷḷa paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṉ vāḻkkai muṟaikaḷ avarkaḷ vāḻnta nilaṅkaḷukkēṟpat tōṉṟi vaḷarcci yaṭaintaṉa eṉpatait teḷivākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pāṭalkaḷ
tamiḻnāṭu nila amaippukkēṟpap pirikkappaṭṭirunta kālattil pāṭalkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. ovvoru nilattilum annilattiṉ vāḻkkaik kēṟṟa pāṭalkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ākavē tiṇai eṉpatu kuṟitta nilattuk kuriya pāṭal vakaiyaik kuṟippatāyiṟṟu. maṉitaṉiṉ kātal, pōr eṉpavai kaḷē pulavar pāṭutaṟku ēṟṟa karuttukkaḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ākavē pāṭalkaḷ iru vakaippaṭṭaṉa. akattiṇai, puṟattiṇai eṉpaṉa avaṟ ṟiṉ pirivukaḷ. akam eṉpatu kātaluṭaiya iruvar māttiram uṇarntu aṉupavikkak kūṭiyatu; puṟam maṟṟavarōṭu kalantu paṅkupaṟṟat takkatu. aintu nilaṅkaḷukkumuriya kātaṟ ceyalkaḷ aintu eṉakkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. kuṟiñci nilattukkuriyavai kātalar oruvarai oruvar kaṇṭu kātalittu uṭaṉ maṇattal, malaik kāṭci mutaliyaṉa. āṭavarum makaḷirum taṉit taṉiyē etirppaṭṭu kātalippataṟku ēṟṟa iṭam malaiyātalil kūṭalum kūṭal nimittamum kuṟiñci nilattukku uripporuḷākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
kātalaṉ kātaliyai curavaḻiyē peṇ vīṭṭār aṟiyātu koṇ ṭēkutal, kātalaṉ kātaliyai viṭṭup pirintu cellutal pōṉṟa poruḷ kaḷaip pālaip pāṭalkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. pirintu ceṉṟa kātalaṉ varun tuṇaiyum āṟṟiyiruppatākiya ceyalaik kūṟuvatu mullai. talai vaṉaip pirinta kātali avaṉ varavai niṉaintu iraṅkiyiruttal neytal. maruta nilattil vitaippu muṭintu aṟuppuk kālam varaiyil makkaḷ poḻutu pōkkutaṟku ēṟṟa oḻivu uṇṭu. akkālaṅkaḷil kātalaruk kiṭaiyil ūṭal nērum. itu perumpālum talaivaṉ parattaiyaruṭaṉ naṭpuk koṇṭiruttalāl uṇṭākum.
tiṇaikkuriya makkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷiṟ cila ikkālattil cātip peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kuṟavar, maṟavar, paratavar, iṭaiyar, vēḷāḷar eṉpaṉa avaṟṟuṭ cila.
tirāviṭanāṭu mika muṟkālattilēyē nākarikam peṟṟiruntatu
mika mika muṟkālattilēyē tirāviṭaruṭaiyanākarikam mika uyar vaṭaintiruntatu. nāṭṭiṉ ceḻippum vāṇikamum celvattai vaḷark kiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷiṉ kuḻappamillāta vāḻkkaiyum celvam vaḷarvataṟku maṟṟoru kāraṇam. vaṭanāṭṭu makkaḷ aṭikkaṭi aṉṉiyap paṭai eṭuppuk kaḷāl kalakkuṇṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaiyil vāḷaip piṭittukkoṇṭu vīṭṭaiyum uṟaviṉaraiyum kākkavēṇṭiyiruntatu. aṅku makkaḷukkum cottukkum pātukāppu irukkavillai. teṉṉāṭṭu makkaḷukku ivvakait tollai irukkavillai. nāṭṭiṉ celvattai vaṟṟac ceyyum periyapōrkaḷ teṉṉāṭṭil nikaḻavillai. teṉ kaṭalkaḷil kaṭaṟkoḷḷaikkārarum kāṇappaṭavillai. taraiyil amaiti nilaviṟṟu. ak kālattil aṟiyappaṭṭirunta kaṭalai aṭutta nāṭukaḷōṭu tamiḻar vāṇikam purintaṉar. pāpilōṉ, ekiptu, aciriyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷ intiyāvōṭu vāṇikam purintaṉa. nāṭu celvavaḷamaṭaintatu. vaṭakkē vāḻnta makkaḷaiviṭat teṟkē vāḻnta makkaḷ celvam paṭaittirun taṉar. kaṭaṟ payaṇaṅkaḷ teṉmēṟku intiyāvukkum pāpilōṉukku miṭai yil oḻuṅkāka naṭaipeṟṟatu. marakkalamōṭṭum mālumikaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷākaviruntaṉar. tantam, kuraṅku, mayil pōṉṟavaikaḷiṉ tamiḻp peyarkaḷ mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkiṉa. nilam ceḻippuṟṟiruntatu. kaṉikaḷil poṉ arikkappaṭṭatu. vairam, muttu, pavaḷam mutaliyavum atikam kiṭaittaṉa. tēkku, cantaṉam mutaliya maraṅkaḷ mēṟkut tēcaṅ kaḷukku ēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil kāṇappaṭum nāṭu, ūr eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tamiḻariṉ paḻaiya paṇpāṭṭai uṇarttuvaṉa. nāṭu eṉṉum col naṭu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. palavakai marañceṭikaḷai naṭṭu uṇ ṭākkum iṭam nāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. ūr eṉpataṟku vayal cūḻnta iṭam eṉpatu poruḷ. ūr eṉpatu uḻu eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. ūr eṉpatu uḻutalai uṇarttum. muṟkālattil nāṭṭaippaṟṟiyum ūraip paṟṟiyumuḷḷa tamiḻaruṭaiya karuttu ituvākum. paḻaiya cālatiyāviṉ talainakar 1ūr eṉappaṭṭatu. urōmariṉ nakaraik kuṟikkum peyar ūrps. ivai ūr eṉṉum tamiḻccol campantamāṉavai. nāṭu eṉpataṟku etirc col kāṭu. kāṭu eṉpataṟkuk kaṭattaṟku ariyatu eṉpatu poruḷ. kāṭṭaik keṭuttup payiriṭum nilam nāṭu eṉappaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟu tirāviṭariṉ nākarikam it tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē paṭippaṭi vaḷarcciyaṭaintuḷḷatu eṉa aṟintukoṇṭōm. iṉit tamiḻar nākarikattiṉ ciṟappu iyalpukaḷait taṉittaṉi eṭuttuk kūṟuvōm.
nākarika maṭainta tirāviṭar
tolaiviṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟutal
cūḻnilaikkēṟpa makkaḷ evvāṟu nākarika vaḷarcci peṟṟārkaḷ eṉpatai viḷakkiyuḷḷōm. iṉṟu teṉṉintiya makkaḷ ilaṅkai, malāyā, moricas, teṉṉāppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntu varukiṉṟaṉar. mikap paḻaṅkālattilēyē tirāviṭa makkaḷ āpirikkā mēṟkup pāracīkam, malāyā, kampōtiyā, cumattirā, yāvā, cīṉā mutaliya pala nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟi vāḻntārkaḷ. in nikaḻcciyai valiyuṟuttum cāṉṟukaḷ pala iṉṟu kiṭaittuḷḷaṉa.
“paṇṭai nāḷilē cīrum ciṟappum eytiyirunta miciram eṉṉum ekiptu nāṭṭiṉam, pāracīkak kaṭaṟkaraiyiliruntu aḻintupōṉa cumēru (Sumeria) nāṭṭilum, cōḻar kuṭiyēṟiṉamaiyiṉālē. cōḻatēyam eṉṉum peyariṉai eytip piṟkālattilē moḻic citaiviṉālē cālatēyā (Chaldea) eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa tolpatiyilum cōḻar kulattār kalattiṟ ceṉṟu veṟṟi peṟṟut tamatu āṇai celuttiya kirēttat (Crete) tīvilum ataṟkaṇittākiya yavaṉapurattilum (Greece) urōmar varuvataṟkumuṉ paḻaiya ittāli (Italy) tēcattilum aipīriyā eṉappaṭṭa paḻaiya speyiṉ (Spain) tēcattilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum tamiḻkkulattār vāḻntu nākarikam parappiṉārkaḷeṉa mēṟṟicai aṟiñar ārāycciyāṟ kaṇṭu veḷiyiṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉar......cintunati tīrattilē pāṇṭiya maṉṉar āḷukaiyilē mīṉāṭu eṉṉum peyarōṭu tikaḻntatum piṉṉāḷilē ‘iṟantōr mēṭu’ eṉṉum karuttuṭaiya mukañcatarai (Mohonjo Daro) eṉṉum peyareytiyatumākiya nāṭṭilē mituṉarāciyāṉatu yāḻ eṉṉum peyariṉāl vaḻaṅkappaṭṭu iṇaiyāḻuruvattilē kuṟiyīṭu ceyyap paṭṭateṉa aṟiñar kūṟuvar.
“maṇimalar eṉṉum kaṭṭurait tokutiyiṉuḷḷē paṇṭaittamiḻar perumai eṉṉum uraiyiṉai eḻutiya ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattu varalāṟṟuppakuti āciriyar vi.ār. irāmacantira tīṭcitaravarkaḷ, ‘teṉ ṉāṭṭiliruntu tamiḻ nākarikam urōmā purikkum, kirēkka nāṭṭiṟkum paraviyatu. iṉṉum takkaṇa pīṭapūmiyaic cērnta vēṭṭuvar1 (kirātar) eṉṟa kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ yavaṉa tēcattiṟkuc ceṉṟu taṅkaḷ aracai nilai niṟuttit taṅkaḷ peyaraiyum annāṭṭiṟkuk koṭuttārkaḷ. ituvē kirīṭ (Crete) tēcam. itaṉ nākarikattukkum tamiḻar nākarikattukkum oppumai mikutiyāyuḷḷatu.’ eṉa eḻutiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. villavar eṉṉum cērar peyarai ‘hunters’ eṉa mēṉāṭṭār moḻipeyarkka atu tirumpavum tamiḻiṟku moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭu vēṭṭuvar eṉa amaivatu iyalpu. ātaliṉālē takkiṇa pīṭapūmiyaic cērnta vēṭṭuvar eṉṟa kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ eṉat tīṭcitaravarkaḷ uraiyilē kuṟikkappaṭṭōr villavarākiya cērar kulattiṉarē eṉpatu teḷivākiṉṟatu. cilappatikārattilum patiṟṟup pattilum kāṇappaṭum cērar meykkīrtti ivvuṇmaiyiṉai nilai niṟuttukiṉṟatu. cēranāṭu kēraḷam eṉat tirivupaṭṭatupōlac cērar tīvu ‘krīt’ āyirukkalām. tamiḻnāṭṭuvēntar yavaṉarkaḷait tamatu kōṭṭai vāyilkaḷilē kāvalāḷarkaḷāka vaittirunta ceytiyum, urōmāpuri vēntaṉākiya ākasṭas maṉṉaṉukkup pāṇṭiyaṉ tūtaṉuppiya ceyti yum, piṟavum ātāramākat tīṭcitaravarkaḷ, ‘mattiyatarai nākarikameṉṟu ippōtu vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu teṉṉintiya nākarikam eṉṟu tuṇivāyc collalām’1 eṉṟu muṭivu kūṟukiṉṟār”2
ulakiṉ perum pakutiyil tirāviṭam vaḻaṅkiyataṟkuc cāṉṟu
mokañcatarō arappā mutaliya cintuveḷi aḻipāṭukaḷil kiṭait tuḷḷa muttiraikaḷil akkālattu vaḻaṅkiya eḻuttukkaḷ poṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avvakai eḻuttukkaḷ haitarāpāttu, tirunelvēli mutaliya viṭaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa maṭ pāṇṭaṅkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ilaṅkaiyilē kēkālaiyi luḷḷa malai oṉṟilum ivvakai eḻuttup poṟikkappaṭṭiruppatai heras pātiriyār kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. mokañcatarō eḻuttukaḷai mika otta eḻuttukaḷ teṉṉamerikkāvukku aṇmaiyiluḷḷa īsrar tīvukaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ekiptu, mēṟku āciyā, ciṉṉa āciyā, kirēttā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkiya paḻaiya eḻuttukaḷukkum mokañcatarō eḻuttu kaḷukkum oṉṟumai iruppataip paḻaiya eḻuttu ārāycciyāḷar kāṭṭi yuḷḷaṉar. ivveḻuttukkaḷai ellām naṉku ārāynta heras pātiri yār paḻaiya cīṉam, cumēriyam, paḻaiya ellam, paḻaiya arāpi, acōkaṉ, pirāmi, miṉōvaṉ, paḻaiya ekiptiyam, ilipiyaṉ, eṟṟūskaṉ eḻuttuk kaḷellām paḻaiya tirāviṭa eḻuttiliruntu tōṉṟiyavai eṉak kūṟi yuḷḷār. mēṟku āciya, miṉōva, mokañcatarō, īsṭar tīvu eḻuttukaḷ ellām orē toṭakkattaic cērntaṉaveṉṟum avai avaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttiya makkaḷiṉ pōkkiṉpaṭi vevvēṟu vakaiyil vaḷarcci yaṭaintaṉa eṉṟum carṭayāṉ mārcal, pērāciriyar lāṅkaṭaṉ ji.ār. haṉṭar pōṉṟa ārāycci vallār karutiṉārkaḷ. ikkoḷkai nāḷ vītam valiyaṭaikiṉṟatu. oṭṭuc coṟkaḷuṭaiya moḻikaḷ perum pālum tirāviṭa moḻiyiṉiṉṟum piṟantaṉaveṉak kūṟalām.1
mokañcatarō akaḻ ārāycci aṟiyappaṭutaṟkup pala āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉṉarēyē pērāciriyar cuntaram piḷḷai avarkaḷ,
“catumaṟai āriyam varumuṉ cakamuḻutu niṉatāyiṉ
mutumoḻinī yaṉātiyeṉa moḻikuvatum viyappāmē”
eṉak kūṟiyatu mika viyakkattakkatu. tamiḻp paṟṟu mikkuṭaiyavarāy viḷaṅkiya virutai civañāṉayōkikaḷ iṟṟaikku muppattumūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ kūṟiyuḷḷatu varumāṟu:
“tirumalar maṇameṉa vorumaiyiṉ malki
eṇṇil poḻiṟpayir paṇṇik kākkum
oppil perumai meypporu ḷaṉpu
pāṅki ṉilaipeṟ ṟōṅkun tamiḻakat
tāṟaṟi vuṭaimaip pēṟuṟu makkaḷ
muṉṉart tōṉṟi maṉṉik keḻumi
imiḻiya lolicār tamiḻmoḻi pēci
maṇṇiṟ palaviṭa naṇṇik kuṭiyirun
tavvav viṭattuk kovviya vaṇṇam
kūṟṟu naṭaiyuṭai vēṟṟumai yeyti
paṟpala viṉappeyar peṟṟup perukiṉar.
* * * * * * *
mutaliṭaik kaḻaka muṉṉīr koḷḷap
paṭutali ṉintiyap paṭiyiṉ vaḷamvavi
ayaṉāṭ ṭavarī rāyira māṇṭāp
paṭaiyeṭuttut tuṉ puṟuttiya paṭiyāl
nūlkaḷu mavaṟṟi ṉuṇṇiya vaḻakkum
aruki maṟaintaṉa vātaliṉ”
intiyanāṭu muḻumaiyilum vāḻnta makkaḷ tirāviṭar
ituvaraiyilum akaḻ ārāycciyiṉāl aṟiyappaṭṭa paḻaiya nākarikaṅkaḷuḷ intiya nākarikamē mikap paḻamai uṭaiyatu. cintuveḷi nākarikattiṉ kālam ki.mu. 3500 eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu. cintuveḷi nākarika kālattil intiyā muḻumaiyilum orē moḻiyai vaḻaṅkiya orē iṉamakkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. ammakkaḷ tirāviṭar eṉappaṭuvar. āriya makkaḷ ki.mu. iraṇṭāyirattil allatu ataṟkuppiṉ intiya nāṭṭai aṭaintārkaḷ. akkālattil vaṭanāṭu muḻumaiyilum tirāviṭa moḻi vaḻaṅkiyateṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu āriya makkaḷiṉ paḻaiya pāṭalkaḷākiya vētaṅkaḷil tirāviṭac coṟkaḷ pala iruppatum piṟavumeṉa ārāycci yāḷar naṉku āyntu niṟuviyuḷḷāhkaḷ.1 āriyar varukaikkup piṉpē intiya nāṭṭil vevvēṟu iṉa makkaṭ kalappum moḻikkalappum uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ki.mu. iraṇṭāyirattil vaṭakkē imayattukkum teṟkē kumarikkum iṭaippaṭṭa peru nilapparappu tirāviṭa nāṭāka viḷaṅkiṟ ṟeṉa, teḷitil aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
putiya makkaḷiṉ varukaiyāl uṇṭāṉa māṟutalkaḷ
putiyamakkaḷukkum vaṭakkē vāḻntukoṇṭirunta tirāviṭa makkaḷukkumiṭaiyil pōrkaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. āriyaruṭaiya vētapāṭal kaḷāl akkālattil tirāviṭar uyarnta nākarikam peṟṟuviḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu.2 nākarikattil uyarnilai aṭaintirunta tirāviṭarai velvatu āriyamakkaḷukku iyalātatākaviruntatu. nāḷaṭaivil irucātiyārukkiṭaiyil tirumaṇakkalappukaḷāl toṭarpukaḷ uṇ ṭāyiṉa.3 tirāviṭap peṇkaḷai āriya āṭavarum āriyap peṇkaḷait tirāviṭa āṭavarum maṇantaṉar. tirāviṭaruṭaiya camūkam tāyāṭci muṟai yiṉatu. āriyaruṭaiya camūkam tantaiāṭci muṟaiyiṉatu. tirāviṭap peṇkaḷ mūlam āriya āṭavarukkup piṟantavarkaḷ tantai āṭci muṟaippaṭi āriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āriyappeṇkaḷ mūlam tirāviṭat tantaiyārukkup piṟantavarkaḷ tirāviṭar tāyāṭci muṟaippaṭi āriyar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivvakaiyāṉa kalappup pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka nikaḻntatu. vintiyamalaikku vaṭakkē uḷḷa makkaḷ tammai āriyar eṉak kūṟik koḷvārāyiṉar. āṉāl avarkaḷiṭattil āriyak kulattiṉarukkuriya kuṇaṅkuṟikaḷ mikacciṟitē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.1
makkaḷiṭai kalappu uṇṭāṉapōtu moḻikaḷum koḷkaikaḷum kalappavāyiṉa. tirāviṭa moḻic coṟkaḷ pala tirittu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa mikappala coṟkaḷ iṉṟu vaṭamoḻiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. accoṟkaḷukku mūlam aṟiyamuṭiyāmal irukkiṟatu. intō āriyamoḻikku iṉamuṭaiyatāka airōppiya nāṭukaḷil vaḻaṅkum āriyamoḻikaḷil iccoṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭavillai.2
putitāka vanta makkaḷaiviṭa vaṭakkē vāḻntukoṇṭirunta tirāviṭa makkaḷ palar. makkaṭ kalappiṉāl cila māṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāki moḻi māṟupaṭṭapōtum vāḻkkai muṟaiyil avarkaḷ tirāviṭa camūkattiṉar pōlavē kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. puttar kālamakkaḷ vāḻkkaiyaippaṟṟi rais ṭēviṭs eṉpār kūṟiyiruppatu itaṉai valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu.3
vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyamoḻi camakkirutamā?
vaṭanāṭṭilē makkaḷ vaḻaṅkiya moḻi camakkirutameṉṟu palar niṉaikkiṟārkaḷ. ikkaruttut tavaṟuṭaiyatu. camakkirutam orupōtum pēccu moḻiyākavirukkavillai. atu kurumārukkuriya cātimoḻi yākavē iruntatu. intiyanāṭṭu moḻiyiṉ tiripukaḷākiya pirākirutam eṉappaṭṭa moḻikaḷē pēcappaṭṭuvantaṉa.4
āriya moḻiyaic cērntaṉaveṉṟu karutappaṭum vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa amaippu tirāviṭa moḻi ilakkaṇattai ottuḷḷateṉṟum, oru moḻiyiṉ kuṭumpattoṭarpai aṟivatu ilak kaṇa amaippiṉālallatu coṟkaḷaik koṇṭu aṟital kūṭāteṉṟum, vaṭaintiya moḻikaḷ tirāviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ tiripu eṉṟum pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār tuṇintuḷḷār.1
pāracīkar vaṭanāṭṭiṉ cilapakutikaḷai veṉṟu cila kālam āṭci naṭattiṉārkaḷ. piṉpu kirēkkar paṭaieṭuppu nērntatu. piṉpu cittiyar, maṅkōliyar, avuṇar (Huns) mutaliya pala cātiyiṉar vaṭanāṭṭiṉmītu paṭai eṭuttuvantu nāṭṭil ciṟcila pakutikaḷiṟ kuṭiyēṟi intiya makka ḷōṭu kalantārkaḷ. itaṉāl alakcāntar paṭai eṭuppuk kālam mutal mēlum makkaṭ kalappum moḻik kalappum intiya nāṭṭil uṇṭāyiṉa.
intiya makkaḷiṉ kula muṟaiyāṉa ārāycci naṭattiya raisli eṉpār iṉṟu intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum kulappiriviṉarai kīḻvarumāṟu pirittuḷḷār; paṭattiṟ kāṇka.
tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar eṉṉum koḷkai
tirāviṭar intiya nāṭṭukku ayaliliruntu vantārkaḷ. avar varukaikkumuṉ nikurōvak kuṇaṅkuṟikaḷuḷḷa orucātiyiṉar aṅku vāḻntukoṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉṟum, avarkaḷ tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar (Pre-Dravidians) eṉappaṭu var eṉṟum, am makkaḷē iṉṟu malaikaḷil vāḻvōreṉṟum oru cilar eḻutivarukiṉṟaṉar. ik koḷ kaikku ātāram oru ciṟitum kiṭaik kavillai. tirāviṭa makkaḷ, vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷukkēṟpa, aintu vēṟu vakait tiruttaṅkaḷuṭaiyavarkaḷāka vaḷarcciyaṭaintārkaḷ eṉa muṉ kūṟiyuḷḷōm. avarkaḷil kuṟiñci pālai nilamakkaḷē maṟṟa makkaḷiṉ toṭarpiṉṟip paḻaiya muṟaiyil vāḻntu putiya cila paḻakkavaḻakkaṅ kaḷuṭaiyarāy varukiṉṟaṉar. avar kaḷiṉ uṭal, niṟam, vaḷarcci mutaliyaṉa avarkaḷiṉ uṇavu, vāḻkkai nilai pōṉṟavai ētuvāka uṇṭāṉavai. tiru. vi.ār. irāmaccantira tīṭcitar avarkaḷ kūṟuvatu varumāṟu. “tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar (Pre - Dravidans), paḻaiya tirāviṭar (Proto-Dravidans) eṉṉum koḷkaikaḷ irupatām nūṟṟāṇṭuk kaṟpaṉai. paḻamporuḷ ārāycciyāḷarāvatu varalāṟṟukkārarāvatu teṉṉintiyāvil oṉṟukkuppiṉ oṉṟākat toṭarntu vanta paṇpāṭṭuk kālaṅkaḷil putiya makkaḷ pukuntu māṟutal kaḷaik koṇṭuvantārkaḷ eṉṟu colvataṟkēṟṟa ātāram oṉṟum kāṭṭamuṭiyātu. paḻaiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭṭukkuppiṉ putiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭum ataṟkuppiṉ irumpuk kālap paṇpāṭum muṟaiyē oṉṟai oṉṟu toṭarntu vantaṉa eṉṟu koḷvataṟku ēṟṟa cāṉṟukaḷē kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻam poruḷārāycci ik koḷkaikku vali aḷikkiṉṟatu. kāṭukaḷilum, malaikaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷ kulamuṟaiyākat teṉṉintiya makkaḷōṭu poruntātavarkaḷ eṉṟu koḷḷutal cālātu. putiya kaṟkālam mutal teṉṉintiyāvil aintu vakaiyāṉa paṇpāṭukaḷ iruntu varukiṉṟaṉa veṉpatait teṉṉintiya makkaḷaippaṟṟi ārāycci ceyvōr naṉku aṟivar. vēṭṭaiyāṭiyum mīṉ piṭittum vāḻvōr paḻaiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭṭaic cērntavar. avarkaḷ toṭarpākak kāṭu kaḷilum kaṭaṟkaraikaḷilum vāḻntamaiyāl avarkaḷiṉ maṉappāṅkum vāḻkkaip paḻakkaṅkaḷum taṉimuṟaiyil vaḷarcciyaṭaintaṉa. niṟattaip paṟṟi nām ārāyavēṇṭiyatillai. itu cūḻaliṉ veppanilai, kai yāḷum toḻil campantaṅkaḷāl uṇṭāvatu. payiriṭum toḻil paraviṉa tāl paḻaiya poruḷātāra vaḻikaḷ muṟṟāka māṟutalaṭaintaṉa eṉṟu koḷḷutal muṭiyātu. cila cūḻalkaḷil viṭappaṭṭōr paḻaiya toḻil kaḷaiyum paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷayum paṟṟit tam vāḻkkaiyai naṭattiṉar. vēḷāḷar, kārāḷar, āyarvēṟu vakaiyāṉa paṇpāṭukaḷai eytiṉar. pālai nila makkaḷ kuṟiñci makkaḷōṭu kalantu oṉṟupaṭṭaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭil pālai eṉṟu taṉi nilam illai. ākavē kaṭaṟkaraikaḷilum malaikaḷilum vāḻum makkaḷait tirāviṭarukku muṟpaṭṭa tirāviṭar eṉṟu kūṟutal cālātu”1
pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār āriyarukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻar paṇpāṭu eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
2“tamiḻ moḻikkuriya makkaḷaippōlavē ammoḻiyum teṉṉintiyāvilēyē tōṉṟi yātum kuḻappamiṉṟi uyarnta ilakkiya vaḷarcci nilaiyai aṭaintatu. kaṟkālam mutal tamiḻkkulam orē toṭakkattaic cērntatāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. tamiḻ moḻiyaip payiṉṟa ayal nāṭṭu ārāycciyāḷar cilar tamiḻ moḻiyai vaḻaṅkiya paḻaiya makkaḷ mattiya āciyāviṉiṉṟu, vantārkaḷ eṉṟum, ataṟkuk kāraṇam paluccistāṉattil vaḻaṅkum pirākūy moḻiyil tamiḻukku iṉamuṭaiya pala coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum tammaṉampōl kūṟiṉar. pirākūy moḻiyil tamiḻukku iṉamuṭaiya coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭu tal, tamiḻ intiyamoḻiyaṉṟu eṉak kūṟuvataṟkup pōtumāṉa ātāra mākātu. iraṇṭu talaimuṟaikku muṟpaṭṭa ārāycciyāḷar ulakil vāḻnta paḻaiya makkaṭ kulattiṉarai ellām vēṟu eṅkō iruntu vanta varkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvatu iyalpāka iruntatu. avarkaḷukkut teṉṉintiyā vil kaṟkālap paṇpāṭu evvaḷavil paraviyiruntateṉṟum, tamiḻiṉ paḻaiya aṭippaṭaiyil tamiḻr teṉṉintiyāvil aṟiyamuṭiyāta kālam mutal vāḻntu varukiṉṟārkaḷ eṉpataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟukaḷuḷḷaṉa veṉ pataiyum avarkaḷ aṟiyātiruntārkaḷ. putiya kaṟkālap paṇpāṭṭukku mattiya iṭam teṉṉintiyāvē. kaṭappā paḻaiya mattiya kālap paḻaṅkaṟ kālap paṇpāṭṭukku mattiya iṭamākum. ekiptilē nīla āṟṟaṅ karaiyi liruntu maṟaintupōṉa paḻaiya nākarikattiṉ ciṉṉaṅkaḷ cila iṉṟum takkāṇattil uḷḷaṉa. eliyaṭ cimit eṉpār kiḻakku āppirikka makka ḷiṭaiyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉavum, iṉṟum takkāṇattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉavu mākiya tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ nampikkaikaḷ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kāṭṭi iru makkaḷukkum toṭakkam oṉṟu eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ tirāviṭar nākarikam
tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ mikap paḻaiya nākarikattaippaṟṟi mokañca tarō, arappā, caṅkutarō mutaliya aḻipāṭṭu mēṭukaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭa paḻamporuḷkaḷ veḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉa. arappā mokañcatarō mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷil tirāviṭamakkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. akkālattil avarkaḷ ekiptiyar, pāpilōṉiyarkaḷai otta allatu avarkaḷilum uyarnta nākarikamuṭaiyavarkaḷāka vāḻntārkaḷ. akkālattil irumpu payaṉpaṭuttap paṭavillai. nakaraṅkaḷ amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. vīṭukaḷ ceṅkaṟkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. avaikaḷukku māṭikaḷum māṭikaḷukkuc cellap paṭikkaṭṭukaḷumiruntaṉa. māṭikaḷiliruntu kaḻivu nīr kīḻē celva taṟkuc cūḷaiyiṭṭa maṇkuḻāykaḷ porutti vaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kūraivaḻiyāka vaḻiyum nīr pīlikaḷil viḻuntu piṉ kuḻāykaḷ vaḻiyē kīḻē ceṉṟatu. ovvoru vīṭṭukkum kiṇaṟum kuḷikkumaṟaiyum iruntaṉa. kaḻivunīr, kālvāykaḷ vaḻiyāka vītiyiluḷḷa periya kālvāyil viḻuntatu. kālvāykaḷ ceṅkaṟ patikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. taṇṇīr ceṉṟu viḻum iṭattil ceṅkaṟpatitta kuḻi iruntatu. pāramāṉa poruḷkaḷ kuḻiyil taṅkiniṉṟaṉa. cuttañ ceyvōr ak kuḻikaḷil taṅkiniṟkum aḻukkukaḷaic cuttañ ceytaṉar. vīṭukaḷukku malakkūṭaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa.
periya vītikaḷ 33-aṭi akalamuṭaiyaṉa. ciṟiya vītikaḷ 18-aṭi akalamuṭaiyaṉa. makkaḷ vāṟkōtumaiyaiyum, kōtumaiyaiyum viḷaivittārkaḷ. āṭu, māṭu, erumai mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ vīṭukaḷil vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. yāṉaikaḷ paḻakki vēlai ceyvikkappaṭṭaṉa. makkaḷ pañcu viḷaivikkavum pañciliruntu nūl nūṟkavum nūliliruntu āṭai ceyyavum aṟintiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ ekiptu, pāpilōṉ, pāracīkam mutaliya nāṭukaḷōṭum teṉṉintiyāvōṭum vāṇikam purintārkaḷ. oruvakaic cuṇṇāmpuk kalliṟ cetukkappaṭṭa muttiraikaḷum, nāṭṭiyamāṭum pāvaṉaiyuḷḷa pāvaikaḷ, cuvāttika aṭaiyāḷamiṭṭa poruḷkaḷum, erumai kāṇṭāmirukam yāṉai māṭu pōṉṟa vaṭivaṅ kaḷum eḻuttukkaḷum veṭṭappaṭṭa muttiraikaḷum, kal, poṉ, cempu, veṇkalam, veḷḷi mutaliyavaikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷum ivaipōṉṟa aḻakiya poruḷkaḷum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ak kāla makkaḷ iv virupatām nūṟṟāṇṭu makkaḷaiviṭap palavakaiyil uyarnta nākarika naṉṉilai aṭaintiruntārkaḷ eṉa ārāycciyāḷar naviṉṟuḷḷārkaḷ.1 cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷiṉ kālam ki.mu. 3500 varaiyil.
piṟkālattil intiya nāṭṭai vantaṭainta āriya makkaḷum avar kaḷiṉ cantatiyiṉarum appaḻaiya nākarika muṟaikaḷ palavaṟṟaik kaik koṇṭaṉareṉṟum ataṟkuc cāṉṟu avarkaḷiṉ vētapāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟateṉṟum ārāycci aṟiñar kūṟuvar. tirāviṭa makkaḷaip paṟṟiya cila kuṟippukaḷ vētaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pāratam, irāmā yaṇam eṉṉum nūlkaḷil teṉṉāṭṭu aracarkaḷaippaṟṟiyum teṉ ṉāṭṭaippaṟṟiyum cila kuṟippukaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. annūlkaḷ kālat tukkuk kālam eḻutic cērkkappaṭṭaṉa vātaliṉ avaikaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭu vaṉa ciṟanta varalāṟṟuc cāṉṟukaḷākamāṭṭā.
“māpāratattaiyum irāmāyaṇattaiyum nām ki.mu. 5ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭaiya nūlkaḷ eṉakkoḷḷalām. annūlkaḷiṉ ep pakuti ek kālattil ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉṉum aiyappāṭum uṇṭākiṉṟatu. avai kaḷil kūṟappaṭum intiya nāṭṭiṉ pirivukaḷ ki.mu. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭu varaiyil uṟutiyāka amaintiruntaṉaveṉṟu koḷḷalām. cātakak kataikaḷ patiṉāṟu irācciyaṅkaḷaiyum vēṟu cila nāṭukaḷaiyum paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷai ellām intiya paṭamoṉṟil oḻuṅkupaṭa vaittup pārttāl mikat teṟkē uḷḷa nāṭu kōtāvarikkut teṟkē pōkavillai. paḻaiya pautta ilakkiyaṅkaḷ vintiyattukkuk kīḻē uḷḷa nāṭukaḷaip paṟṟi aṟiyā.
pāṇiṉikkut teṉṉāṭṭaip paṟṟit teriyātu avar teṟkē uḷḷaṉavākak kuṟippiṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ kaccā, avanti, kōcalam, karūcā, kaliṅkam eṉpaṉa. ivai vintattukku vaṭakkē uḷḷaṉa. pāṇiṉikku iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉ viḷaṅkiya kārttiyāyaṉar teṉṉāṭṭaip paṟṟi aṟintiruntār. patañcali kālattil teṉṉāṭu naṉṟāka aṟiyap paṭṭiruntatu. patañcaliyiṉ kālam ki.mu. 150. kārttiyāyaṉariṉ kālam, ki.mu. 350. paṇṭākkaratu koḷkaiyiṉpaṭi pāṇiṉi ki.mu. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiṉār.1 ak kālattil vaṭanāṭṭavarāl teṉṉāṭu ciṟitum aṟiyap paṭātiruntatu. mekastīṉas kālattil teṉṉintiyāvaippaṟṟi vaṭa nāṭṭavar ciṟitu aṟintiruntārkaḷ. cāṇakkiyariṉ artta cāttirattil teṉṉāṭṭaippaṟṟik kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. acōkariṉ kalveṭṭukkaḷil cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar nāṭukaḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.2
tamiḻnāṭṭellai
ki.mu. 1000 varaiyil vintiyamalaikku vaṭakē uḷḷa nāṭukaḷ āriyāvarttam eṉṉum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. vintiyattakkut teṟkē tamiḻ vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ki.mu. 1000 varaiyil epirēya moḻiyil ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkiya peyarkaḷ tamiḻākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyam ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ vaṭa vellai tiruppati malai yākaviruntatu. akkālattil teluṅku taṉimoḻiyākap pirintuviṭṭatu. orukālattil tamiḻnāṭṭellai kiruṭṭiṇā āṟuvaraiyil irunta teṉac ciṟpanūliṟ kāṇappaṭuvatāka āciriyar u.vē. cāmināta aiya ravarkaḷ caṅkakālattamiḻum piṟkālat tamiḻum eṉṉum nūliṟ kuṟip piṭṭuḷḷār. oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṟpaṭṭa ilakkiyaṅkaḷ teluṅku moḻikku illai. 1caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷil vēṅkaṭattukku vaṭakkē vaṭukar nāṭu iruntateṉak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
paḻam poruḷ ārāycci varalāṟṟu ārāyccikku evvakaiyil tuṇaipurikiṉṟatō avvāṟē paḻaiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷum ataṟkut tuṇaipurikiṉṟaṉa. caṅkanūlkaḷaik koṇṭu iṟṟaikku iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ tamiḻakattiṉ paṇpāṭu evvakaiyiṉateṉa nām teḷḷitil aṟiyakkūṭum.
caṅkakālattil tamiḻnāṭu
2kiruṭṭiṇā āṟṟukkut teṟkē kaṉṉiyākumari varaiyilumuḷḷa nilapparappu mūṉṟu peruvēntarālum ēḻu kuṟunila maṉṉarālum āḷap paṭṭatu. ivarkaḷai aṉṟip pala ciṟṟaracarkaḷum iruntārkaḷ. kaṭaṟ karaiyai aṭuttavum veḷiyākavuḷḷavum pakutikaḷē periya aracarāl āḷappaṭṭaṉa. malaikaḷum kāṭukaḷum kuṟunila maṉṉarukku uriyaṉavā yiruntaṉa. kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkarai ōramākak kiruṭṭiṇā āṟutoṭakkam irāmanātapurappakutiyiluḷḷa toṇṭi varaiyiluḷḷa nāṭu cōḻar āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭiruntatu. itaṉ mattiyil kāñci āḷukaikkuṭpaṭṭa tirukkōyilūraic cūḻnta malaināṭu malaiyamāṉukku uriyatāyiruntatu. cōḻa irācciyattukkut teṟkē pāṇṭiya nāṭu iruntatu. itu kaṭaṟkaraiyi liruntu kaṭaṟkarai varaiyum parantu ikkāla maturai tirunelvēli tiruvi tāṅkūr mutaliya mākāṇaṅkaḷum kōyamuttūr kocci mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷum aṭaṅkiyatāyiruntatu. mēṟkut toṭarcci malaiyil āyiṉ malai nāṭu iruntatu. tālami āyai ayoy (Aioi) eṉak kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷāṉ. tirunelvēliyiluḷḷa koṟkait tuṟai mukattai aṭuttu evvināṭu iruntatu. paḻaṉi malaiyaic cuṟṟip pēkaṉ nāṭu iruntatu. itaṟku vaṭakkē kaṭaṟkaraiyai aṭuttuc cēranāṭu iruntatu. itupālak kāṭṭuveḷikku ūṭāka cēlam kōyamuttūr varaiyum nīṇ ṭiruntatu. teṉ maicūril toṭaṅki oṉṟiṉ pakkattē oṉṟāka iruṅkōvēḷiṉ arayam nāṭum, pāriyiṉ paṟampu nāṭum, atiyamāṉiṉ takaṭūrum (tarumapuri), ōriyiṉ kollimalai yum iruntaṉa. mutal mūṉṟu nāṭu kaḷum maicūr ellaikkuḷ irun taṉa. ivvellaikku appāl kiḻakkē kaṅkar nāṭum teṟkē koṅku nāṭum iruntaṉa. tamiḻnāṭ ṭiṉ mēṟkēyuḷḷa vaṭa ellaip puṟam tuḷuva nāṭṭaracaṉākiya naṉṉaṉukkum, kiḻakkēyuḷḷa vaṭa ellaippuṟam vēṅkaṭattup pullikkum, uriyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. ataṟku vaṭakkē āriya nāṭum (vaṭukarnāṭu), taṇṭāraṇiyamum iruntaṉa.
teṉṉāṭṭil paṭaiyeṭuppukaḷ
mauriya cantirakuptaṉ kālamvaraiyil teṉṉāṭṭil ayal nāṭṭup paṭai eṭuppukaḷ nēravillai. cantirakuptaṉuṭaiya maraṇattukkup piṉ avaṉ makaṉ pintucāraṉ tamiḻnāṭṭiṉmītu paṭai eṭuttāṉ. itaṉmēl iṭaiyiṭaiyē vaṭanāṭṭavar paṭai eṭuppukaḷ nērntaṉa. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya aracar āriyaraiveṉṟu villum puliyum mīṉumākiya tattam koṭikaḷai mēruvil nāṭṭiyum ak kuṟikaḷai ammalaiyil tīṭṭiyum uḷḷārkaḷ eṉak tamiḻp paḻam pāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvaṉa āriyar tamiḻar pōrkaḷaiyē kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. cēra aracaruḷ ceṅkuṭṭu vaṉum, pāṇṭiya aracaruḷ neṭuñceḻiyaṉum, cōḻa aracaruḷ karikālaṉum āriyarai veṉṟatāka varalāṟu uṇṭu.
kaḷappirar
ki.pi. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaḷappirar eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar kiḷampit toṇṭai nāṭu oḻinta maṟṟai mūvēntar nāṭukaḷaiyum aṭippaṭutti ēṟakkuṟaiya irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷ āṭci purintaṉar. kaḷappirar eṅkiruntu vantārkaḷ eṉpatum, piṟavum aṟiyamuṭiyāmal irukkiṉṟaṉa. kaṭuṅkōṉ kaḷappirarai veṉṟu pāṇṭiya irācciyattai avariṭamiruntu mīṭṭāṉ.
pallavar
ki.pi. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭumutal pallavar kāñciyait talainaka rākakkoṇṭu toṇṭaināṭṭai āṇṭu vantaṉar. toṇṭaimāṉ iḷantiraiyaṉ maṇipallavattu nākakulappeṇṇukkum cōḻaṉukkum piṟantavaṉeṉṟu caṅkanūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. maṇipallava araca kuṭumpap peṇvaḻit tōṉṟiya iḷantiraiyaṉiṉ cantatiyiṉar pallavar eṉappaṭṭārkaḷākalām. pallavam koṭiyaik kuṟikkum. pallavar āṭci oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭil maṟaintupōyiṟṟu. pallavam eṉpataṉ maṟu peyarē toṇṭai eṉpatu es.kē. aiyaṅkār karuttu.
cāḷukkiyar
āntira nāṭṭiṉ mēṟkukkaraiyil vintayamalaikkut teṟkilum cēranāṭṭu ellaikku vaṭakkilum cāḷukkiyar eṉṉum oru kūṭṭattiṉar atikāramuṭaiyavarāy ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu toṭakkam āṭci purintaṉar. cāḷukkiyarukkum pallavarukkum pōrkaḷ naṭantaṉa. pulukēci eṉṉum cāḷukkiya vēntaṉ makēntiravaṉmaṉ eṉṉum pallava aracaṉōṭu porutu kiḻakkē kiruṭṭiṇāvukkum kōtāvarikkum iṭaiyiluḷḷa veṅki nāṭṭaik kaippaṟṟi ataṉait taṉatu tampiyai āḷumpaṭi niyamittāṉ. atu kālattil kiḻakku cāḷukkiya nāṭu eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
ūr amaippu
kōyilaiccuṟṟi allatu aracaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaiyaiccuṟṟiyē nakaraṅkaḷ eḻuntaṉa. ovvoru kirāmattukkum kōyil mattiya iṭamāka viruntatu.1 makkaḷ kōyilkaḷaiccuṟṟik kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. ovvoru toḻil ceyvōrum taṉittaṉi vītikaḷil vāḻntārkaḷ. kirāmattiṉ naṭuvil veḷiyiruntatu. atu maṉṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. aṅku niḻalmaram niṉṟatu. ataic cuṟṟit tiṇṇaiyiṭappaṭṭiruntatu. ōyvu nēraṅkaḷil makkaḷ aṅkē kūṭip poḻutupōkkiṉārkaḷ. aṅku ūrkkūṭṭaṅkaḷuṅ kūṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. niyāyattīrppum aṅku ceyyappaṭṭatu. paḷḷikkūṭaṅ kaḷum aṅku naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. ellāvakaiyāṉa nikaḻccikaḷukkum kiḷarcci kaḷukkum maṉṟam mattiya iṭamāyiruntatu. aṅku aruḷtaṟi niṟutti vaḻipaṭappaṭṭatu. kirāmattil naṭakkavēṇṭiya ovvoru mukkiya nikaḻcciyaip paṟṟiyum aṅku kūṭṭaṅkaḷ kūṭṭi ālōcikkappaṭṭaṉa. kirāmaṅkaḷ kirāmacapaikaḷāl āḷappaṭṭaṉa. perumpālum avvak kirāmaṅkaḷiluḷḷa kaṭavuḷiṉ peyarāl kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ aṟiyap paṭṭaṉa. ovvoru kōyilum ōr araṇmaṉaiyiṉ amaippaip pōlak kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. aracaṉuṭaiya māḷikaiyum kōyil eṉappaṭṭatu.
nakaram
aracaṉuṭaiya māḷikai allatu araṇmaṉaiyaic cuṟṟi matil iṭappaṭṭirukkum. ataṟku aṭuttu araca kuṭumpattiṉarum aracaṉatu karumattalaivarkaḷ vāḻum vītikaḷum irukkum. ivvītikaḷaiccuṟṟi matiliṭappaṭṭirukkum. matilukku veḷiyēyuḷḷa vītikaḷil paṟpala toḻil puriyum makkaḷ vāḻvārkaḷ. aṅku kaṭaikaḷum kaṭaitteruk kaḷum uṇṭu. pakaivar ilakuvil aḻikkavum ēṟavum muṭiyāta matil nakaraic cūḻntu iruntatu. matiliṉ vāyilkaḷukku periya maraṅkaḷāl irumpu cērttuc ceyyappaṭṭa katavukaḷ iṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avai paḻaiya kōyilkaḷiliṭappaṭṭuḷḷa katavukaḷ pōṉṟavai. uḷḷē tiraṇṭa maraṅkaḷāl avai tāḻiṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kōṭṭai vāyilil koṭikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. matiṟcuvarkaḷ vīrarniṉṟu pōrceytaṟkēṟṟa akalamuṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. koti eṇṇeyai iṟaippaṉavum, neruppai vīcuvaṉavum, kal eṟivaṉavum, ampu umiḻvaṉavum pōṉṟa poṟikaḷ pala matiṟcuvarkaḷil amaikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. veḷimatilaic cuṟṟip periya akaḻ iruntatu. itu āḻamuṭaiyatākavum nīr niṟaivuṭaiyatākavum iruntatu. nīril periya mutalaikaḷum mīṉkaḷum iruntaṉa. kōṭṭaivāyililiruntu akaḻiyaik kaṭakkak kūṭiyatākap palakai iṭappaṭṭiruntatu. pōrkkālaṅkaḷil palakai eṭukkappaṭṭatu. akaḻil veḷiyē muḷmaraṅkaḷ naṭappaṭṭuḷḷa kāvaṟkāṭu iruntatu. aṅku vēṭṭuvar vāḻntaṉar. ataṟku veḷiyē viḷai nilaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa.
ki.pi. 1225il intiyāvukku vantu tirumpiya1 cīṉap pirayāṇi oruvaṉ eḻutiyiruppatu koṇṭu akkālak kōṭṭai evvā ṟiruntateṉṟu. nām aṟintu koḷḷalām.
“iṅku (cōḻanāṭṭil) ēḻucuṟṟumatilkaḷuḷḷa nakaram uṇṭu. vaṭakkiliruntu teṟkunōkkiyuḷḷa veḷimatiliṉ nīḷam paṉṉiraṇṭu li (LI) kiḻakkiliruntu mēṟkē cellum matiliṉ nīḷam ēḻu ‘li’ ovvoru cuṟṟu matilukkumiṭaiyil nūṟaṭi veḷiyuṇṭu. ēḻu matilkaḷil nāṉku ceṅkallāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭavai; iraṇṭu kaḷimaṇṇāl eṭukkappaṭṭavai; mattiyiluḷḷatu marattiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. aṅku paḻamaraṅkaḷum pū maraṅkaḷum naṭappaṭṭa cōlai uṇṭu. mutal iraṇṭu cuvarkaḷukku iṭaiyiluḷḷa veḷiyil makkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷ uṇṭu. avai akaḻiyāṟ cūḻappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mūṉṟām nāṉkām cuvarkaḷuk kiṭaiyiluḷḷa veḷiyil aracāṅka karumakārarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷuṇṭu. aintāvatu matilukkuḷ aracaṉuṭaiya kumārar vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. āṟāvatu cuvarukkuḷ kōyilkaḷum pūcārikaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷum uṇṭu. ēḻāvatu cuvarukkuḷ nāṉūṟuvīṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya araṇmaṉai uṇṭu.”
mauriyacantirakuptaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaiyaippaṟṟic collap paṭum viparamum piṟavum caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiluma utayaṇaṉ kataiyilum kūṟappaṭuvaṉavaṟṟai ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟai iṅku tarukiṉṟōm.
