“aracaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉai periya pūñcōlaiyiṉ naṭuvil uḷḷatu. aṅku paḻakiya mayiṟ cēvalkaḷum aḻakiya kōḻi iṉaṅkaḷum ulāvit tirintaṉa. niḻal maraṅkaḷiṉ kompukaḷ oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu piṉṉik kiṭantaṉa. aṅku nāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa maraṅkaḷ eppoḻutum paccaiyāka irukkum. avai orupōtum ilai utirttuvatillai. aṅku paṟavaikaḷ pala vāḻkiṉṟaṉa. avai kiḷaikaḷil kūṭukaṭṭiyuḷḷaṉa. aṅku pala kuḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaikaḷil mikap periya paḻakiya mīṉkaḷ viṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. aracaṉuṭaiya kumārarallāta piṟar ak kuḷaṅkaḷil mīṉpiṭikka aṉumatikkappaṭa māṭṭārkaḷ.
aracaṉuṭaiya araṇmaṉaiyai alaṅkarikkum mulām pūcappaṭṭa tūṇkaḷil poṉkoṭikaḷ cuṟṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷmītu veḷḷi yāl ceyyappaṭṭa paṟavaikaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. araṇmaṉaiyaiccuṟṟi matilum akaḻum uḷḷaṉa. araṇmaṉaiyiṉ piṉpuṟattil peṇkaḷ uṟaiyum aṟaikaḷ uṇṭu. aṅku kūṉum kuṟaḷum iruppar. peṇkaḷ uṟaiyum aṟaikaḷukku veḷiyē irācakumārarum irācakumārikaḷum uṟaiyum aṟaikaḷ iruntaṉa. avaikaḷukku veḷiyē kolumaṇṭapa mum karumakārariṉ aṟaikaḷum iruntaṉa. aracaṉukkut taṉi aṟaikaḷ iruntaṉa.
araṇmaṉaiyil pala irakaciya aṟaikaḷum, curaṅka vaḻikaḷum, uḷḷē veḷiyuḷḷa tūṇkaḷum, maṟaivāṉa paṭikkaṭṭukaḷum, kīḻē viḻuntu viṭakkūṭiya taraiyum iruntaṉa.-Chandra Gupta Maurya and his time-p 194.-R K. Mookerji.
aracaṉum āṭciyum
aracaṉuṭaiya atikāram ellā atikāraṅkaḷukkum mēlāka viruntatu. tantaikkuppiṉ makaṉ eṉṟavāṟu aracurimai talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāka vantatu. aracaṉuṭaiya kaṭamai tuṭṭarait taṇṭippatum makkaḷiṉ amaitiyaik kāppatumākum. araṉuṭaiya viruppamē nāṭṭiṉ caṭṭam; ataṉai maṟuttal evarukkum muṟaiyākātu. pōril aracaṉē paṭaiyai naṭattic ceṉṟāṉ. camātāṉa kālattil ellāt tīrppukaḷukkum avaṉē talaivaṉāka viruntāṉ.
araṉ irukkum maṇṭapam mikavum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭiruntatu. avaṉaic cūḻntu pala āṭamparaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. makkaḷ ataṉaiyē virumpiṉārkaḷ.1 aracaṉ irukkumiṭam kolu, aracirukkai allatu olakkam eṉappaṭṭatu. aracirukkaivēḷai allāta maṟṟak kālaṅkaḷil aracaṉ makkaḷōṭu aḷavaḷāviṉāṉ. nakarattil naṭakkum viḻākkaḷilum avaṉ kalantukoḷvāṉ. avaṉuṭaiya veṟṟikkākavum nīṇṭa vāḻviṟkākavum kōyilkaḷil tutikaḷ collappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ taṅkum māḷikaikaḷ avaṉ nāṭṭiṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷil iruntaṉa. nāṭu pōriṉṟi amaitiyuṟṟirukkum kālaṅkaḷil aracaṉ perumakkaḷōṭu nāṭu muḻuvataiyum cuṟṟivantāṉ. āṭci avaṉ atikārattil iruntapōtum oḻuṅkum nītiyum nilaviṉa. aracaṉaic cūḻntu pirapukkaḷ, perumakkaḷ, karuma viṉaiñar mutaliyōr iruntaṉar. mukkiyamāṉa karumaṅkaḷaic ceyvataṉ muṉ avaṉ uṭaṉ kūṭṭattu atikārikaḷ eṉṉum kūṭṭat tārōṭu ālōcaṉai ceytāṉ. aṉupavamum kalviyum uḷḷavarkaḷē ālōcaṉaiyāḷarāka avaṉāl teriyappaṭṭaṉar. kūṭṭaṅkaḷ aracirukika maṇṭapattil naṭantaṉa. araca kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ nīṇṭa ālōcaṉaikkup piṉṉarē veḷiyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ veḷiyāṉatum peruntaram allatu piramarāyaṉ eṉappaṭṭa amaiccaṉ avaikaḷait tirumantira ōlaikkāraṉ eṉṉum mūlaolai kāppāṟṟum atikāriyāl pottakattil eḻutumpaṭi ceytāṉ. piṉpu avai paṭi eṭukkappaṭṭu viṭaiyil atikārikaḷāl irācciyattiṉ pala pakutikaḷukkup pōkkap paṭṭaṉa. ak kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ kirāmak kūṭṭaṅkaḷukkum aṟivikkappaṭṭaṉa. ik kūṭṭaṅ kaḷukku aracaṉuṭaiya makkaḷ allatu uṟaviṉarē talaivarākaviruntaṉar. ak kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ mukkiyamuṭaiyaṉavum nilaiyāṉavumāyiṉ avai kōyiṟ cuvarkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa.
aracaṉukkup potu amaiccar, antaraṅka amaiccar mutaliyōr iruntaṉar. araṇmaṉaiyil talaimaip peruntaṉam (karuvūlam) iruntatu. aṅkiruntu pōr vīrar, karumakārar mutaliyōr campaḷattukkum araṇmaṉaic celavukkum paṇam eṭukkappaṭṭatu. mākāṇaṅkaḷil ciṟuntaṉaṅkaḷ1 iruntaṉa. ivai perumpālum kōyilkaḷiliruntaṉa. ivaikkup poruḷ perumpālum iṟaiyili nilaṅkaḷālum, naṉkoṭai kaḷālum vantatu. ipporuḷil kōyiṟ celavu pōka mīti makkaḷ nalattiṟkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
aracāṅkattil matippum oḻukkamum vāynta oṟṟar iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ mākāṇat talaivarkaḷiṉaip paṟṟiya ceytikaḷai aṟintu aracaṉukkut terivittārkaḷ. aracaṉ māṟuvēṭam pūṇṭu iraviṟceṉṟu makkaḷiṉ eṇṇaṅkaḷai aṟintāṉ. oṟṟar tuṟavikaḷ, vaṇikar pōṉṟu vēṭantāṅki ayal nāṭukaḷiṟ ceṉṟu pakai aracariṉ maṟaivāṉa ceytikaḷai aṟintu vantaṉar. aracaṉait talaivaṉākakkoṇṭu āṭci ceyyum muṟaiyai āriya makkaḷ tirāviṭariṭamiruntu aṟintārkaḷ eṉpataṟku ātāraṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 1avarkaḷ tamatu moḻiyai yum tirāviṭariṉ utaviyaip peṟṟut tiruttik koṇṭaṉar.2
maṇṭala āṭci
āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa nilam āḷukaiyiṉ poruṭṭu 3maṇṭalaṅkaḷ allatu mākāṇaṅkaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. maṇṭalaṅkaḷ nāṭukaḷākavum vaḷanāṭukaḷākavum pirikkap paṭṭaṉa. ovvoru nāṭṭilum pala kirāma capaikaḷ iruntaṉa. pala kirāmaṅkaḷ cērāta kirāmam taṉiyūr eṉappaṭṭatu. kirāmaṅkaḷ cērikaḷākavum kuṭumpukaḷā(vaṭṭaṅkaḷ)kavum pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. cila kōṭṭaṅkaḷ aracaṉiṉ mēṟpārvaiyiṉkīḻ nāṭṭukkuriya talaivaṉāl (kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ) āḷappaṭṭaṉa. maṟṟa maṇṭa laṅkaḷukku aracaṉ maṇṭalikarai niyamittāṉ. avarkaḷiṉ āṭcik kālam avarkaḷiṉ oḻukkattaiyum aracaṉiṉ nalleṇṇattaiyum poṟuttiruntatu. aracaṉ maṇṭalaṅkaḷiṉ pōkkaittāṉ ceyyum cuṟṟup payaṇaṅkaḷālum oṟṟarkaḷālum aṟintāṉ.
maṇṭalikaṉ varikaḷait taṇṭiṉāṉ; niyāyat tīrppuc ceytāṉ; nāṭṭil amaitiyum niyāyamum nilavumpaṭiyum, āṭci toṭarpāṉa karumaṅkaḷai naṉku naṭaipeṟumpaṭiyum ceytāṉ.
maṇṭalaṅkaḷ muḻumaiyilum cuṟṟup payaṇañceytu makkaḷōṭu toṭarpu vaittiruttal, kaṇakku kaḷaip paricōtittal, capaikaḷaik kaṇkāṇittal mutaliyaṉa avaṉuṭaiya kaṭamaikaḷ. avaṉ kōyiṟ kaṇakku kaḷaiyum paricōtittāṉ. kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷiṉ kuṟaipāṭukaḷ aintu pēraṭaṅkiya kūṭṭattōṭu maṇṭalika rāl ārāyappaṭṭaṉa.
maṇṭalikarukku aṭuttapaṭiyi luḷḷavar nāṭṭut talaivar. avar nāṭukaṇkāṭci allatu nāṭuvākai eṉappaṭṭār. nāṭṭut talaivarkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ āṭcimuṟaiyaik kaṇkāṇittārkaḷ.
kirāmacapai
ovvoru kirāmattiṉ āṭcikkum kirāmacapai poṟuppā yiruntatu. mākāṇa atikārikaḷ paṭait talaivarōṭu mākāṇam muḻumai yum payaṇañceytu vicāraṇaikaḷ ceytum kaṇakkukaḷai mēṟpārttum vantaṉar. kirāmacapaikaḷ kōyilkaḷil kūṭiṉa. kirāma capai uṟuppiṉariṉ eṇ kirāmaṅkaḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu iruntatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil ainnūṟu pēr varaiyil iruntaṉar.
aṅkattavarkaḷait teriyum vāyppukkākak kirāmam pala kuṭumpu (vaṭṭaṅkaḷ)kaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. ovvoru kuṭumpum capaikku aṅkattavar ākakkūṭiyavarkaḷiṉ peyar eḻutiya ōlainaṟukkuk kaṭṭoṉṟai aṉuppiṟṟu. kalviyum cottum iruppatu uṟuppiṉaṉāva taṟkut takutiyai uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟu. kālvēlikku atikam varikoṭukkum nilamuḷḷavaṉ allatu taṉatu conta nilattil vīṭukaṭṭi vāḻpavaṉ terivukkut takutiyuṭaiyavaṉākak koḷḷappaṭṭāṉ. aṅkattavaṉākat teriyappaṭupavaṉ 35 vayatukkum 70 vayatukkum iṭaippaṭṭavaṉāyirut tal vēṇṭum. avaṉ kaṭanta mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷil capai uṟuppiṉaṉāka iruntavaṉātal kūṭātu. kuṭumpukaḷukkuc cīṭṭukaḷ mūlam aṟivikkap paṭṭapiṉ kūṭṭam kūṭṭappaṭṭatu. ataṟku ellāk kōyil nampikaḷum aḻaikkappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷil mutiyavaṉ capaikkut talaimai vakit tāṉ. ac cīṭṭukaḷ ellām oru veṟum pāṉaikkuḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṉpu ciṟuvaṉ oruvaṉ avaikaḷuḷ oṉṟai eṭuttāṉ. ap peyar vācikkappaṭṭatu. ap peyaruṭaiyavaṉ kuṭumpiṉ aṅkattavaṉākat teriyappaṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu ovvoru kuṭumpiṉ aṅkattiṉarum teriyap paṭṭārkaḷ.
kuḷaṅkaḷaik kaṇkāṇippōr
kuḷaṅkaḷ āṇṭutōṟum tiruttappaṭuvatai mēṟpārppataṟku oru capai iruntatu. atu kuḷattil cēṟṟai appuṟappaṭuttutal, nīrp pāyccutal, payiriṭuvōrukkuc camamāka nīr aḷittal mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṇkāṇittatu. vītikaḷai amaittal avaikaḷai akalamākkutal mutaliyavaikaḷai mēṟpārkkum capaiyum iruntatu.
kuṭumpuvāriyāka aṅkattavar teriyappaṭṭa piṉpu capai avar kaḷaip palavēṟu vēlaikaḷaip pārkkumpaṭi terivuceytatu. ovvoru aṅkattavaṉum kaṭamai pārkkum kālam 360 nāḷkaḷākum. ic capaiyiṉ nikaḻccikaḷ ‘karṇattāṉ’ eṉṉum kaṇakkaṉāl eḻuti vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. kōyil nīṅkalākak kirāmattiṉ nirvākam capaiyiṉ poṟuppil iruntatu.
niyāyattīrppu
aracaṉ tāṉē nēril niyāyat tīrppuc ceytāṉ. uṭaṉ kūṭṭattār avaṉukku ñāyattīrppil utavipurintaṉar. ciṟiya vaḻakkukaḷ ovvoru cēriyaiyum mēṟpārkkum capaiyāl viḷaṅkit tīrppaḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. kolaivaḻakkukaḷ kirāmacapaiyāl vicāraṇai ceyyappaṭṭaṉa; cila camayaṅkaḷil mākāṇat talaivar vicāraṇai ceytār. mutaṉmaiyāṉa vaḻakkukaḷ paṭaittalaivaṉāl (cēṉāpatiyāl) aivar aṭaṅkiya capaipiṉ utaviyōṭu vicāraṇai ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mika mukkiyamāṉa vaḻakkukaḷē aracaṉuṭaiya vicāraṇaikku viṭappaṭṭaṉa. aracaṉ nāṭukaḷaic cuṟṟip payaṇam ceyyum kālaṅkaḷil avai vicāraṇai ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
tiruṭṭukkuṟṟattukkup paṇam aparātam vitikkappaṭṭatu. at taṇṭam taruma ācaṉam eṉappaṭṭatu. oppantaṅkaḷaimīṟiya kuṟṟaṅ kaḷukkup paṇam aparātam vitikkappaṭṭatu; cila camayaṅkaḷil ciṟait taṇṭaṉaiyum vitikkappaṭṭatu. kaṇṇukkukkaṇ kaiyukkukkai eṉpatu pōla uṟuppukaḷaik kaḷaintuviṭuvatu potuvāṉa caṭṭamāka viruntatu. koti eṇṇaiyil kaivaittal pōṉṟa kuṟṟaccōtaṉai aṟiyappaṭāta taṉṟu. kolaikkut taṇṭaṉai kolaiyākaviruntatu. vēṇṭumeṉṟu ceyyappaṭāta kolaikku 16 pacu māṭukaḷ aparātam vitikkappaṭṭatu. iṟantavariṉ uyir āṟutal aṭaitaṟkuk kirāmak kōyilil eppoḻutum eriyumpaṭi neyviḷakku ēṟṟi vaikkappaṭṭatu. irācatturōkattukkuc caṭṭappaṭi taṇṭaṉai vitikkappaṭṭatu. irācatturōkañ ceytavariṉ cottukkaḷ viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. avvāṟu kiṭaittaporuḷ kōyilkaḷuk kum aṟanilaiyaṅkaḷukkum koṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. nakarak kāvalar (Police) talaivaṉ taṇṭanāyakaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. nakarkkāvalar vītikaḷaikkāval purintaṉar. avarkaḷ patikāval eṉa aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ.
paṭai
tamiḻ aracar oruvarai maṟṟavar kīḻppaṭuttumpaṭi perumpālum pōr viḷaittaṉar. tēr, yāṉai, kutirai, kālāḷ eṉap paṭai nālvakaip paṭṭatu. yāṉai vīraṉ pulittōṟ caṭṭai aṇintu yāṉaimītu iruntāṉ. avaṉaic cūḻntu paṭaikkalan tāṅkiya vīrar ceṉṟaṉar. tērkaḷ niṟamūṭṭiya tuṇikaḷil alaṅ karikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avai pōrkkaḷat tukku niraiyākac ceṉṟaṉa. pōril vil, vēl, īṭṭi, vāḷ, kavaṇ, mutaliya pala vakai āyutaṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. paṭaikaḷ aṇi, uṇṭai, oṭṭu mutaliya pala aṇivakuppukaḷāka niṉṟu pōrceytaṉa. muṉ aṇi ākkam, koṭippaṭai, tār, tūci eṉappaṭṭatu. piṉpaṭai kūḻai eṉappaṭṭatu.
nāṭu irāṇuva campantamāṉa pirivukaḷākap pirikkappaṭṭiruntatu. 1paṭaittalaivaṉ aracaṉōṭu nēril toṭarpu vaittiruntāṉ. paṭai yiṉ perum pakuti kālāḷ. muṉṉē kutiraip paṭaiyum yāṉaip paṭaiyum ceṉṟaṉa. tērkaḷ peritum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭavillai. arāpiyar kirēkkar mutaliya ayal nāṭṭiṉarum paṭaiyil iruntārkaḷ. tūtuvarai ava māṉappaṭuttuvatum kolvatum nītik kēṭākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. ic caṭṭattai mīṟuvatāl pōrkaḷ mūṇṭaṉa.
vari
nilavari tāṉiya vakaiyākak koṭukkappaṭṭatu. perumpālum viḷaivil āṟiloṉṟu variyākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷum tōppukaḷum paṇavakaiyil variyai iṟuttaṉa. varik kaṇakku eḻutum ēṭu varip pottakam eṉappaṭṭatu. varitaṇṭum karumikaḷ (uttiyōkattar) iruntārkaḷ. ovvoru nāṭṭilum uḷḷa periya vītiyil cuṅkam taṇṭappaṭṭatu. cuṅkam koṭāviṭiṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷai appuṟaṅ koṇṭu cellamuṭiyāmal iruntatu.
pōril kiṭaitta koḷḷaip poruḷ, aracāṅkattukkuriya nilaṅkaḷ, kāṭu, muttuk kuḷippup pōṉṟa vakaiyilum aracaṉatu karuvūlattukkup poruḷ vantatu. ivaikaḷallāmal kaṭai vari, necavu vari, cekku vari pōṉṟa varikaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. kaṇṇālak kaṭam eṉṉum kaliyāṇa variyum iruntatu.1
nīrpāyccum vāyppu
aracar payirukku nīrppāyccum vacatikaḷaiatikam ceytaṉar. karikāṟ cōḻaṉ kaṭṭiya kāviri aṇai kiṭṭiya kālam varaiyil iruntatu. āṟukaḷum, kiḷai āṟukaḷum nīrppāyccum vāyppiṉ poruṭṭu maṟittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. tiricciṉāppaḷḷiyil kaṅkaikoṇṭa cōḻapurat tiluḷḷa kuḷattiṉ aṇaikkaṭṭup patiṉāṟu mail nīḷamuḷḷatu. pala veṭṭu vāykkālkaḷum veṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. celvar vīti ōraṅkaḷil kiṇaṟukaḷ veṭṭic cumaitāṅki amaittārkaḷ. vītikaḷiṉ apāyamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil pōrvīrar niṟuttappaṭṭiruntaṉar. ākavē pōkku varattuc ceyvōr tiruṭar payamiṉṟic ceṉṟaṉar. kālvāy veṭṭutal ēri veṭṭutal pōṉṟavaikaḷ makkaḷāl kaṭṭāyappaṭuttic ceyvikkap paṭṭaṉa. avvakaivēlai ūḻiyam, āḷmāñci eṉappaṭṭatu. āṟṟuk kālveṭṭa, ērikuḻiveṭṭa, cumai tūkka eṉṉum colvaḻakkukaḷ paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aracarukkuppuriyum kaṭṭāya ūḻiyam ilaṅkaiyil irācakāriyam eṉappaṭṭatu.
poḻutupōkku
maṟpōr, cilampam, tērōṭṭam, yāṉaippōr, kutirai ōṭṭam, māṭṭup pōr, āṭṭup pōr, kōḻip pōr, kāṭai kavutārippōr eṉpaṉa makkaḷiṉ potuvāṉa poḻutu pōkkukaḷākum. peṇkaḷ vīṭṭil aṭaikkappaṭṭu irukkavillai; avarkaḷ ammāṉai, pantu, pāṭṭu, āṭṭam, kummi, kuravai viḷaiyāṭṭukaḷiṟ poḻutu pōkkiṉar; kiḷi, nākaṇavāy mutaliyavaikaḷai vaḷarttaṉar.
pōkkuvarattu
muṟkālap pōkkuvarattu perumpālum kālnaṭai, vaṇṭi, pallakku, taṇṭikai, kaḻutai, erutu, yāṉai, kutirai, tēr vakaikaḷā yiruntaṉa. ōriṭattiliruntu iṉṉōriṭattiṟkup paṇṭaṅkaḷ koṇṭu celvataṟkup potimāṭukaḷum kaḻutaikaḷum vaṇṭikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. aracarum perumakkaḷum tēr pallakku, yāṉai mutaliyavai kaḷil ceṉṟaṉar.
kaṭaliṟ celvataṟkup palavakai marakkalaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. marakkalaṅ kaḷiṉ muṉpuṟam, ciṅka mukam, yāṉai mukam, kutirai mukam pōṉṟu ceyyap paṭṭiruntatu. marakkalaṅkaḷukkup pāy maraṅkaḷum pāyum iruntaṉa.
aḷavaikaḷ
eṇṇey, ney, tayir pōṉṟavaiyum tāṉiyaṅkaḷum aḷantu viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. poṉ veḷḷi mutaliyavai niṟuttuviṟkappaṭṭaṉa. tāṉiya aḷavai; 5 ceviṭu 1 āḻākku; 2 āḻākku 1 uḻakku; 2 uḻakku 1 uri; 2 uri 1 nāḻi; 8 nāḻi 1 kuṟuṇi; 2 kuṟuṇi 1 patakku; 4 kuṟuṇi allatu 2 patakku 1 tūṇi; 3 tūṇi allatu 12 kuṟuṇi 1 kalam.
paḻaiya paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟāl perumpālum poruḷkaḷ paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyappaṭṭaṉaveṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. 2 paṭi aricikku 5 paṭi nelluk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. 5 paṭi nelluk kuttuvataṟku araippaṭi arici kūliyākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu. oru nāḻi nel arici kūliyākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu. oru nāḻi nel 1/16 nāḻi neyyukkum, oru nāḻi tayir 10p pākku, 20 vāḻai ilai allatu 1/16 nāḻi neykkum paṇṭamāṟṟuṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. nellum tayirum orē peṟupati uṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa veṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. nāḷ vāḻkkaikku vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷai vāṅkuvataṟku nel payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. paṇappuḻakkam iruntapōtum potuvāka nellē paṇattaippōlap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
poṉ veḷḷi niṟai: 1 kaḻañcu 20 mañcāṭi; 1 mañcāṭi 2 kuṟuṇi; 1 mā 1/10 mañcāṭi; 1 kāṇi 1/40 mañcāṭi.
1 “akkālattē niṟuttal, mukattal mutaliya aḷavukaṭkum, poṉ irattiṉaṅkaḷ niṟuttaṟkum, poṉ māṟṟu nōkkutaṟkum aracāṅkattā rāl niyamikkappaṭṭa marakkāl mutaliyaṉa viruntaṉa. aracaṉ muttiraiyiṭṭa marakkālukku irācairācaṉ kālattil irācakēcari eṉpatu peyar. itaṟkuc camāṉamāṉa tañcāvūrk kōyil marakkālukku āṭavallāṉ eṉpatu peyar. āṉāl nāṭutōṟum marakkāl peyarkaḷ māṟupaṭṭiruntaṉa. aracāṅka muttiraiyiṭṭa eṭaikkallukkuk kuṭiñaikkal eṉṟu peyar. tañcāvūrk kōyilil itaṟkuc cama eṭaiyuḷḷa kallukku āṭavallāṉ eṉpatu peyar. (ip peyarkaḷ civapirāṉ peyarāliṭappaṭṭaṉa) poṉmāṟṟukku vaittirunta āṇikkut taṇṭavāṇiyeṉpatu peyar.”
tamiḻar tirumaṇam
paruvam aṭainta āṭavarum makaḷirum oruvarai oruvar kātalittuc ciṟitukālam piṟar aṟiyātu cērntu oḻukuvārkaḷ eṉṟum piṉ āṭavaṉ taṉatu kātaliyait taṉatu ūrukku yārumaṟiyātu koṇṭu ceṉṟu aṅku maṇantukoḷvāṉ; allatu maṇamakaḷatu peṟṟōriṉ icaivaip peṟṟu avaḷ illattilēyē avaḷai maṇantukoḷvāṉ eṉṟum caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ip pāṭalkaḷ paḻaiya marapaip piṉpaṟṟic ceyyappaṭṭaṉavēyaṉṟi akkālat tirumaṇam avvāṟu nikaḻntateṉat tuṇital muṭiyātu.
ak kālattil āṭavaṉ oruvaṉ maṇañ ceyya vēṇṭiyiruntāl avaṉ cila mutiyōraip peṇkēṭkumpaṭi peṇvīṭṭukku aṉuppu vāṉ. peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr peṇkoṭukka icaintaṉarāyiṉ, kaṇi eṉap paṭṭa cōtiṭaṉāl kuṟikkappaṭṭa nallanāḷilē maṇamakaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattā ruṭaṉ maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭukkuvantu peṇṇai maṇantu taṉatu vīṭṭukkuk koṇṭupōvāṉ. tirumaṇattukku muṉ peṇṇukku vilaiyāka maṇamakaṉ pariyam eṉap poruḷ vaḻaṅkuvāṉ. ivvāṟu koṭukkappaṭum poruḷ cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḷarppukkūli eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. peṇkaḷai vilaikoṭuttu vāṅki maṇattalākiya vaḻakkattiṉ niḻalē pariyam eṉac cilar kūṟuvar. tirāviṭa makkaḷuḷ cila kūṭṭattiṉar peṇkaḷai vilai koṭuttu vāṅki maṇantukoḷḷum vaḻakkam iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālattil purōkitariṉ utaviyiṉṟiyē tirumaṇaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉa. ak kālat tirumaṇaccaṭaṅku evvāṟirunta teṉpataic caṅka nūlkaḷaik koṇṭu aṟitalkūṭum. akanāṉūṟṟil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatai iṅku tarukiṉṟōm.
“cōṟu paruppiṭṭuc camaikkappaṭṭatu. palarkūṭi uṇṭapiṉpum cōṟu kuvintukiṭantatu. niraiyākak kālkaḷai naṭṭup pantariṭappaṭ ṭiruntatu. taraiyilveṇmaṇal parappappaṭṭiruntatu. viḷakkukaḷ koḷutti vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pūraṇai nāḷiṉ viṭiyaṟkālaiyil tīyakōḷkaḷ akaṉṟa nalla nēramāka viruntatu. talaiyilē kuṭattaiyum kaiyilē maṭpāttirattaiyum vaittukkoṇṭu peṇkaḷ vantārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaiyil vaittirunta pāttirattai oruvar kaiyiliruntu oruvarkaikku māṟṟiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu mutiya peṇkaḷ periya āravārañceytaṉar. piṉpu āṇmakavai yīṉṟavarkaḷum, vayiṟṟil aḻakutēmal uṭaiyavarkaḷum aḻakiya nakaikaḷ aṇintavarkaḷumākiya peṇkaḷ muṉṉē vantārkaḷ. maṇamakaḷiṉ talaiyil nīrai ūṟṟiṉārkaḷ. appoḻutu nīril kiṭanta pūvum nellum kūntalil kiṭantu viḷaṅkiṉa. appoḻutu avarkaḷ “nī kaṟpilvaḻuvātu kaṇavaṉukku iṉiyaḷākaviruntu neṭuṅkālam vāḻvā yāka” eṉa vāḻttiṉāhkaḷ. aṉṟu mālaikkālam vantatu. peṇkaḷ vantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. “nī vīṭṭukkup peruntalaiviyākuka” eṉṟu vāḻttiṉārkaḷ. cuṟṟattavar avaḷ kaiyaip piṭittu maṇamakaṉiṭam koṭuttārkaḷ.” (akam. 86)
“nalla ariciyaic camaitta neykalanta cōṟṟaic cuṟṟattavar mutiyavarkaḷukkuk koṭuttārkaḷ. naṟcakuṉam kāṭṭum paṟavaikaḷ teḷinta vāṉattē paṟantaṉa. tiṅkaḷ urōkiṇiyōṭu cērukiṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta nalla nēram vantatu. perukkic cuttañceyta vīṭu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭatu. ellōrum kaṭavuḷait toḻutārkaḷ. periya mēḷamum maṇamuracum olittatu. maṇaviṉaiyaik kāṇa virumpiya peṇkaḷ viraintuvantu kūṭiṉārkaḷ. aṟukaṅkiḻaṅkai veḷḷiya nūlāṟkaṭṭi vākaip pūviṉ mēl vaikkappaṭṭatum, maṇal parappappaṭṭatumākiya pantaliṉkīḻ tamar avaḷai maṇamakaṉiṭam koṭuttārkaḷ. maṇaviṉaiyaik kaṇṭa peṇkaḷ tamatu vīṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟārkaḷ.” (akam. 136)
alaṅkāram
tamiḻ makkaḷ tamatu uṭampaiyum tām payaṉpaṭuttiya ovvoru poruḷaiyum alaṅ karittārkaḷ. vīṭṭuc cuvarkaḷil aḻakiya ōviyaṅkaḷ eḻutap paṭṭaṉa. peṇkaḷ muṟṟaṅkaḷil aḻakiya kōlaṅkaḷiṭṭaṉar. vīṭu kaḷait tāṅkum tūṇkaḷ, kai maraṅkaḷ, nilaikaḷ, katavukaḷ, vīṭṭiṉ muṉpuṟaṅkaḷ tāṉum aḻakiya vēlaippāṭukaḷ peṟṟiruntaṉa. teṉṉintiyak kōyilkaḷil cuvarkaḷilum matilkaḷilum kōpuraṅkaḷilum aḻakiya uruvaṅkaḷ veṭṭappaṭṭiruttalai nām kāṇalām. intiyac ciṟpikaḷ kaṟkaḷai marattaippōlap payaṉpaṭutti atil pala kaṇkavarum vēlaippāṭukaḷ ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. vīṭṭil payaṉ paṭuttum kaṭṭil, mukkāli, nāṟkāli, maṇai, āyutaṅkaḷiṉpiṭi, nīr aruntum pāttiraṅkaḷum aḻakiya vēlaipāṭukaḷuṭaiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. teṉṉintiyak kōyilkaḷilum, amarāvati, acantā, ellōrā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum kāṇappaṭum ciṟpaṅkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷiṉ alaṅkāra viruppattait terivikkiṉṟaṉa.1
peṇkaḷ tamatu kūntalaip palavakaiyāka vāri muṭintārkaḷ. kūntalmītu palavakaip pūkkaḷaic ceruki vēyntārkaḷ. cuṟāmīṉ, valampuriccaṅku, piṟai, irupakkamum yāṉai kuṭattiliruntu nīr koṭṭu vatum naṭuvē ilakkumi vīṟṟiruppatumākiya vaṭivuṭaiya cītēviyār pōṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷai aṇintārkaḷ. koṇṭaiyil palavakaip pūk kaḷaiyum mālaiyaiyum cūṭiṉārkaḷ; cantaṉam kuṅkumam mutaliyavai kaḷaip pūci uṭalai aḻakupaṭuttiṉārkaḷ; uṭampil mañcaḷpūcik kuḷit tārkaḷ. mārpu, tōḷkaḷil toyyil eṉṉum oruvakai kuḻampaip pūci ataṉmēl karumpu pūṅkoṭi mutaliyavaikaḷai eḻuti aḻaku ceytārkaḷ. kātil tōṭu allatu kuḻai aṇintārkaḷ. vaṟiya makkaḷ kātil ōlaiyaic curuḷākkic cerukiṉārkaḷ. muttumālai, pavaḷamālai, caṅkili, kuṟaṅkuceṟi, kavāṉaṇi, paṭṭikai, cataṅkai, kiṇkiṇi, cilampu, kaḻal, vaḷai, mōtiram, mēkalai eṉpaṉa avarkaḷ aṇikalaṉkaḷiṟ cila. tiruppātam, tirumuṭi, puḷḷit toṅkal, koṟṟakkuṭai, poṟpū, tiraḷ maṇivaṭam, kāṟai, aṭikkāṟai, aṉṉam, kiḷi, cōṉakacciṭukku, makuṭam, vāḷi, vaṭukavāḷi, cūṭakam, kaṇmalar, cuṭṭi, tāli, tālimaṇivaṭam, kaṇṭa ñāṇ, poṭṭu, patakkam, ēkāvali, kālvaṭam, kamalam, ceṭi, vākuvalayam, tuṭar eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa pala aṇikalaṉkaḷum aṇiyappaṭṭaṉa.
icai
2ovvoru nilattilum ovvoru icai vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kuṟiñci nilamakkaḷ kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭiṉārkaḷ. mullainila makkaḷ mullaip paṇpāṭiṉārkaḷ. atu iṉṟu mattiyamāvati eṉappaṭum. marutanila makkaḷ marutap paṇpāṭiṉāhkaḷ. atu kētāram eṉappaṭum. neytalukkuriya paṇ iṉṟu puṉṉākavarāḷi eṉappaṭum. palavakai mēḷaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. vevvēṟu ōcaikaḷaip piṟappikkum paṟaikaḷ vevvēṟu kālaṅ kaḷil koṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. māṭukaḷaik koḷḷaiyiṭum pōtu koṭṭappaṭuvatu ēṟukōṭpaṟai, veṟiyāṭumpōtu aṟaiyappaṭu vatu murukiyam. maṇak kālattil cāṟṟappaṭuvatu maṇamuḻavu. tēriḻukkum pōtu muḻakkappaṭuvatu tērōṭṭuppaṟai. nellaṟukkum pōtu taṭṭappaṭuvatu nellarikkiṇai. aracaṉ veḷikkiḷampumpōtu olikkappaṭuvatu puṟappāṭṭuppaṟai. mīṉ piṭittaṟkuc cellumpōtu koṭṭappaṭuvatu mīṉ kōṭpaṟai. cūṟai āṭumpōtu aṭikkappaṭuvatu cūṟaikōṭṭai.
pōrkkālattil yāṉai, kutirai, kālāṭpaṭai kaḷukku uṟcākam mūṭṭum poruṭṭup palavakai vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkappaṭṭaṉa. avai iyam, icaik karuvi, vāttiyam, vācciyam eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avai tōṟkaruvi, tuḷaikkaruvi, narampukkaruvi, miṭaṟṟuk karuvi, tuḷaikkaruvi eṉa nālvakaippaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻa rukkuṭaiya ēḻicaikaḷ kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai, kural, uḷai, viḷari, tāram. mūṅkil, koṉṟaippaḻat tiṉ kōtu mutaliyavaikaḷāl ceyyappaṭṭa kuḻal kaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. ekkāḷa vakaikaḷuḷ tārai, kāḷam, kākāḷam, ammiyam, ciṉṉam mutaliyavaiyum, kompuvakaiyil kōṭu, iralai, vayir mutaliyavum iruntaṉa. narampuk karuviyil ciṟappuṭaiyatu yāḻ 1avaikaḷukku ēḻu, irupattoṉṟu allatu ataṟku mēṟpaṭṭa narampukaḷ iruntaṉa.
paṟai, muracu, pērikai, ākuḷi, ellari, callikai, kiṇai mutaliyaṉa mēḷavakaikaḷuṭcila. iṉṉum ilaṅkaittīvil vaḻaṅkum mēḷavakai yil eḻupatukku mēluṇṭu. tārai uṭukkai mutaliyaṉa pāṇar kaiyil koṇṭutirintu aṭittuppāṭum ciṟiyamēḷaṅkaḷ.
tōṟkaruvikaḷ
“pērikkai paṭaka miṭakkai yuṭukkai
cīrmiku mattaḷañ callikai karaṭikai
timilai kuṭamuḻāt takkai, kaṇappaṟai
tamarukan taṇṇumai tāvi ṟaṭāri
yantari muḻavoṭu cantira vaḷaiya
montai muracē kaṇviṭu tūmpu
nicāḷan tuṭumai ciṟupaṟai yaṭakka
māci ṟakuṇiccam viralēṟu pākan
tokka vupāṅkan tuṭiperum paṟaiyeṉa
mikka nūlōr viritturait taṉarē”
uṭai
parutti intiya nāṭṭil uṇṭākum ceṭi, maṟṟaiya nāṭṭumakkaḷ āṭai illāmal iruntapōtu allatu tōlai uṭaiyākak koṇ ṭirunta kālattil intiya makkaḷ pañcai nūlākkavum, nūlāl āṭai neyyavum āṭaikkup palavakaic cāyaṅkaḷ ūṭṭavum aṟintiruntaṉar. itaṟkuc cāṉṟu paḻaiya cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷiṟ kiṭaittuḷḷatu. intiya nāṭṭu veppanilaikkēṟpa makkaḷ aṟpa uṭaiyaiyē aṇintaṉar. āṭavar muḻaṅkālvaraiyum uṭai uṭuttu araiyil kaccukkaṭṭiyirun taṉar. mikappaḻaṅkāla makkaḷ talaiyaiccuṟṟi nāṭākkaṭṭiyiruntaṉar. piṉpu nāṭā pākai āka māṟiṟṟu. peṇkaḷ araiyil māttiram uṭai aṇintaṉar. peṇkaḷiṟcilar mārpaik kaccukkaṭṭi maṟaittaṉar. perum pālum peṇkaḷ araikkumēl uṭai aṇiyavillai. itaṉai cāṉci, amarāvati, piṟkālac ciṟpaṅkaḷilum kōyiṟ cuvarkaḷilum kōpuraṅkaḷi lum kāṇappaṭum uruvaṅkaḷaiyum koṇṭu nām naṉku aṟiyalām. iṟavukkai aṇiyum vaḻakkam akkālattil araṇmaṉaikaḷilirunta kirēkkap peṇkaḷāl koṇṭuvarap paṭṭirukkalāmeṉac cilar karutu kiṉṟaṉar. akkālattil kirēkkap peṇkaḷ intiya nāṭṭukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭamaiyai srāpō (Strabo) pōṉṟa varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ.1
2 “parutti intiya nāṭṭukku uriya ceṭi. maṟṟaiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ tōlkaḷai uṭukkumpōtu allatu uṭaiyiṉṟiyirukkumpōtu tamiḻ makkaḷ pañciliruntu nūlnūṟṟu nūlāl aḻakiya āṭaikaḷaic ceytu uṭuttārkaḷ.3 taṟkālak kuṭiyiruppukaḷil nūlnūṟkum katirkaḷ kaṇ ṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. mokañ catarō kālattilēyē makkaḷ pañciliruntu āṭai neyyavum, ataṟkuc cāyamūṭṭavum, aḻakiya vēlaippāṭukaḷ ceyyavum aṟintiruntār kaḷeṉpatu aṅkukiṭaitta maṇpāvaikaḷaik koṇṭu naṉku aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. makkaḷ āṭai neyya aṟi vataṉmuṉ marappaṭṭaikaḷai yum taḻaikaḷaiyum uṭuttār kaḷ. pañcu uṭaikkumuṉ maravuri tōṉṟiya tātaliṉ atu tuṟavikaḷukkum tūya uṭai yākap piṟkāla makkaḷāl karutappaṭṭatu. āṭaiyaik kuṟikka aimpatiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa peyarkaḷ tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru peyarum akkālattil vaḻaṅkiya vev vēṟu vakai āṭaiyaik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. caṅkakāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ pālāvipōṉṟa āṭai, iḻai ceṉṟa iṭam aṟiyamuṭi yātu nuṇṇiya nūlāl neyyappaṭṭa āṭaikaḷaip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. mullai nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷ āṭṭu mayiri liruntu kampaḷi ney tārkaḷ. āṭaikaḷ veḷuttuk kañciyiṭappaṭṭaṉa. āṭavar veḷuttuk kañciyiṭṭa maṭiyaik kulaittu araiyiṟkaṭṭi ataṉ mēl oru āṭaiyai varintu kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. āṭavar caṭṭai tarikkavillai caṭṭai tarittal ūḻiyam purivōrukkuriyatāka viruntatu. iṟavukkai aṇital iraṇṭāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ intiya aracariṉ meykāppāḷarā yirunta kirēkkap peṇkaḷāl pukuttappaṭṭatākalām. paḻaiya ōviyaṅ kaḷilum ciṟpaṅkaḷilum iṟavukkai kāṇappaṭavillai. peṇkaḷ tamatu taṉaṅkaḷai acaiyavoṭṭāmal kaccukkaṭṭiyiruntārkaḷ. āṭavar talaippākai aṇintārkaḷ. uttariyam tarittārkaḷ. kōvaṇam kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ.1
payircceykai
tamiḻ makkaḷ payirc ceykaiyaiyē mutaṉmaiyāṉa toḻilākak koṇṭaṉar. “uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvārē vāḻvār” eṉa vaḷḷuvarum uḻaviṉ ciṟappai mutaṉmaiyākak kūṟiyuḷḷār. piṟkālattu viḷaṅkiya kamparum ēreḻupatil vēḷāṇ ciṟappai naṉkeṭuttiyampiyuḷḷār. payiriṭum nilaṅkaḷ naṉcey, puṉcey eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. nel viḷaiyum vayal naṉcey eṉavum maṟṟaiya tāṉiyaṅkaḷ viḷaiyum nilam puṉcey eṉavum paṭṭaṉa. payiriṭappaṭāta nilam taricu eṉappaṭṭatu. vēḷ eṉpatu nilattaik kuṟikkum peyar. nilattait tiruttip payiriṭuvōr vēḷ āḷar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. āḷar eṉpataṟku āḷpavar eṉṟu poruḷ. veḷḷattai ēri kuḷaṅkaḷil tēkki vaittu veḷḷattai āṇṭamai yiṉ vēḷāḷar veḷḷāḷar eṉavum paṭuvar. nilaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya vēḷāḷar vēḷ allatu vēḷir eṉappaṭṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ kuṟunila maṉṉarākavum iruntaṉar. vēḷāḷar koṭaiyāṟ pukaḻpeṟṟōr. itaṉāl vēḷāṇmai eṉṉum collukkuk koṭai, upakāram eṉṉum poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. “iruntōmpi ilvāḻvatellām viruntōmpi-vēḷāṇmai ceytal poruṭṭu” eṉṉum kuṟaḷil vēḷāṇmai upakāram eṉṉum poruḷ tarutal kāṇka. cēra cōḻa pāṇaṭiya aracar vēḷāṇ marapil tōṉṟiyavarkaḷē yāvar.
cāti
tamiḻ nāṭṭil nilam aintu vakaiyiṉatāka viruntateṉa muṉṉō riṭattiṟ kūṟiṉōm. ovvoru nilamakkaḷum vevvēṟu peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭārkaḷ. oru nilattil vāḻum makkaḷ iṉṉoru nilattil vāḻnta makkaḷukkut tāḻntavareṉṟō uyarntavareṉṟō koḷḷum koḷkai ak kālattil uṇṭākavillai. celvam, vaṟumai, oḻukkam, oḻukkak kēṭu eṉpavai pōṉṟa taṉmaikaḷāl uyarvu tāḻvukaḷ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. uyarkuṭi eṉpatu pāvam paḻikaḷukku añcum kuṭi eṉpatu poruḷ. vaḷḷuvar tamatu nūlil aṭikkaṭi kuṟippiṭum kuṭip piṟappu ivviyalpiṉatātalai aṟiya māṭṭātār atu vaṭanāṭṭār varuṇattait taḻuviyateṉa maruṇṭu kūṟuvar. “piṟappokkum ellā vuyirkkum ciṟappovvā ceytoḻil vēṟṟumaiyāl” eṉa vaḷḷuvar piṟappiṉāl cātiyuṇṭeṉpārai maṟuttamai kāṇka.
makkaḷ paṭippaṭiyē tiruttamaṭaintu payirc ceykai vaḷarcci yaṭaintatu. perumpālum nakar maruta nilattilēyē tōṉṟiṟṟu. ulakilē paḻaiya nākarikac ciṟappup peṟṟu viḷaṅkiya nakaraṅkaḷ ellām vēḷāṇmai ceḻittōṅkiya āṟṟōraṅkaḷilēyē viḷaṅkiṉa. vēḷāṇmaiyāl ceḻippuṟṟa nāṭukaḷil vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭum uṇaviṉ poruṭṭum paṟpala toḻil puriyum makkaḷ vantu taṅkuvā rāyiṉar. tantaiyiṉ toḻilai makaṉ ceytal eṉpatupōlak kālvaḻi kāl vaḻiyāka ovvoru kuṭumpamum perukiyapōtu ovvoru kuṭumpa mum taṉittaṉic cātiyākap perukiyatu. nikaṇṭu nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum cātippeyarkaḷ toḻilkaḷaiyē kuṟittal kāṇka. taṉittaṉik kūṭṭa mākap perukiya makkaḷ tattamakkuḷḷēyē tirumaṇaṅkaḷ ceytukoḷ ḷum vaḻakkam nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu varutalālum cātik kaṭṭappāṭu vali aṭaintatu.
tamiḻ nāṭṭil antaṇar aracar vaṇikar veḷḷāḷar eṉṉum nāṉku perum piriviṉar, iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷallāta kaittoḻilum ēvaṟṟoḻilum ceyyum makkaḷum iruntaṉar. muṉ kūṟiya nālvarum uyarntōreṉṟum, piṉ kūṟiya iruvakaiyiṉarum tāḻntōreṉṟum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉar.
antaṇa reṉpōr tuṟavikaḷ. kōyiṟ pūcai ceyyum makkaḷaiyum kuṟikka ippeyar piṟkālattil vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. āṭci purivōr aracar eṉavum, vāṇikam purivōr vaṇikar eṉavum, vēḷāṇmai ceyvōr vēḷāḷar eṉavum paṭṭaṉar. ippirivukaḷ perumpālum nakaraṅkaḷilēyē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
iccātip pirivukaḷ maruta nilattilēyē perumpālum tōṉṟi vaḷarcciyaṭaintaṉa. vaṭanāṭṭavar toṭakkattil niṟampaṟṟip pirittirunta varuṇaṅkaḷum tamiḻaratu toḻil paṟṟiya cātikaḷum vēṟupāṭuṭaiyaṉa. varuṇam eṉpataṟku niṟam eṉpatu poruḷ. vaṭanāṭṭavariṉ varuṇat tukkup piṟappē kāraṇam. vaṭanāṭṭavariṉ pirāmaṇa, cattiriya, vaiciya, cūttira eṉṉum nāṟ pirivukaḷē teṉṉāṭṭu antaṇar, aracar, vaṇikar, vēḷāḷar eṉṉum pirivukaḷ eṉap piṟkālattavar mayaṅkiṉamaiyāl pala taṭumāṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
antaṇar, pārppār, pirāmaṇar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ paṟṟiya mayakkam
antaṇar eṉpōr tuṟavikaḷāvar eṉpatu caṅka nūlkaḷālum, tirukkuṟaḷālum piṟacāṉṟukaḷālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. tamiḻ nāṭṭil kōyiṟ pūcaiceyvōr kōyilkaḷai mēṟpārttal paṟṟip pārppār eṉṉum peyar peṟṟiruntaṉar. toṭakkattil iṉṉavartāṉ pārppā rāyirukkalām eṉṉum niyati irukkavillai. kōyilkaḷai mēṟ pārkkum kuṭumpattiṉar peruki oru pirivākap pirintaṉar. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuṭ paṇi ceytamaipaṟṟi upacāra vaḻakkil antaṇar eṉpaṭuvārāyiṉar. pārppār tuṟavikaḷallar. pirāmaṇar tamiḻ nāṭṭavarallar. ivarkaḷ vaṭa nāṭukaḷiliruntu teṉṉāṭukaḷilvantu kuṭiyēṟiṉōr. tillaimūvā yiravar, 48,000var, 8,000var, 3,700var eṉa ikkūṭṭattiṉar paṭṭaiyaṅkaḷil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ pārppaṉak kuṭumpaṅkaḷōṭu kalantuḷ ḷārkaḷ mattiya kālat tamiḻnāṭṭu aracar vaṭamoḻikku matippuk koṭuttup pirāmaṇarai ātarittamaiyāl pārppaṉa vakuppiṉarum tammaip pirāmaṇar eṉak kūṟuvarāyiṉar. ituvē antaṇar pārppār pirāmaṇar eṉpōriṉ varalāṟākum.
vāṇikam
“iṟṟaikku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉmuṉ intiyā tūrakkiḻakku, tūramēṟku nāṭukaḷōṭu mutaṉmaiyāṉa kaṭal vāṇikam naṭattiṉatu eṉṟukāṭṭuvataṟkuḷḷa nalla cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. kampōtiyā, yāvā, cumattirā, pōrṇiyō, yappāṉ, teṉcīṉā, malāyā, arāpiyā, pāracīkat tiluḷḷa mutaṉmaiyāṉa paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ, āpirikkāviṉ kiḻakkukkarai eṉpavaikaḷil intiyar vāṇikattiṉ poruṭṭuk kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ innāṭukaḷōṭu māttiramaṉṟu. akkālattil aṟiyappaṭṭirunta ulakam muḻuvatōṭum, urōma irācciyaṅkaḷōṭum vāṇikam naṭatti ṉārkaḷ”1
“iṟṟaikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ cintuveḷiyil vāḻnta makkaḷ ayal nāṭukaḷōṭu oḻuṅkupaṭṭa muṟaiyil vāṇikam naṭattiṉārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.”2 cintuveḷi nakaraṅkaḷ vāṇikattukkum kaittoḻilukkum mattiya iṭaṅkaḷākaviruntaṉa. uḷnāṭṭuppōkkuvarattukku iraṇṭu cakkaraṅkaḷuḷḷa vaṇṭikaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. avvaṇṭikaḷ iṉṟu cintuvil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum vaṇṭikaḷaip pōṉṟavai. iṉṟu cintu āṟṟil ōṭu kiṉṟa ōṭaṅkaḷum akkāla ōṭaṅkaḷai ottaṉavē. kaṭaṟ karaiyai aṭutta nāṭukaḷiliruntu uṇavup poruḷkaḷaip peṟum muṟaiyil māttiram vāṇikam naṭaipeṟavillai. ayal nāṭukaḷiliruntu ulōkap poruḷkaḷum piṟavum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. paluccistāṉattilum irācaputtāṉattilumiruntu ulōkaṅkaḷum, teṉṉintiyāviliruntu caṅkum, ullāca vāḻkkaikku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ piṟa iṭaṅkaḷi liruntum peṟappaṭṭaṉa. pāracīkattilum āpkāṉistāṉattilum iruntu niṟakkaṟkaḷum, cīṉāvilum parmāvilum iruntu nīlakkaṟkaḷum peṟap paṭṭaṉa. muttirai veṭṭum kaṟkaḷ, cuṟṟivarakkumiḻi vaitta pāṉaikaḷ, pāṇṭaṅkaḷ ceyyum kaṟkaḷ pōṉṟa intiyap poruḷkaḷ ki.mu. 3500 varaiyil pāpilōṉukkuc ceṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.1
“ekiptukkum intiyāvukkum iṭaiyil muṟkālattil pōkkuvarattu naṭaipeṟṟateṉat terikiṟatu. alakcāntiriyāvil cantitta paṭitta intiyarkaḷ mūlam tāṉ pala ceytikaḷaik kēṭṭu aṟintukoṇṭatākat tālami kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. intiyāviliruntu camayayāttirikarkaḷ ciriyāvi luḷḷa herāpolicukkuc (Hierapolis) ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṟu lūciyaṉ (Lucian) kūṟiyuḷḷār. kiṟittu piṟappataṟkumuṉ intiyar apicīṉiyāvōṭu vāṇikam purintārkaḷ eṉpatu kiṟittuva vētaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. intiyar arāpiyāvilum kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil avarkaḷ cīṉak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷil kuṭiyēṟat toṭaṅki ṉārkaḷ. ki.mu. 350 il varalāṟu eḻutiya parōcas (Berossus) pāpilōṉil aṉṉiyar kūṭṭamākak kuṭiyēṟiyiruntaṉar eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ki.mu. 63 il piṟanta srāpō (Strabo) eṉpār urōma nāṭṭut taṅkam intiyāvukku atikam celvataippaṟṟik kavalaippaṭṭār. piḷiṉi kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: intiyāvukkup payaṇañ ceypavarkaḷ ocelis (Ocelis) eṉṉum tuṟaimukattil iṟaṅkuvatu nallatu. kippālus eṉṉum paruvakkāṟṟu vīcumāṉāl nāṟpatu nāṭkaḷil muciṟit tuṟaimukattai aṭaiyalām. itaṉ cūḻalkaḷil kaṭaṟ koḷḷaikkārar irukkiṉṟārkaḷ. ātalāl atu iṟaṅkuvataṟkut takunta iṭamaṉṟu. nāṉ itai eḻutukiṉṟa kālattil aṅku āḷkiṉṟa aracaṉiṉ peyar celiputturus. vēṟu oru vāyppāṉa tuṟaimukam nelciṇṭi pakkāri (Nelcyndi Bacare) it tuṟaimukam tolaivilē uḷḷa maturaiyiliruntu āṭcipuriyum pāṇṭiyaṉukkuriyatu. puḷuṟṟā eṉpavar “ākastaciṉ parivāraṅkaḷuḷ oruvarāṉa putta caṉṉiyāci. avar nāṭṭukkuḷḷa vaḻakkappaṭi neruppil viḻuntu iṟantupōṉār. avaruṭaiya camātik kaṭṭiṭam iṅku kiṭakkiṟatu” eṉṟu kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
“kaṭal vāṇikam peritum tirāviṭar vacam iruntatu. atil āriyar ciṟupaṅku paṟṟiṉārkaḷ. arāpiyā, āpirikkā, cīṉak karaikaḷil kuṭiyēṟiya intiyariṟ perumpālōr tirāviṭarātal vēṇṭum. vaṭanāṭṭu vāṇikat taiviṭat teṉṉāṭṭu vāṇikam mutaṉmaiyuṭaiya teṉṟum, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ pōṉṟa vilaiyuyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷ teṟkiliruntu vantaṉa eṉṟum vaṭanāṭu tōl, kampaḷi eṉpavaikaḷai aḷittaṉaveṉṟum, irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ, caṅku mutaliyaṉa teṟkiliruntu vantaṉaveṉṟum kauṭaliyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. tāmpiraparaṇi āṟu, ilaṅkai, pāṇṭiyanāṭu eṉpaṉa avarāl kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
“intiyāviliruntē nalla yāṉaikaḷ mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷukkum koṇṭupōkappaṭṭaṉa. tantam, kuraṅku, mayil mutaliyavai intiya nāṭṭukkuriyavai. ivaikaḷaik kuṟikka epirēya (Hebrew) moḻiyil vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷ epirēyam alla! tirāviṭam. intiyāviliruntu oru mayil ekiptukku aṉuppappaṭṭatu. atu yūpitar kaṭavuḷukku nērntu viṭappaṭṭatu. pāpilōṉukku mayil aṉuppappaṭṭataippaṟṟip pavēru cātakam kūṟukiṉṟatu. innūl ki.mu. 400 varaiyil ceyyappaṭṭatu. kirēkka moḻiyilvaḻaṅkum oricā eṉpatu arici eṉṉum tirāviṭattiṉa tiripu. kirāmpum cātikkāyum malāya tīvukaḷil muṟkālattil kiṭaippaṉavā yiruntaṉa. avai ki.pi. 180il alakcāntiriyāvukkuk koṇṭupōkap paṭṭaṉa. ākavē ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil intiya malāya vāṇikam naṭaipeṟṟirukkalām. apicīṉiyāviṉ talainakarākiya akcamil (Auxam) naṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oru kallil eḻuttup poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ikkal ki.pi. mutal nūṟṟāṇṭil nāṭṭappaṭṭatu. avveḻuttukkaḷ intiyāvaik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. ekiptil talainakarākiya mempis aḻipāṭukaḷil intiya āṭavar makaḷiraik kāṭṭum ōviyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, aṅku intiyar kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷeṉṟum piḷiṇṭērs peṟṟi (Flinders Petrie) eṉpār kūṟiyuḷḷār. puḷuṟṟā kālattil vāḻntavarākiya ṭaiyō kiṟistōtam (Dio Chrysostom) alakcāntiriyāvil naṭamāṭiya pala nāṭṭu makkaḷiṭaiyē intiyarait tāṉ pārttatākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.”1
“kōṭaikālac cūriya ayaṉa kālattil āṇṭutōṟum nūṟṟi yirupatu marakkalakaṅkaṉ mōyis harmus (Mois Harmos) eṉṉum ceṅ kaṭaṟ karaiyiluḷḷa ekiptiya tuṟaimukattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭaṉa. paruvak kāṟṟiṉ utaviyaip peṟṟu avai nāṟpatu nāḷkaḷil intiyat tuṟai mukaṅkaḷai allatu ilaṅkait tīvai aṭaintaṉa. marakkalaṅkaḷ vilai uyarnta paṇṭaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tirumpi vantaṉa. paṇṭaṅkaḷ ceṅkaṭaṟkaraiyi liruntu nīla natikku oṭṭakaṅkaḷilēṟṟik koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. avai nīla āṟṟu vaḻiyāka alak cāntiriyāvai aṭaintu piṉ urōma nāṭṭut talainakaraic cērntaṉa.” ivvāṟu urōmaṉ varalāṟṟālariyar oruvar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. iñci, miḷaku, kaṟuvā pōṉṟavaikaḷukku mēṟku nāṭukaḷil mikat tēvaiyiruntatu. vayiram, muttu, paṭṭu eṉpa vaṟṟaiyum avarkaḷ evvaḷavu poruḷ koṭuttum vāṅka āyattamāka viruntārkaḷ. kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷiliruntu ceṉṟa paṇṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟukku nūṟu maṭaṅku ilāpattukku viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl kavarappaṭṭu mikap palar kiḻakkuk kāṭukaḷōṭu vāṇikattil īṭupaṭṭiruntārkaḷ avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya paṇṭaṅkaḷai cēra nāṭum pāṇṭiya nāṭum aḷittaṉa. kumari, koṟkai, kāyal pāmpaṉ eṉṉum tuṟaimukaṅkaḷ atika varuvāyaḷittaṉa. kiṟittuva āṇṭiṉ ārampa kālak kiḻakku nāṭukaḷiṉ vāṇikam urōmar vacam iruntatu. vayiraṅkaḷukku aṭuttapaṭiyil muttu vilai ēṟap peṟṟatāyiruntatu. urōmap peṇkaḷ paṭṭu āṭaikaḷai uṭukka virum piṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ poṉ eṭaikkup paṭṭai niṟuttu vāṅkiṉārkaḷ irattiṉaṅkaḷ iḻaittuc ceyyappaṭṭa aṇikalaṉkaḷ teṉṉintiyāvi ṉiṉṟum urōmukku aṉuppap paṭṭaṉa. kōyamuttūrp pakutiyiluḷḷa pāṭiyūril kiṭaikkum kōmētakattai urōma vaṇikar atika vilai koṭuttu vāṅkiṉārkaḷ. intiyap poruḷkaḷ urōmil nūṟu maṭaṅku vilaikku viṟkappaṭṭateṉṟum āṇṭil eṭṭu ilaṭcam taṅka nāṇa yattai intiyā vāṇika mūlam iḻuttu viṭukiṉṟateṉṟum avvāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār.
camayakkoḷkaikaḷ
āyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉarēyē tirāviṭa makkaḷ ulakam, uyir, iṟaivaṉ eṉṉum mupporuḷ uṇmaikaḷai ārāyum uyarnta camayakkoḷkai uṭaiyavarkaḷāyiruntārkaḷ. mokañcatarō, arappā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟkiṭaitta muttiraik kaṟkaḷil palavakai vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṭaiyē ālamarattiṉ kīḻ yōkattil vīṟṟirukkum kōlamuṭaiya vaṭivam oṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvaṭivam, kaṭavuḷ uyirkaḷukkut talaivaṉ (pacupati) eṉṉum karuttai viḷakkukiṉṟateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. akkālattilēyē makkaḷ yōkattiṉ taṉmaikaḷaiyum ataṉāl peṟum āṟṟalkaḷaiyum naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ. paḻaiya tamiḻariṉ camayakkaruttukkaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭē pautta caiṉa matakkoḷkaikaḷum eḻuntaṉa. puttar aracamarattiṉ kīḻ yōkat tiruntār. caiṉak kaṭavuḷ acōkamara niḻalil iruppavar eṉṉum koḷkaikaḷ mokañcatarō makkaḷ koṇṭirunta karuttaip piṉ paṟṟiyavē. kaṭavuḷ yōkattiliruppatākak kāṭṭum vaṭivam kaṭavuḷāy varutaṟkum yōkappayiṟci avaciyam eṉpatai akkāla makkaḷ koṇ ṭiruntārkaḷ eṉpatai uṇarttutal kūṭum. pautta, caiṉa mataṅkaḷ paḻaiya tirāviṭa matakkoḷkaikaḷait taḻuvi eḻuntaṉa veṉpatai varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ oppukkoḷkiṉṟaṉar.
paḻaiyakālattil camayakkaruttukkaḷ ēṭṭil eḻuti vaikkappaṭātu āciriya māṇākkamuṟaiyil collik koṭukkappaṭṭuvantaṉa. ivvāṟu talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāka vanta camaya uṇmaikaḷ maṟaikaḷ eṉap paṭṭaṉa. upaniṭataṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭa camayanūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭum karuttukkaḷ tirāviṭariṉ koḷkaikaḷē. akkālattil pirāmaṇar aracavakuppiṉariṉ pātaṅkaḷiliruntu upaniṭata uṇmaikaḷaip payiṉṟārkaḷ. upaniṭata ñāṉaṅkaḷ pirāmaṇarāl aṟiyappaṭātavai. ikkaruttukkaḷē tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camayakkaruttukaḷākum. ak koḷkaikaḷ ippoḻutu caiva cittāntak koḷkaikaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkum. caivacittāntak koḷkaikaḷ varalāṟṟuk kālattukku muṉ toṭṭut tamiḻ makkaḷiṭaiyē vaḻaṅkivarum uyarnta karuttukkaḷ eṉa ṭākṭar pāp kūṟiyuḷḷār.
tamiḻaruṭaiya matam ellāmakkaḷukkum potuvāṉatu. ituvē vaḷḷuvaratum, tolkāppiyaratum matamākum. tirukkuṟaḷil viṉaip payaṉ, maṟupiṟappu, uyiriṉ aḻiviṉmai, pāva puṇṇiyam, iṟaivaṉ, iruviṉaikaḷaiyum paṟṟāmal iṟaivaṉ niṟkum taṉmai, tavam, tavatti ṉāl peṟum āṟṟal pōṉṟavai kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mantira vitataikaḷi ṉālō kaṭavuḷukkuk kāṇikkaikaḷ koṭuppatiṉālō uyirkaḷ uyar nilai aṭaiya māṭṭā veṉpatē aṟivuṭaiyōr matamākum.
toṭakkattil camayam accam, utaviyaṟṟa taṉmai pōṉṟa ētuk kaḷāl tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu; piṉpu ciṟitu ciṟitākap paṇpāṭaṭaintu uyar nilai aṭaintatu. ikkoḷkaikaḷai muḷḷuḷḷa tāḻai marattil pūtta naṟuma™muḷḷa pūvukku oppiṭalām. iṉṟaiya makkaḷiṉ kuṟaipāṭu pūvaip pōṟṟātu tāḻaiyiṉ muḷḷuḷḷa ilaikaḷaip pōṟṟutal pōṉ ṟiruppatē.
uṇavu
makkaḷ tām vāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷukku ēṟpa vevvēṟu vakai uṇa viṉai uṇṭuvantārkaḷ. tāvaram ūṉ eṉṉum iruvakai uṇavukaḷum perumpālum koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ tāvaravakaiyē uṇaviṉ perum pakutiyākavum ūṉ kaṟivakaipōṉṟu ciṟitaḷavākavum iruntaṉa. caṅkakāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl vēḷāṉ vakuppiṉar pārppaṉar eṉṉum iruvakuppiṉarallāta maṟṟaiyōrellām ūṉ uṇavu koṇṭārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. ūṉ uṇavu koḷḷutal ciṟantataṉṟu eṉṉum koḷkai nilaviyiruntateṉpatu tirukkuṟaḷāl naṉku terikiṉṟatu.
kaṟivakaikaḷukku miḷaku payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. miḷakukkuk kaṟi eṉpatu maṟṟoru peyar. muṟkālat tamiḻ makkaḷ miḷakāyaippaṟṟi aṟiyār. airōppiyar mūlam miḷakāy intiyāvai aṭaintatu. miḷakāyc ceṭi teṉṉamerikkāvukkuriyatu. itaṉkāy miḷakupōl kāramāyiruttal paṟṟi miḷakāy (miḷaku+kāy) eṉappaṭṭatu. airōppiyar intiyāviṉiṉṟum miḷakai vāṅkic ceṉṟārkaḷ. kirēkkarum urōmarum itaṉai atikam virumpiṉamaiyāl itaṟku ‘yavaṉappiriyā’ eṉṉum iṉṉoru peyarum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. yavaṉar eṉpatu kirēkkaraiyum urōmaraiyum kuṟikka vaḻaṅkiya peyar. miḷakaik kuṟikkak kirēkkar pippili eṉṉum peyarai vaḻaṅkiṉar. pippili eṉpatu tippili eṉpataṉ tiripu. pippili eṉṉum kirēkka colliṉ tiripē peppar eṉṉum āṅkilaccol. muṟkālattil miḷaku airōppiya makkaḷiṉ cokucāṉa uṇavup poruḷāka viruntatu. mattiya kālattil atu avarkaḷukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta uṇavup poru ḷāka viruntatu. mutalil veṉīciya vaṇikarum, piṉpu pōrttukkēyarum, ollāntarum miḷakai mēṟku āciya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu vāṇikam purintaṉar. patiṉōrām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil ollāntar miḷakiṉ vilaiyai irumaṭi ākkiṉamaiyāl āṅkilarāl 1599il ‘kiḻakkintiya vāṇikak kampaṉi’ toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl intiyā vil āṅkilar āṭci tōṉṟi vaḷarvatāyiṟṟu.
nikaṇṭu nūlkaḷil cōṟu, ciṟṟuṇṭi vakaikaḷaik kuṟikkap paṟpala peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaḷ ellām vevvēṟu vakaiyākac ceyyappaṭṭa uṇavuvakaikaḷaik kuṟikkiṉṟaṉa. “aṟucuvai uṇṭi yamarntu illāḷūṭṭa” eṉṉum nālaṭiyār aṭiyiṉāl celvar aṟu cuvaiyuḷḷa uṇavukaḷai uṇṭārkaḷ eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm.
kaṟivakaikaḷukkut tāḷitam ceyyappaṭṭatu. tēṉ, kāy, piñcu, paḻam, kiḻaṅku, taḷir, ilai mutaliyaṉa uṇavākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. tamiḻil matuvakaikaḷaik kuṟikka aṟupatu peyarkaḷ varaiyil kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉāl akkālamakkaḷ matuvai atikam uṇṭārkaḷ eṉat terikiṉṟatu. nel, tēṉ, paḻavakaikaḷ, pūvakaikaḷiliruntu matuvakaikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. avai cāṭikaḷil ūṟṟik kāramēṟumpaṭi nilattil putaittu vaikkappaṭṭaṉa. “tēṭkaṭuppaṉṉa nāṭpaṭutēṟal” eṉak kaḷḷiṉ kāram kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. akkālak kaḷ vakaikaḷ ikkālac cārāya vakai pōṉṟu vālaiyiliṭṭu vaṭikkappaṭṭavaiyalla; ikkālapīr (beer) eṉṉum matuppōṉṟavai.
ayalnāṭṭup payaṇakkārar intiya makkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷaippaṟṟik kūṟiyuḷḷa cila kuṟippukaḷ:
1iṟciṅ eṉṉum cīṉayāttirikaṉ kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: viruntu koṭuppōr puttakurumārai vaṇaṅki avarkaḷai vīṭṭukku aḻaik kiṟārkaḷ. viruntil perumpālum ceppup pāttiraṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. avai cāmpaliṭṭu miṉukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kuruvum ovvoru maṇaiyil iruppār. oruvarōṭu oruvar muṭṭātapaṭi maṇaikaḷ eṭṭa iṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. maṭpāttiraṅkaḷ orumuṟaikkumēl payaṉpaṭuttappaṭuvatillai. orumuṟai payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ kuḻiyil vīcappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. payaṉpaṭuttiya maṭpāttiraṅkaḷai orupōtuma vaittiruttal kūṭātu. piccai iṭum iṭaṅkaḷil ivvāṟu vīcappaṭṭa pāttiraṅkaḷ kuvintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. cīṉātēcattil ceyyappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟa uyarnta maṭpāttiraṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭapiṉ cuttañceytu vaikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. viruntiṉar cāppiṭum iṭam cāṇiyāl meḻukappaṭṭiruk kum. vāyilil periya pāṉaikaḷil taṇṇīr vaikkappaṭṭirukkum. kuru mār vantatum avarkaḷ tamatu mēl aṅkiyiṉ kaṭṭukaḷai aviḻp pārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tamatu kaikaḷaip payaṟu allatu maṇṇaittēyttuk kaḻuvuvārkaḷ. kurumār tamatu kuṇṭikaiyiluḷḷa nīraip payaṉpaṭuttuvar; allatu viruntukku aḻaittavar taṇṇīrai ūṟṟa ataṉaip payaṉ paṭuttuvar. piṉpu avarkaḷ maṇaikaḷil amarvar. avarkaḷukku muṉṉāl uṇṇum taṭṭukaḷ vaikkappaṭum. uṇpataṉmuṉ kaṭavuḷait tutippatu vaḻakkamaṉṟu. viruntukku aḻaittavar uṇavaip parimāṟuvār. parimāṟumpōtu peruviraṟ parumaiyuḷḷa iñcittuṇṭum orukaraṇṭi uppum koṭukkappaṭum. uppuk koṭuppavaṉ talaimaik kuruviṉmuṉ kaikaṭṭi muḻaṅkāl paṭintu niṉṟu vaṇaṅkuvāṉ; appoḻutu avar ellārukkum uṇavaic carivarap parimāṟu eṉṟu colvār. ellōruk kum uṇavu paṭaittapiṉ uṇṇavēṇṭumeṉṟu kāttirukka vēṇṭiya tillai.
mārkkōpōlō kūṟiyiruppatu: malapāril ellā āṭavarum makaḷirum nāḷil irumuṟai kuḷikkiṟārkaḷ. avvāṟu ceyyātavar tāḻntavar eṉak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ valatu kaiyāl uṇkiṉṟaṉar. ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyāvatu avarkaḷ uṇavai iṭakkaiyāl toṭamāṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ iṭakkaiyai acuttamāṉa tēvaikaḷukkup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoruvaṉum nīr parukuvataṟkut taṉimaiyāṉa ēṉam vaittirukkiṟāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉ payaṉpaṭuttum ēṉattiliruntu iṉṉoruvaṉ nīr uṇṇamāṭṭāṉ. nīr uṇṇumpōtu avarkaḷ ēṉattai vāyil muṭṭaviṭamāṭṭārkaḷ; vāykkuc ciṟitu tūrattil piṭip pārkaḷ. evaṉāvatu ēṉattai vāyil vaittalkūṭātu; nīr kuṭippataṟku ataṉai aṉṉiyaṉiṭam koṭukkavum kūṭātu, aṉṉiyaṉiṭam ēṉam illāviṭiṉ avarkaḷ nīrai avaṉ kaiyil ūṟṟuvārkaḷ. avaṉ kaiyai ēṉamākak payaṉpaṭuttuvāṉ.
1makkaḷ mikavum nuṭpamākac camaiyal ceykiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ nūṟuvakaiyākac camaiyal ceykiṟārkaḷ. tiṉamum camaiyal vevvēṟu vakaiyākavuḷḷatu.
muṭivurai
tirāviṭa makkaḷē intiya nāṭṭiṉ ātimakkaḷ. immakkaḷ iṟṟaikku āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ciṟanta nākarikavaḷam peṟṟu viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. tirāviṭar nākarikap paṇpāṭukaḷ, camayakkaruttukkaḷ, kalaikaḷ eṉpaṉa putitāka intiyanāṭṭai vantaṭainta makkaḷ tirāviṭa makkaḷōṭu kalantu tōṟṟuvitta putiya cantatiyiṉarāl ciṟcila vēṟupāṭu kaḷuṭaṉ kaikkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. iṉṟu avaikaḷē, intiyakkalai, intiya matakkoḷkaikaḷ, intiya nākarikam eṉṉum peyarkaḷ peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.1 intiya nākarikattukku māttiramaṉṟu paḻaiya ulaka nāka rikattukkē tirāviṭa aṭippaṭai uṇṭu eṉpatai ēṟṟa mēṟkōḷ kaḷuṭaṉ icciṟiya nūlil viḷakkiyuḷḷōm.

1. We have a continuity of culture from Paleolithic to neolithic, from neolithic to megalithic and megalithic to iron age in South India-Origin and Spread of the Tamils-V.R. Ramachandra Dikshitar. M.A.
1. I ask what is the objection to state that a branch of Dravidians from south went to North and North-West and settled there? Diffusion of Dravidians in Rajaputana and Central India in prehistoric times is seen from the dialects Villi and Santal prevalent today, bearing close affinity to Dravidian languages. Add to this, the Mohenjo daro script which is very probably Dravidian.-Ibid pp. 10, 11.
2. If we take into consideration all these circumstances and examine them critically one has to assume that the authors of these early cultures in the East Mediterranean were emigrants from South India speaking Dravidian dialects. The language migrated and with it the peoples who spoke that language. So my humble thesis is that civilization of the future was borne not on the shores of the Mediterranean but on the coasts of the Indian peninsula and on the banks of its mihghty rivers the Kaveri, Thambraparni, the Periyar and Amarovathi, not to speak of the Krishna, Godavari and Narbada-Ibid p.27.
1. The word Dravida is the name for the speakers of a group of South Indian languages - Tamil, Malayalam, Kanarese and Telugu. No stretch of imagination is required to believe that of them Tamil is the oldest dialect and in my opinion the parental dialect. Though a claim has been recently made for the ancientness of the Kannada tongue, still it is safe to assert that Malayalam, Kanarese and Telugu became cultivated languages only a thousand years ago, when the influence of Sanskrit language had reached its high water mark in the Peninsula. So the term Dravidian we can definitely say, originally stood for the Tamil language and its descendants. - Ibid p. 14.
1. kaḷvar eṉṉum peyar valiyai uṇarttum; kaḷ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu - kaḷiṟu, (pōrk)kaḷam mutaliya coṟkaḷ kaḷ eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantavai. matuvaik kuṟikkum kaḷ eṉpataṟku valiyait taruvatu eṉpatu poruḷ. pi.ti. cīṉivāca aiyaṅkār.
1. ....whose growth in South India is evidenced by the presences of the artifacts of the palaeolithic, neolithic and early iron ages throughout the country, and is traceable in the earliest strata of Tamil language and literature, show us that the growth of civilization in the Tamil land is entirely due to geographical causes operating in situ and not to historical events such as incursions of foreigners-Environments and Culture-P.T.S. Aiyengar.
1. The very name ur spells like a pukka Tamil name-Ibid.
1 Elsewhere I have suggested that the Kirata tribes of South India might have been the people who were responsible for the Cretan civilization and Crete derived its name probably after this tribe - Ibid p.49.
The term Elam in Mesopotamia is suggestive and shows that to be a name given possibly by the emigrants from Ceylon who settled there and were responsible for the growth of the Town. For is not Ceylon known as Ilam (īḻam) in literature and epigraphy? The people of Elam were non Semitic and closly related to Sumerian culture. Their language was agglutinative but did not belong to the group of Alarodian like the Sumer tongue. In the same way Caria adjoining Lycia was very probobly Cera after a settlement from Kerala-Ibid p. 39.
1. We therefore conclude that the so called Mediterranean race had its origin in peninsular India, which was a part of the original Dravidian home which was in the submerged continent and connected south India with Africa, when the Indo Gangetic basin had not probably been formed. So the Dravidian element is not to be found in Indian culture alone but is largely traceable in Cretan, Aegean, Sumerian, Baby-lonian, Egyptian, Polynesian and other cultures of ancient world-Ibid p. 29
2. yāḻnūl-pa. 26, 49, vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷ.
1. The linguistic affinity between Somali language and Tamil is remarkable. A distinct contribution of the ancient Dravidian to world culture is the Dravidian tongue. The group of agglutinative dialects with a few exceptions look to the ancient Tamil language primarily as their parent. Can we say that the service of Dravida was to give the tongue to the tongueless? -Origin and spread of the Tamils P. 39
1. Dialects of the same family of languages were spoken throughout India, except in the Vindyan regions, in the Neolithic age; and that is what has been called Dravidian family-Stone age in India p. 43. P.T.S. Ayangar.
It will thus appear that the Dravidian speakers were the latest occupants of India before the Indo-Europeans arrived...It will thus appear that the civilization of the Indus valley was associated with speakers of the Dravidian languages...Lastly the Brahmi script of later Vedic civilizations is itself traced to Indus valley pictographs...Thus the non aryan of the Rlgveda may be in a sense taken to the non Aryan responsible for the Indus civilization-Hindu civilization-pp 38, 30. Radha Kumud Mukerji.
2. He had to subdue or assimilate the aboriginal element. But the overthrow of the black skin was no means an easy task for the Aryan; The non-aryan of the Rigveda was fully fortified in the stronghold of his own civilization which was meterially quite advanced. Remnants of this civilization are traced in the ruins of cities unearthed at Harappa and Mohenjo-Daro-Ibid p 69.
3. There was also inevitably at work a process of fusion between the Aryan and the non aryan by intermarriage or by alliance-Hindu civilization-Ibid.
1. The Aryan ethnical type which closely intermingled with the non-aryan population before the caste system was fully developed never constituted more than a small fraction even of the population of the north - A short history of India Havell p. 29.
But as Dravidian society was matriarchal such intermarriages, with or without consent always exerted powerful influence in the Aryanisation of India, for in the course of time all the highest Dravidian families, both in the north and south claimed Aryan descent on-their mother‘s side and and adopted Aryan custiom and religion - Ibid p. 31.
2. As a rule however the Aryan in adopting a Dravidian word changed it considerably in order to suit it to their tongue and whenever such a word was imperfectly understood and negligenlty reproduced the change became still greater.-Kannada Eng. Dictionary. p XV-Rev E. Kettel.
3. Rhys Davids gives of an account of the life in the days of Buddha which probably holds true generally for the Dravidian communities-The children of the Sun.-p. 337. W.J. Perry M.A.
4. The Vedic language was a “caste language” a “scholastic dialect of a class” employing forms of different linguistic periods, an artificially archaic dialect,
handed down from one generation to the other within the class of priestly singers - Macdonnel.
Sankrit came into prominance about five thousand years ago in northern India. It appeared from the beginning not as an Indian vernacular, but as the hand maiden of the Arya cult, a literary language, used as the vehicle of the aspirations of the Rishis when they appealed to the gods to satisfy their longings in this world and the next. The Hindus have called it the language of the gods-deva bhasa-and this apparently means that there is no evidence of its employment as a vernaculur by common men in their ordinary secular life before it became the language of the mantras by means of which men spoke to the gods-Stone age in India p. 45-P.T.S. Iyengar.
1. The so called Gaudian dialects now spoken in Northern India from Punjab down to Aryavarta agree in grammatical structure with the so called Dravidian dialects of south India. The family relationships of languages can best be ascertained not so much by similarities of their vocables but by and examination of the essential structure of the languages, by their schemes of accidence of gender, number and cases of nouns and adjectives, of voices, mood number gender, tenses and other inflections of verbs, of their essential structure-such as the order of words in sentences and the methods of formation of idioms. A comparative study of modern North Indian and South Indian dialects reveals the fact that their fundamental grammatical structure is so verymuch the same that it is possible to translate from oneof these languages into any other by the simple process if the substitution of one word for another-a procedure absolutely impossible when translation from Sanskrit or English into any of the spoken dialects of ancient or modern India-Stone age in India. p. 44.
1. Origin and Spread of the Tamils p. 28. 2. Pre Aryan Tamil culture p. 12.
1. The excavations at Mohenjo Daro have revealed the state of things of a date long anterior to that of the Birth of Buddha. In the words of Dr. Hirananda Sastri, the late Epigraphist of the Government of India. “There is no known structure in the pre-historic Egypt or Mesopatamia or elsewhere to compare
with the well built commodious houses of the citizens of Mohenjo Doro. Their sanitary baths and the elaborate system of drainage show that even the ordinary town folk there enjoyed a degree of comfort and luxury unknown in other parts of the then civilized world”. That was some what about B.C. 5000. Bulletin No. 3 of Sri Rama Varma Research Institute.

1. cōyukuvā (Chau Ju Kua 1225 A.D.) eṉṉum cīṉap pirayāṇi ilaṅkai aracaṉaip paṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatu piṉvarumāṟu: “aracaṉ pala niṟa āṭaikaḷai uṭuttirukkiṟāṉ; civapput tōlāṟceytu poṉvāriṭṭa ceruppait tarittirukkiṟāṉ; veḷiyē cellumpōtu pallakkil allatu yāṉaimītu celkiṉṟāṉ; vaiṭūriyam, nīlam civappu mutaliya maṇikaḷ patittucceyta nakaikaḷaip pūṇṭirukkiṟāṉ. kiḻakkilum mēṟkilum kolumaṇṭapaṅkaḷ uṇṭu, iraṇṭu maṇṭapaṅkaḷilum poṉ maraṅkaḷ nāṭṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa, avaikaḷiṉ kompukaḷum aṭimaramum poṉ mayamāṉavai; pūkkaḷum ilaikaḷum kāykaḷum vaiṭūriyam nīlam civappu eṉṉum maṇikaḷāl amaikkappaṭṭavai. aracaṉ ulāvum taraiyum alaṅkarikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ammaraṅkaḷiṉ aṭiyil poṉ ciṅkācaṉaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṉ kālaiyil kiḻakku maṇṭapattilum mēṟku maṇṭapat tilum koluvirukkiṟāṉ. aracaṉ irukkumpōtu cōṭippukaḷ veḷiyē miṉṉum. poṉmaraṅkaḷum kaṇṇāṭi maṟaippukkaḷum oṉṟiṉ niḻal oṉṟil paṭṭuc cūriya utayampōl viḷaṅkum.
mēṟkūṟiyapaṭi pirayāṇi malaiyāḷa aracaṉaippaṟṟik kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu:
aracaṉ uṭampaip pōrttirukkiṟāṉ; avaṉ kālil cerupput tarittirukkavillai. avaṉ talaiyil talaippākai tarittirukkiṟāṉ; araiyil āṭai uṭuttirukkiṟāṉ. avai iraṇṭum veṇṇiṟamuṭaiyaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil avaṉ veḷḷaic caṭṭai aṇikiṟāṉ. avaikaḷukku nīṇṭu oṭuṅkiya kaikaḷuṇṭu. veḷiyē cellumpōtu avaṉ yāṉaimītu celkiṉṟāṉ. appoḻutu avaṉ poṉmuṭi aṇikiṟāṉ. atu muttiṉālum irattiṉaṅkaḷālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. avaṉuṭaiya kaiyil poṉnāṭāk kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkiṟatu; avaṉuṭaiya kālil poṉcaṅkili kiṭakkiṟatu. avaṉuṭaiya ciṉṉaṅkaḷil civapputtaṭiyil mayiliṟaku kaṭṭiyakoṭi oṉṟu. atai irupatu pēr niṉṟu kāvalpurikiṉṟaṉar. avaṉait tiṭakāttiramuḷḷa ainnūṟu aṉṉiyanāṭṭup peṇkaḷ kāval purikiṉṟaṉar. muṉṉē celpavarkaḷ. nāṭṭiyamāṭik koṇṭu vaḻiyaik kāṭṭic celkiṉṟaṉar. nāṭṭiyap peṇkaḷukku muṉṉāl aracaṉiṉ karumattalaivar koṇṭu cellappaṭukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tuṇiyiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa ēṇaipōṉṟa tūkkil iruttappaṭṭu poṉṉālum veḷḷiyiṉālum alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa kampukaḷ mūlam cumakkappaṭukiṟārkaḷ.”
aracaṉuṭaiya meykāppāḷar peṇkaḷāka viruntaṉar eṉa mekastīṉas kūṟiyuḷḷār. srāpō (Strabo) eṉpār, “aracaṉuṭaiya meykāppāḷar peṇkaḷ. avaṉ vēṭṭaikkuc cellumpōtu peṇkaḷ avaṉaic cūḻntu celkiṉṟaṉar. cila peṇkaḷ tērilum, cilar kutirai mēlum, cilar yāṉaimēlum āyutam tāṅkiyavarkaḷākac celkiṉṟaṉar” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. kauṭaliyar kūṟiyiruppatu koṇṭu kirēkka varalāṟṟukkārar kūṟiyiruppatu varumāṟu: aracaṉ paṭukkaiyaiviṭṭu eḻuntatum villut tāṅkiya peṇkaḷ avaṉai etirkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. peṇ aṭimaikaḷē avaṉaik muḻukkāṭṭa vēṇṭum, paṭukkaiyai virikkavēṇṭum, avaṉatu uṭaikaḷai veḷukkavēṇṭum, avaṉai pūmālaikaḷāl alaṅkarikkavēṇṭum. kauṭaliyar kūṟukiṉṟapaṭi araṇmaṉai yil mūṉṟu taraṅkaḷāṉa peṇ vēlaiyāṭkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷil kaṇikaiyar
eṉappaṭṭa kīḻttarattiṉar aracaṉukkuk kuṭai piṭippar. avaṉukkup pakkattē eccil umiḻum poṉ kuṉṭikaiyaip piṭittu niṟpar. ivarkaḷukku aṭutta uyartarattiṉar aracaṉ māḷikaiyil irukkumpōtu pakkattē ālavaṭṭaṅkaḷōṭu niṉṟu avaṉaic caṉattilum tērilum irukkumpōtu cēvippar. ivarkaḷil mūppaṭaikiṉṟavarkaḷ aracaṉuṭaiya kaḷañciyattil allatu aṭukkaḷaiyil vēlai ceyyumpaṭi viṭappaṭuvar.” (Chandra Gupta Maurya and his times pp. 97-8.)
1. Subtreasruies.
1. The Vedic theory is found in the Aitareya brahmana. It asserts that the Devas i.e. their worshippers, the Hindus, originally had no king. In their struggle against the Asuras-when the Devas found that they were repeatedly defeated that they came to the conclusion that it was because the Asura had a king to lead them, they were successful. Therefore they decided to try the same experiment and they agreed to elect a king. It has a historical reference. It would refer to the tribal stage of the Aryans in India and it would suggest that the institution of kingship was borrowed from the Dravidians-Hindu polity-p. 5. K.P. Jayaswal M.A.
2. Dravidian characteristics have been traced alike in Vedic and classical Sanskrit in the Prakrits or early popular dialects and in the modern vernaculars derived from them. The presence of the second series of dental letters the so called cerebrals in the language of the Rigveda and their absence from any other Indo- European language is ascribed to Dravidian influence-The quarterly Journal of the Mythic society Vol. XVI. Kalipada Mitre M.A.B.L.

3. Our knowledge of the costumes of Mohenjo Daro people is scanty as naked figures preponderate. A shawl is worn by a male figure covering the left shoulder
and passed under the right arm. It is difficult to say what was worn under the shawl, but the heroes and deities wore a thin strip of cotton on their loins. Some very rare fiqurines are depicted wearing kilt or drawers. The hair was tied with a woven fillet. The woven sari terminating well above the knees always fastened with girdles and in one case with a kamarband is also seen. The narrow strip of cloth used as sari at Mohenjo Daro very much resembles the niva mentioned in Vedic literature. Vol. 1. p. 53-Dr. Motichandra M.A. Ph.D.

The manufacture of it into cloth in imitation of the fabrics of Egypt and India was first attempted by the Italian States in the thirteenth century-Industrial arts of India p. 241.

vīṭṭu vēlai ceyyum peṇkaḷ
